        console.log('[OPT Phase 3] âœ“ Done. Removed:', buffersRemoved.length, '| f2 reduction:', f2Reduction.toFixed(2));
        return { buffersRemoved, f2Reduction };
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // PHASE 4 INTERNALS
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    /**
     * Get all upstream ancestor node IDs of a given node.
     * "Upstream" = children in the BOM tree (toward raw materials).
     */
    function getUpstreamAncestors(nodes, nodeId) {
        const ancestors = [];
        const visited   = new Set();
        const queue     = [];

        // Start from direct children
        const startNode = nodes.get(nodeId);
        if (!startNode || !startNode.children) return ancestors;
        startNode.children.forEach(c => queue.push(c.id));

        while (queue.length > 0) {
            const current = queue.shift();
            if (visited.has(current)) continue;
            visited.add(current);

            const node = nodes.get(current);
            if (!node) continue;
            ancestors.push(node);

            if (node.children) {
                node.children.forEach(c => {
                    if (!visited.has(c.id)) queue.push(c.id);
                });
            }
        }
        return ancestors;
    }

    /**
     * Evaluate a swap: remove buffer B, add upstream node(s) U.
     * Returns { netDeltaF2, valid } where valid means constraints respected.
     */
    function evaluateSwap(network, nodeB, upstreamNodes, lockedF0, lockedF1, budgetMax) {
        const core  = window.DDOptim.optCore;
        const nodes = network.nodes;

        const f2Before = core.computeF2(nodes);

        // Apply swap
        nodeB.hasBuffer = false;
        upstreamNodes.forEach(u => { u.hasBuffer = true; });
        core.fullRecalc(network);

        const newF0 = core.computeF0(nodes);
        const newF1 = core.computeF1(nodes);
        const newF2 = core.computeF2(nodes);

        const valid = newF0 <= lockedF0
                   && newF1 <= lockedF1
                   && core.isBudgetRespected(nodes, budgetMax)
                   && newF2 < f2Before;

        const netDeltaF2 = f2Before - newF2; // positive = improvement

        // Undo
        nodeB.hasBuffer = true;
        upstreamNodes.forEach(u => { u.hasBuffer = false; });
        core.fullRecalc(network);

        return { netDeltaF2, valid };
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // PHASE 4 â€” SWAP SEARCH
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    /**
     * Phase 4: swap search.
     * For each expensive buffered non-locked node B, try 1â†’1 and 1â†’2
     * substitutions with upstream ancestors. Accept the best valid swap
     * that reduces f2. Restart outer loop after each accepted swap.
     *
     * Returns: { swapsApplied: [{removed, added, deltaF2}] }
     */
    function runPhase4(network, lockedF0, lockedF1, budgetMax) {
        console.log('[OPT Phase 4] Swap search...');
        const core  = window.DDOptim.optCore;
        const nodes = network.nodes;

        const swapsApplied = [];
        let changed = true;

        while (changed) {
            changed = false;

            // Sort buffered non-locked nodes by cost descending
            const removable = core.getRemoveCandidates(nodes)
                .sort((a, b) => {
                    const va = a.bufferSizing ? (a.bufferSizing.inventoryValue || 0) : 0;
                    const vb = b.bufferSizing ? (b.bufferSizing.inventoryValue || 0) : 0;
                    return vb - va;
                });

            let bestSwap = null;

            for (const nodeB of removable) {
                const upstream = getUpstreamAncestors(nodes, nodeB.id)
                    .filter(u => !u.bufferLocked && !u.hasBuffer);

                // 1â†’1 swaps
                for (const u of upstream) {
                    const result = evaluateSwap(network, nodeB, [u], lockedF0, lockedF1, budgetMax);
                    if (result.valid && result.netDeltaF2 > 0) {
                        if (!bestSwap || result.netDeltaF2 > bestSwap.netDeltaF2) {
                            bestSwap = {
                                nodeB,
                                upstreamNodes: [u],
                                netDeltaF2: result.netDeltaF2
                            };
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 1â†’2 swaps (limit to first 15 upstream to keep complexity manageable)
                const upstreamLimited = upstream.slice(0, 15);
                for (let i = 0; i < upstreamLimited.length; i++) {
                    for (let j = i + 1; j < upstreamLimited.length; j++) {
                        const u1 = upstreamLimited[i];
                        const u2 = upstreamLimited[j];
                        const result = evaluateSwap(network, nodeB, [u1, u2], lockedF0, lockedF1, budgetMax);
                        if (result.valid && result.netDeltaF2 > 0) {
                            if (!bestSwap || result.netDeltaF2 > bestSwap.netDeltaF2) {
                                bestSwap = {
                                    nodeB,
                                    upstreamNodes: [u1, u2],
                                    netDeltaF2: result.netDeltaF2
                                };
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (bestSwap) {
                // Apply best swap
                bestSwap.nodeB.hasBuffer = false;
                bestSwap.upstreamNodes.forEach(u => { u.hasBuffer = true; });
                core.fullRecalc(network);
                changed = true;

                swapsApplied.push({
                    removed: {
                        nodeId: bestSwap.nodeB.id,
                        product: bestSwap.nodeB.product || bestSwap.nodeB.id
                    },
                    added: bestSwap.upstreamNodes.map(u => ({
                        nodeId: u.id,
                        product: u.product || u.id
                    })),
                    deltaF2: bestSwap.netDeltaF2,
                    phase: '4'
                });
                console.log('[OPT Phase 4] Swap applied: removed', bestSwap.nodeB.product || bestSwap.nodeB.id,
                    '-> added', bestSwap.upstreamNodes.map(u => u.product || u.id).join(' + '),
                    '| Î”f2:', bestSwap.netDeltaF2.toFixed(2));
            }
        }

        console.log('[OPT Phase 4] âœ“ Done. Swaps applied:', swapsApplied.length);
        return { swapsApplied };
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // EXPORT
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.optPhases = window.DDOptim.optPhases || {};
    Object.assign(window.DDOptim.optPhases, {
        runPhase3,
        runPhase4
    });

    console.log('[OPT Phases 3-4] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300663 | OPT Engine - Orchestrator (runOPT entry point) ===
/**
 * OPT Engine â€” Orchestrator
 *
 * Public API: window.DDOptim.OPT.run(options)
 *
 * options: {
 *   network:   object    â€” current DDOptim network (nodes Map, profiles, etc.)
 *   budgetMax: number|null â€” max total inventory value (null = unlimited)
 * }
 *
 * Returns: {
 *   feasible:   bool,
 *   feasibilityReport: { structural, budget } | null,
 *   before:     { f0, f1, f2 },
 *   after:      { f0, f1, f2 },
 *   changes:    { added: [...], removed: [...], swaps: [...] },
 *   log:        string[]
 * }
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';

    console.log('[OPT Orchestrator] Loading...');

    /**
     * Run the full OPT algorithm.
     * The live network is modified in-place during execution.
     * On infeasibility, the network is restored to its pre-run state.
     * On success, the network reflects the optimized buffer positioning.
     */
    function run(options) {
        const { network, budgetMax = null } = options;

        if (!network || !network.nodes) {
            console.error('[OPT] No network provided');
            return { feasible: false, error: 'No network loaded' };
        }

        const core   = window.DDOptim.optCore;
        const phases = window.DDOptim.optPhases;

        if (!core || !phases) {
            console.error('[OPT] Core or phases modules not loaded');
            return { feasible: false, error: 'OPT engine modules not loaded' };
        }

        const log = [];
        const push = (msg) => { log.push(msg); console.log('[OPT]', msg); };

        // Snapshot to restore on infeasibility
        const preRunSnapshot = core.snapshotNetwork(network.nodes);
        const before = core.computeObjectives(network.nodes);

        push(`Starting OPT. Budget: ${budgetMax !== null ? 'â‚¬' + budgetMax.toFixed(2) : 'unlimited'}`);
        push(`Before â€” f0: ${before.f0.toFixed(1)}, f1: ${before.f1.toFixed(1)}, f2: â‚¬${before.f2.toFixed(2)}`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 0: Feasibility â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 0: Feasibility check...');
        const feasResult = core.runFeasibilityCheck(network, budgetMax);
        if (!feasResult.feasible) {
            push('Phase 0: INFEASIBLE â€” aborting.');
            core.restoreSnapshot(network.nodes, preRunSnapshot);
            core.fullRecalc(network);
            return {
                feasible: false,
                feasibilityReport: feasResult.report,
                before,
                after: null,
                changes: null,
                log
            };
        }
        push('Phase 0: âœ“ Feasible.');

        // Collect buffer changes vs pre-run state for the final report
        const preRunBuffers = new Set();
        network.nodes.forEach(node => {
            if (node.hasBuffer) preRunBuffers.add(node.id);
        });

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 1: Initialize â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 1: Initializing from zero buffers...');
        const p1 = phases.runPhase1(network);
        push(`Phase 1: âœ“ ${p1.buffersPreserved} locked buffer(s) preserved.`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 2a: Mandatory coverage â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 2a: Greedy coverage (mandatory nodes)...');
        const p2a = phases.runPhase2a(network, budgetMax);
        push(`Phase 2a: âœ“ Added ${p2a.buffersAdded.length} buffer(s). Final f0: ${p2a.finalF0.toFixed(1)}`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 2b: All demand node coverage â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 2b: Greedy coverage (all demand nodes)...');
        const p2b = phases.runPhase2b(network, budgetMax);
        push(`Phase 2b: âœ“ Added ${p2b.buffersAdded.length} buffer(s). Final f1: ${p2b.finalF1.toFixed(1)}`);

        // Lock f0 and f1 for reduction phases
        const lockedF0 = core.computeF0(network.nodes);
        const lockedF1 = core.computeF1(network.nodes);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 3: Greedy reduction â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 3: Greedy inventory reduction...');
        const p3 = phases.runPhase3(network, lockedF0, lockedF1);
        push(`Phase 3: âœ“ Removed ${p3.buffersRemoved.length} buffer(s). f2 reduced by â‚¬${p3.f2Reduction.toFixed(2)}`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Phase 4: Swap search â”€â”€â”€
        push('Phase 4: Swap search...');
        const p4 = phases.runPhase4(network, lockedF0, lockedF1, budgetMax);
        push(`Phase 4: âœ“ ${p4.swapsApplied.length} swap(s) applied.`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Compute final objectives â”€â”€â”€
        const after = core.computeObjectives(network.nodes);
        push(`After  â€” f0: ${after.f0.toFixed(1)}, f1: ${after.f1.toFixed(1)}, f2: â‚¬${after.f2.toFixed(2)}`);
        push(`f2 improvement: â‚¬${(before.f2 - after.f2).toFixed(2)} (${before.f2 > 0 ? ((before.f2 - after.f2) / before.f2 * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%)`);

        // â”€â”€â”€ Build change list vs pre-run state â”€â”€â”€
        const added   = [];
        const removed = [];
        network.nodes.forEach(node => {
            const wasBuffered = preRunBuffers.has(node.id);
            const isBuffered  = node.hasBuffer;
            if (!wasBuffered && isBuffered) {
                added.push({
                    nodeId: node.id,
                    product: node.product || node.id,
                    location: node.location || '',
                    inventoryValue: node.bufferSizing ? (node.bufferSizing.inventoryValue || 0) : 0
                });
            } else if (wasBuffered && !isBuffered) {
                removed.push({
                    nodeId: node.id,
                    product: node.product || node.id,
                    location: node.location || ''
                });
            }
        });

        push(`Net change: +${added.length} buffers added, -${removed.length} buffers removed.`);

        return {
            feasible: true,
            feasibilityReport: feasResult.report,
            before,
            after,
            changes: {
                added,
                removed,
                swaps: p4.swapsApplied,
                phase2aBuffers: p2a.buffersAdded,
                phase2bBuffers: p2b.buffersAdded,
                phase3Removals: p3.buffersRemoved
            },
            log
        };
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€ Export â”€â”€â”€
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.OPT = { run };

    console.log('[OPT Orchestrator] âœ“ Loaded. API: window.DDOptim.OPT.run(options)');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300700 | DLT Calculator ===
// DLT (Decoupled Lead Time) Calculator
// Calculates lead time from nearest buffer (or raw material) to each node
// Bottom-up calculation: children before parents (reverse topological order)
// Formula: DLT = leadTime + MAX(child DLT for unbuffered children only)
// Buffered children decouple the chain - their DLT is NOT propagated upward

const DLTCalculator = (function() {
  
  function calculateDLT(nodes) {
    console.log('ðŸ“Š DLT Calculator: Starting calculation...');
    
    // Convert Map to object if needed for topological sort
    const nodesObj = nodes instanceof Map ? Object.fromEntries(nodes) : nodes;
    
    // Get topological order (parents before children)
    const sorted = topologicalSort(nodesObj);
    
    if (!sorted) {
      console.error('âŒ DLT calculation failed: Invalid network topology');
      return false;
    }
    
    // Reverse the order for bottom-up processing (children before parents)
    const bottomUp = sorted.reverse();
    
    // Calculate DLT for each node (bottom-up)
    bottomUp.forEach(nodeId => {
      const node = nodes instanceof Map ? nodes.get(nodeId) : nodes[nodeId];
      if (!node) return;
      
      // Buffered node: DLT = own lead time only (buffer decouples from upstream)
      if (node.hasBuffer) {
        node.dlt = node.leadTime;
        console.log(`  ${node.product || node.id}: DLT = ${node.dlt} days (buffered node)`);
      } else if (!node.children || node.children.length === 0) {
        // Leaf nodes (no children): DLT = own lead time only
        node.dlt = node.leadTime;
        console.log(`  ${node.product || node.id}: DLT = ${node.dlt} days (leaf node)`);
      } else {
        // Non-leaf nodes: DLT = leadTime + MAX(unbuffered child DLT)
        let maxUnbufferedChildDLT = 0;
        let unbufferedCount = 0;
        let bufferedCount = 0;
        
        node.children.forEach(child => {
          const childNode = nodes instanceof Map ? nodes.get(child.id) : nodes[child.id];  // âœ“ Use child.id (canonical format)
          if (childNode) {
            if (childNode.hasBuffer) {
              // Buffered child - ignore its DLT (it decouples the chain)
              bufferedCount++;
            } else {
              // Unbuffered child - consider its DLT
              unbufferedCount++;
              if (childNode.dlt !== undefined && childNode.dlt !== null) {
                maxUnbufferedChildDLT = Math.max(maxUnbufferedChildDLT, childNode.dlt);
              }
            }
          }
        });
        
        node.dlt = node.leadTime + maxUnbufferedChildDLT;
        console.log(`  ${node.product || node.id}: DLT = ${node.leadTime} + ${maxUnbufferedChildDLT} = ${node.dlt} days (${unbufferedCount} unbuffered, ${bufferedCount} buffered children)`);
      }
    });
    
    console.log('âœ“ DLT calculation complete');
    return true;
  }
  
  return {
    calculateDLT
  };
})();

// Expose to window
window.DLTCalculator = DLTCalculator;
console.log('âœ“ DLT calculator loaded');


=== BLOCK: 0300800 | Network Renderer - D3.js Hierarchical Layout ===
// Network Renderer Module
const NetworkRenderer = (function() {
  let svg, g, zoom, nodes, links;
  let simulation;
  let tooltip;
  let currentTransform = null;
  const nodeWidth = 120;
  const nodeHeight = 50;
  const badgeRadius = 18;

  const TYPE_CONFIG = {
    'finished_product':          { color: '#3b82f6', label: 'Finished Product' },
    'intermediate':              { color: '#10b981', label: 'Intermediate' },
    'machined':                  { color: '#6b7280', label: 'Machined' },
    'purchased_local':           { color: '#f59e0b', label: 'Purchased Local' },
    'purchased_international':   { color: '#ef4444', label: 'Purchased International' }
  };

  const autoColorCache = {};

  // Color-by mode: 'type' (default) or 'location'
  let colorByMode = localStorage.getItem('ddoptim_color_by') || 'type';

  // Layout orientation: 'vertical' (top-down) or 'horizontal' (right-to-left, parents right)
  let layoutOrientation = localStorage.getItem('ddoptim_orientation') || 'vertical';

  function getAutoColor(key) {
    if (autoColorCache[key]) return autoColorCache[key];
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < key.length; i++) {
      hash = key.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
    }
    const hue = Math.abs(hash) % 360;
    const adjustedHue = (hue < 20 || (hue >= 100 && hue <= 150) || hue >= 340)
      ? (hue + 180) % 360 : hue;
    const color = `hsl(${adjustedHue}, 55%, 45%)`;
    autoColorCache[key] = color;
    return color;
  }

  // Distinct palette for locations (up to 8, then fallback to hash)
  const LOCATION_PALETTE = [
    '#7c3aed', '#0891b2', '#d97706', '#be123c',
    '#15803d', '#b45309', '#1d4ed8', '#9d174d'
  ];
  const locationColorMap = {};
  let locationColorIndex = 0;

  function getLocationColor(location) {
    if (!location) return '#9ca3af';
    if (locationColorMap[location]) return locationColorMap[location];
    const color = locationColorIndex < LOCATION_PALETTE.length
      ? LOCATION_PALETTE[locationColorIndex]
      : getAutoColor(location);
    locationColorMap[location] = color;
    locationColorIndex++;
    return color;
  }

  function resetLocationColors() {
    Object.keys(locationColorMap).forEach(k => delete locationColorMap[k]);
    locationColorIndex = 0;
  }

  function getTypeColor(type) {
    return TYPE_CONFIG[type] ? TYPE_CONFIG[type].color : getAutoColor(type);
  }

  function getTypeLabel(type) {
    if (TYPE_CONFIG[type]) return TYPE_CONFIG[type].label;
    return type.replace(/[_-]/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
  }

  // Unified color accessor used by the renderer
  function getNodeColor(node) {
    if (colorByMode === 'location') return getLocationColor(node.location);
    return getTypeColor(node.type);
  }

  function renderLegend(network) {
    const legendEl = document.getElementById('network-legend');
    if (!legendEl) return;
    const presentTypes = new Set();
    network.nodes.forEach(node => { if (node.type) presentTypes.add(node.type); });
    if (presentTypes.size === 0) { legendEl.innerHTML = ''; return; }

    // Restore collapsed state from localStorage
    const isCollapsed = localStorage.getItem('ddoptim_legend_collapsed') === 'true';
    if (isCollapsed) legendEl.classList.add('legend-collapsed');
    else legendEl.classList.remove('legend-collapsed');

    // Header with toggle button
    let html = `
      <div class="legend-header">
        <span class="legend-header-label">Legend</span>
        <button class="legend-toggle-btn" id="legend-toggle-btn" title="${isCollapsed ? 'Show legend' : 'Hide legend'}">
          ${isCollapsed ? 'â–²' : 'â–¼'}
        </button>
      </div>`;

    // Body (collapsible)
    html += '<div class="legend-body">';

    // Node Types or Locations section
    if (colorByMode === 'location') {
      html += '<div class="legend-title">Locations</div>';
      const presentLocations = new Set();
      network.nodes.forEach(node => { if (node.location) presentLocations.add(node.location); });
      presentLocations.forEach(loc => {
        html += `
          <div class="legend-item">
            <div class="legend-color" style="background: ${getLocationColor(loc)};"></div>
            <span>${loc}</span>
          </div>`;
      });
    } else {
      html += '<div class="legend-title">Node Types</div>';
      presentTypes.forEach(type => {
        html += `
          <div class="legend-item">
            <div class="legend-color" style="background: ${getTypeColor(type)};"></div>
            <span>${getTypeLabel(type)}</span>
          </div>`;
      });
    }

    // Link Types section â€” detect if any transport links exist
    let hasTransport = false;
    network.nodes.forEach(node => {
      if (node.children) {
        node.children.forEach(c => { if (c.linkType === 'transport') hasTransport = true; });
      }
    });

    html += '<div class="legend-title" style="margin-top: 10px;">Link Types</div>';
    html += `
      <div class="legend-item">
        <svg width="32" height="14" style="flex-shrink:0;">
          <line x1="2" y1="7" x2="30" y2="7" stroke="#6b7280" stroke-width="2"/>
          <polygon points="26,4 30,7 26,10" fill="#6b7280"/>
        </svg>
        <span>BOM</span>
      </div>`;
    if (hasTransport) {
      html += `
        <div class="legend-item">
          <svg width="32" height="14" style="flex-shrink:0;">
            <line x1="2" y1="7" x2="30" y2="7" stroke="#6b7280" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="5,3"/>
            <polygon points="26,4 30,7 26,10" fill="#6b7280"/>
          </svg>
          <span>Transport</span>
        </div>`;
    }

    html += '</div>'; // close legend-body

    legendEl.innerHTML = html;

    // Toggle button handler
    document.getElementById('legend-toggle-btn').addEventListener('click', function() {
      const collapsed = legendEl.classList.toggle('legend-collapsed');
      localStorage.setItem('ddoptim_legend_collapsed', collapsed);
      this.textContent = collapsed ? 'â–²' : 'â–¼';
      this.title = collapsed ? 'Show legend' : 'Hide legend';
    });
  }

  // --- COLLAPSE STATE ---
  const collapsedNodes = new Set();

  function getLeadTimeDisplay(node) { return node.leadTime; }

  function createBufferZonesSVG(node) {
    if (!node.hasBuffer || !node.bufferSizing) return '';
    const sizing = node.bufferSizing;
    const totalHeight = sizing.topOfGreen || 0;
    if (totalHeight === 0) return '';
    const red = sizing.red || 0;
    const yellow = sizing.yellow || 0;
    const green = sizing.green || 0;
    const MAX_BAR_HEIGHT = 150;
    const scale = Math.min(1, MAX_BAR_HEIGHT / totalHeight);
    const redHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(red * scale));
    const yellowHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(yellow * scale));
    const greenHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(green * scale));
    const startY = 20;
    const totalBarHeight = redHeight + yellowHeight + greenHeight;
    const redY = startY + totalBarHeight - redHeight;
    const yellowY = redY - yellowHeight;
    const greenY = yellowY - greenHeight;
    const svgHeight = 20 + totalBarHeight + 10;
    return `
      <svg width="140" height="${svgHeight}" style="margin: 8px 0;">
        <text x="70" y="12" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="600" fill="#374151">Buffer Zones</text>
        <rect x="20" y="${greenY}" width="80" height="${greenHeight}" fill="#d1fae5" stroke="#065f46" stroke-width="1"/>
        <text x="105" y="${greenY + greenHeight/2}" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="10" fill="#065f46">G: ${green}</text>
        <rect x="20" y="${yellowY}" width="80" height="${yellowHeight}" fill="#fef3c7" stroke="#92400e" stroke-width="1"/>
        <text x="105" y="${yellowY + yellowHeight/2}" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="10" fill="#92400e">Y: ${yellow}</text>
        <rect x="20" y="${redY}" width="80" height="${redHeight}" fill="#fee2e2" stroke="#991b1b" stroke-width="1"/>
        <text x="105" y="${redY + redHeight/2}" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="10" fill="#991b1b">R: ${red}</text>
        <text x="10" y="${greenY + greenHeight/2}" text-anchor="end" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="9" fill="#065f46" font-weight="600">G</text>
        <text x="10" y="${yellowY + yellowHeight/2}" text-anchor="end" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="9" fill="#92400e" font-weight="600">Y</text>
        <text x="10" y="${redY + redHeight/2}" text-anchor="end" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="9" fill="#991b1b" font-weight="600">R</text>
      </svg>
    `;
  }

  function buildTooltipHTML(node) {
    if (!node) return '';
    const typeLabels = {
      'finished_product': 'Finished Product',
      'intermediate': 'Intermediate',
      'machined': 'Machined',
      'purchased_local': 'Purchased Local',
      'purchased_international': 'Purchased International'
    };
    let leftColumn = `
      <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 240px;">
        <div style="font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; color: #111827; margin-bottom: 4px;">
          ${node.product || node.id}
        </div>
        ${node.location ? `<div style="font-size: 10px; color: #9ca3af; margin-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #e5e7eb; padding-bottom: 6px;">ðŸ“ ${node.location}</div>` : `<div style="border-bottom: 2px solid #e5e7eb; margin-bottom: 8px; padding-bottom: 6px;"></div>`}
        <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280; margin-bottom: ${node.description ? '4px' : '10px'};">
          ${typeLabels[node.type] || node.type}
        </div>
        ${node.description ? `<div style="font-size: 10px; color: #9ca3af; font-style: italic; margin-bottom: 10px;">${node.description}</div>` : ''}
    `;
    if (node.missingCustomerLeadTime && node.missingCustomerLeadTime > 0) {
      leftColumn += `
        <div style="background: #fef2f2; border: 2px solid #ef4444; border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;">
            <div style="background: #ef4444; color: white; border-radius: 50%; width: 20px; height: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: 700; font-size: 12px;">!</div>
            <div style="font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; color: #991b1b;">Customer Tolerance Gap</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #991b1b; margin-top: 4px; margin-left: 26px;">
            Gap: ${node.missingCustomerLeadTime.toFixed(1)} days<br/>
            Current DLT (${node.dlt}d) exceeds requirement (${node.customerTolerance || 0}d)
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }
    if (node.ltExceeding && node.ltExceeding > 0 && node.independentADU > 0) {
      leftColumn += `
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #10b981; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 8px;">
          Customer tolerance margin: ${node.ltExceeding.toFixed(1)} d
        </div>
      `;
    }
    if (node.toleranceMandatory && node.independentADU > 0) {
      leftColumn += `
        <div style="font-size: 10px; background: #fef3c7; border: 1px solid #f59e0b; border-radius: 4px; padding: 3px 6px; display: inline-block; margin-bottom: 8px; font-weight: 600; color: #92400e;">
          ðŸ”’ Hard constraint (OPT)
        </div>
      `;
    }
    leftColumn += `
      <div style="margin-bottom: 8px;">
        <div style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #374151; margin-bottom: 4px;">Lead Times</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280; line-height: 1.4;">
          <div>Immediate: ${node.leadTime}d</div>
          <div>Cumulative (CLT): ${node.clt !== undefined ? node.clt + 'd' : 'N/A'}</div>
          <div>Decoupled (DLT): ${node.dlt !== undefined ? node.dlt + 'd' : 'N/A'}</div>
    `;
    if (node.customerTolerance !== undefined && node.customerTolerance !== null) {
      leftColumn += `<div>Customer Requirement: ${node.customerTolerance}d</div>`;
    }
    leftColumn += `</div></div>`;
    leftColumn += `
      <div style="margin-bottom: 8px;">
        <div style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #374151; margin-bottom: 4px;">Average Daily Usage</div>
        <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280; line-height: 1.4;">
    `;
    if (node.independentADU > 0) leftColumn += `<div>Independent: ${node.independentADU.toFixed(1)} units/day</div>`;
    if (node.calculatedADU !== undefined && node.calculatedADU !== null) {
      leftColumn += `<div>Total (Calculated): ${node.calculatedADU.toFixed(1)} units/day</div>`;
    }
    leftColumn += `</div></div>`;
    if ((node.moq && node.moq > 0) || (node.orderCycle && node.orderCycle > 0)) {
      leftColumn += `
        <div style="margin-bottom: 8px;">
          <div style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #374151; margin-bottom: 4px;">Constraints</div>
          <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280; line-height: 1.4;">
      `;
      if (node.moq && node.moq > 0) leftColumn += `<div>MOQ: ${node.moq} units</div>`;
      if (node.orderCycle && node.orderCycle > 0) leftColumn += `<div>Order Cycle: ${node.orderCycle} days</div>`;
      leftColumn += `</div></div>`;
    }
    if (node.unitCost !== undefined && node.unitCost !== null) {
      leftColumn += `<div style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280;">Unit Cost: â‚¬${node.unitCost.toLocaleString()}</div>`;
    }
    leftColumn += `</div>`;
    let rightColumn = '';
    if (node.hasBuffer) {
      rightColumn = `
        <div style="flex: 0 0 180px; border-left: 2px solid #e5e7eb; padding-left: 12px; margin-left: 12px;">
          <div style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #374151; margin-bottom: 8px;">Strategic Buffer</div>
          <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px;">
            <div>Profile: ${node.bufferProfile || 'N/A'}</div>
      `;
      if (node.bufferSizing) {
        rightColumn += `
            <div>Avg Stock: ${Math.round(node.bufferSizing.averageStock || 0)} units</div>
            <div>Value: â‚¬${(node.bufferSizing.inventoryValue || 0).toLocaleString()}</div>
        `;
      }
      rightColumn += `</div>`;
      rightColumn += createBufferZonesSVG(node);
      rightColumn += `</div>`;
    }
    return `
      <div style="font-family: 'Figtree', sans-serif; padding: 12px; display: flex; gap: 0; min-width: ${node.hasBuffer ? '500px' : '280px'}; max-width: 650px;">
        ${leftColumn}${rightColumn}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function init() {
    const container = d3.select('#network-svg');
    const width = container.node().clientWidth;
    const height = container.node().clientHeight;
    svg = container.attr('width', width).attr('height', height);
    g = svg.append('g');
    zoom = d3.zoom()
      .scaleExtent([0.1, 4])
      .on('zoom', (event) => {
        g.attr('transform', event.transform);
        currentTransform = event.transform;
      });
    svg.call(zoom);
    d3.select('#zoom-in').on('click', () => { svg.transition().call(zoom.scaleBy, 1.3); });
    d3.select('#zoom-out').on('click', () => { svg.transition().call(zoom.scaleBy, 0.7); });
    d3.select('#zoom-reset').on('click', () => {
      svg.transition().call(zoom.transform, d3.zoomIdentity);
      currentTransform = null;
    });
    tooltip = d3.select('body').append('div').attr('class', 'ddoptim-tooltip');

    // Color-by toggle buttons
    function updateColorByButtons() {
      const btnType = document.getElementById('color-by-type');
      const btnLoc  = document.getElementById('color-by-location');
      if (!btnType || !btnLoc) return;
      if (colorByMode === 'location') {
        btnType.style.background = 'white';
        btnType.style.color = '#6b7280';
        btnLoc.style.background = '#2563eb';
        btnLoc.style.color = 'white';
      } else {
        btnType.style.background = '#2563eb';
        btnType.style.color = 'white';
        btnLoc.style.background = 'white';
        btnLoc.style.color = '#6b7280';
      }
    }
    updateColorByButtons();

    document.getElementById('color-by-type').addEventListener('click', function() {
      if (colorByMode === 'type') return;
      colorByMode = 'type';
      localStorage.setItem('ddoptim_color_by', 'type');
      updateColorByButtons();
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (network) {
        if (window.FocusManager?.isActive()) {
          window.FocusManager.activate(window.FocusManager.getFocusNodeId());
        } else {
          NetworkRenderer.render(network, true);
        }
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('color-by-location').addEventListener('click', function() {
      if (colorByMode === 'location') return;
      colorByMode = 'location';
      localStorage.setItem('ddoptim_color_by', 'location');
      resetLocationColors();
      updateColorByButtons();
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (network) {
        if (window.FocusManager?.isActive()) {
          window.FocusManager.activate(window.FocusManager.getFocusNodeId());
        } else {
          NetworkRenderer.render(network, true);
        }
      }
    });

    // Layout orientation toggle
    function updateOrientationButtons() {
      const btnV = document.getElementById('layout-vertical-btn');
      const btnH = document.getElementById('layout-horizontal-btn');
      if (!btnV || !btnH) return;
      if (layoutOrientation === 'horizontal') {
        btnV.style.background = 'white';
        btnV.style.color = '#6b7280';
        btnH.style.background = '#2563eb';
        btnH.style.color = 'white';
      } else {
        btnV.style.background = '#2563eb';
        btnV.style.color = 'white';
        btnH.style.background = 'white';
        btnH.style.color = '#6b7280';
      }
    }
    updateOrientationButtons();

    document.getElementById('layout-vertical-btn').addEventListener('click', function() {
      if (layoutOrientation === 'vertical') return;
      layoutOrientation = 'vertical';
      localStorage.setItem('ddoptim_orientation', 'vertical');
      updateOrientationButtons();
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (network) {
        if (window.FocusManager?.isActive()) {
          window.FocusManager.activate(window.FocusManager.getFocusNodeId());
        } else {
          NetworkRenderer.render(network, false);
        }
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('layout-horizontal-btn').addEventListener('click', function() {
      if (layoutOrientation === 'horizontal') return;
      layoutOrientation = 'horizontal';
      localStorage.setItem('ddoptim_orientation', 'horizontal');
      updateOrientationButtons();
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (network) {
        if (window.FocusManager?.isActive()) {
          window.FocusManager.activate(window.FocusManager.getFocusNodeId());
        } else {
          NetworkRenderer.render(network, false);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  // SHARED NODE DEDUPLICATION
  //
  // buildTree()  tracks visitedNodes:
  //   - 1st encounter â†’ place node normally in D3 tree
  //   - 2nd+ encounter â†’ skip node, record a secondaryLink
  //     { sourceNodeId, targetNodeId }
  //
  // After d3.tree() layout, for each shared node (parentCount > 1):
  //   - Collect X positions of ALL its parents (primary + secondary)
  //   - Set node.x = average(parent X positions)  â†’ centered under parents
  //
  // Secondary links are drawn with the same style as primary links.
  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function computeParentCounts(network) {
    const counts = new Map();
    network.nodes.forEach((node, id) => counts.set(id, 0));
    network.nodes.forEach((node) => {
      if (node.children) {
        node.children.forEach(childRef => {
          if (counts.has(childRef.id)) counts.set(childRef.id, counts.get(childRef.id) + 1);
        });
      }
    });
    return counts;
  }

  function prepareHierarchy(network) {
    const roots = Array.from(network.nodes.values())
      .filter(node => !node.parents || node.parents.length === 0);

    console.log('ðŸŒ³ Building hierarchy from roots:', roots.map(n => n.product || n.id));

    const visitedNodes = new Set();
    const secondaryLinks = []; // { sourceNodeId, targetNodeId }

    function buildTree(nodeId, parentNodeId) {
      const node = network.nodes.get(nodeId);
      if (!node) return null;

      if (visitedNodes.has(nodeId)) {
        // Already placed: record secondary link, do not duplicate
        if (parentNodeId !== null) {
          // Find the linkType from the parent's children array
          const parentNode = network.nodes.get(parentNodeId);
          const childRef = parentNode && parentNode.children
            ? parentNode.children.find(c => c.id === nodeId)
            : null;
          secondaryLinks.push({
            sourceNodeId: parentNodeId,
            targetNodeId: nodeId,
            linkType: childRef ? (childRef.linkType || 'bom') : 'bom'
          });
        }
        return null;
      }

      visitedNodes.add(nodeId);

      const children = [];
      if (!collapsedNodes.has(nodeId) && node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
        node.children.forEach(childRef => {
          const child = buildTree(childRef.id, nodeId);
          if (child) {
            child.quantity = childRef.quantity;
            child.linkType = childRef.linkType || 'bom';
            children.push(child);
          }
        });
      }

      return {
        id: nodeId,
        data: node,
        hasBOMChildren: node.children && node.children.length > 0,
        children: children.length > 0 ? children : null
      };
    }

    let root;
    if (roots.length === 0) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ No root nodes found');
      root = { id: 'root', children: [] };
    } else if (roots.length === 1) {
      root = buildTree(roots[0].id, null);
    } else {
      const sortedRoots = sortRootsBySharedChildren(roots, network);
      root = {
        id: 'virtual-root',
        children: sortedRoots.map(r => buildTree(r.id, null)).filter(Boolean)
      };
    }

    console.log(`ðŸ”— Secondary links (shared nodes): ${secondaryLinks.length}`, secondaryLinks);
    return { root, secondaryLinks };
  }

  // Sort roots so parents sharing children end up adjacent (greedy nearest-neighbour)
  function sortRootsBySharedChildren(roots, network) {
    if (roots.length <= 2) return roots;
    function getChildIds(node) {
      return new Set((node.children || []).map(c => c.id));
    }
    function sharedCount(a, b) {
      const setA = getChildIds(a);
      let n = 0;
      getChildIds(b).forEach(id => { if (setA.has(id)) n++; });
      return n;
    }
    const ordered = [roots[0]];
    const remaining = roots.slice(1);
    while (remaining.length > 0) {
      const last = ordered[ordered.length - 1];
      let bestIdx = 0, bestScore = -1;
      remaining.forEach((r, i) => {
        const s = sharedCount(last, r);
        if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; bestIdx = i; }
      });
      ordered.push(remaining.splice(bestIdx, 1)[0]);
    }
    return ordered;
  }


  // â”€â”€â”€ Badge helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function addBufferIcon(nodeGroup) {
    const bufferWidth = 30, bufferHeight = 20;
    const bufferX = nodeWidth / 2 - bufferWidth - 5;
    const bufferY = -nodeHeight / 2 + 3;
    const bufferGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'buffer-icon')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${bufferX}, ${bufferY})`);
    const topWidth = bufferWidth;
    const bottomWidth = bufferWidth * 0.7;
    const widthDiff = (topWidth - bottomWidth) / 2;
    const bandHeight = bufferHeight / 3;
    bufferGroup.append('path')
      .attr('d', `M ${widthDiff} ${bufferHeight} L 0 ${bufferHeight - bandHeight} L ${topWidth} ${bufferHeight - bandHeight} L ${topWidth - widthDiff} ${bufferHeight} Z`)
      .attr('fill', '#ef4444').attr('stroke', '#991b1b').attr('stroke-width', 0.5);
    bufferGroup.append('path')
      .attr('d', `M 0 ${bufferHeight - bandHeight} L 0 ${bufferHeight - 2*bandHeight} L ${topWidth} ${bufferHeight - 2*bandHeight} L ${topWidth} ${bufferHeight - bandHeight} Z`)
      .attr('fill', '#fbbf24').attr('stroke', '#92400e').attr('stroke-width', 0.5);
    bufferGroup.append('path')
      .attr('d', `M 0 ${bufferHeight - 2*bandHeight} L 0 0 L ${topWidth} 0 L ${topWidth} ${bufferHeight - 2*bandHeight} Z`)
      .attr('fill', '#10b981').attr('stroke', '#065f46').attr('stroke-width', 0.5);
  }

  function addIndependentADUBadge(nodeGroup, node) {
    if (!node.independentADU || node.independentADU <= 0) return;
    const badgeX = nodeWidth / 2, badgeY = -nodeHeight / 2;
    const aduGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'adu-badge')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${badgeX}, ${badgeY})`);
    const aduText = node.independentADU % 1 === 0
      ? node.independentADU.toString() : node.independentADU.toFixed(1);
    const fontSize = 10, padding = 4;
    const estimatedWidth = aduText.length * (fontSize * 0.6) + padding * 2;
    const badgeHeight = 16;
    aduGroup.append('rect')
      .attr('x', 0).attr('y', 0)
      .attr('width', estimatedWidth).attr('height', badgeHeight)
      .attr('rx', 3).attr('ry', 3)
      .attr('fill', '#3b82f6').attr('stroke', '#1e40af').attr('stroke-width', 1);
    aduGroup.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'adu-badge-text')
      .attr('x', estimatedWidth / 2).attr('y', badgeHeight / 2)
      .attr('dy', '0.35em').attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .style('font-size', `${fontSize}px`).style('font-weight', '600').style('fill', 'white')
      .text(aduText);
  }

  function addCustomerLeadTimeGapBadge(nodeGroup, node) {
    if (!node.missingCustomerLeadTime || node.missingCustomerLeadTime <= 0) return;
    const alertGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'alert-badge')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${nodeWidth / 2}, ${nodeHeight / 2})`);
    alertGroup.append('circle')
      .attr('cx', 0).attr('cy', 0).attr('r', 10)
      .attr('fill', '#ef4444').attr('stroke', '#991b1b').attr('stroke-width', 2);
    alertGroup.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'alert-icon')
      .attr('x', 0).attr('y', 0).attr('dy', '0.35em').attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .style('font-size', '14px').style('font-weight', '700').style('fill', 'white')
      .text('!');
  }

  function addOpportunityBadge(nodeGroup, node) {
    if (!node.ltExceeding || node.ltExceeding <= 0 || !node.independentADU || node.independentADU <= 0) return;
    const opportunityGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'opportunity-badge')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${nodeWidth / 2}, ${nodeHeight / 2})`);
    opportunityGroup.append('circle')
      .attr('cx', 0).attr('cy', 0).attr('r', 10)
      .attr('fill', '#10b981').attr('stroke', '#065f46').attr('stroke-width', 2);
    opportunityGroup.append('path')
      .attr('d', 'M-3,-6 L2,-1 L0,-1 L3,6 L-2,1 L0,1 Z')
      .attr('fill', 'white').attr('stroke', 'none');
  }

  function addSharedBadge(nodeGroup, parentCount) {
    const sharedGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'shared-badge')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${-nodeWidth / 2}, ${nodeHeight / 2})`);
    const label = `${parentCount}Ã—`;
    const fontSize = 9, padding = 3;
    const bw = label.length * (fontSize * 0.65) + padding * 2;
    const bh = 14;
    sharedGroup.append('rect')
      .attr('x', -bw).attr('y', 0)
      .attr('width', bw).attr('height', bh)
      .attr('rx', 3).attr('ry', 3)
      .attr('fill', '#6b7280').attr('stroke', '#374151').attr('stroke-width', 1);
    sharedGroup.append('text')
      .attr('x', -bw / 2).attr('y', bh / 2)
      .attr('dy', '0.35em').attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .style('font-size', `${fontSize}px`).style('font-weight', '600').style('fill', 'white')
      .text(label);
  }

  function addCollapseButton(nodeGroup, nodeId) {
    const btnRadius = 9;
    const isCollapsed = collapsedNodes.has(nodeId);
    const btnGroup = nodeGroup.append('g')
      .attr('class', 'collapse-btn')
      .attr('transform', `translate(0, ${nodeHeight / 2})`)
      .style('cursor', 'pointer');
    btnGroup.append('circle')
      .attr('cx', 0).attr('cy', 0).attr('r', btnRadius)
      .attr('fill', 'white').attr('stroke', '#9ca3af').attr('stroke-width', 1.5);
    btnGroup.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'collapse-btn-symbol')
      .attr('x', 0).attr('y', 0).attr('dy', '0.35em').attr('text-anchor', 'middle')
      .style('font-size', '13px').style('font-weight', '700').style('fill', '#374151')
      .style('pointer-events', 'none')
      .text(isCollapsed ? '+' : 'âˆ’');
    btnGroup.on('click', function(event) {
      event.stopPropagation();
      if (collapsedNodes.has(nodeId)) collapsedNodes.delete(nodeId);
      else collapsedNodes.add(nodeId);
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (network) {
        if (window.FocusManager?.isActive()) {
          window.FocusManager.activate(window.FocusManager.getFocusNodeId());
        } else {
          NetworkRenderer.render(network, true);
        }
      }
    });
    btnGroup
      .on('mouseover', function(event) {
        event.stopPropagation();
        d3.select(this).select('circle').attr('fill', '#f3f4f6').attr('stroke', '#6b7280');
        tooltip.classed('visible', false);
      })
      .on('mouseout', function(event) {
        event.stopPropagation();
        d3.select(this).select('circle').attr('fill', 'white').attr('stroke', '#9ca3af');
      });
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Main render â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function render(network, preserveView = false, focusNodeIds = null) {
    d3.select('#network-loading').style('display', 'none');
    g.selectAll('*').remove();

    // Apply focus filter: build a filtered network view if focusNodeIds provided
    let renderNetwork = network;
    if (focusNodeIds && focusNodeIds.size > 0) {
      const filteredNodes = new Map();
      network.nodes.forEach((node, id) => {
        if (focusNodeIds.has(id)) filteredNodes.set(id, node);
      });
      renderNetwork = { ...network, nodes: filteredNodes };
    }

    const parentCounts = computeParentCounts(network); // use full model, not focus-filtered view
    const { root: hierarchyRoot, secondaryLinks } = prepareHierarchy(renderNetwork);
    const root = d3.hierarchy(hierarchyRoot);

    const width = svg.node().clientWidth;
    const height = svg.node().clientHeight;

    const levels = {};
    root.each(node => { levels[node.depth] = (levels[node.depth] || 0) + 1; });
    const maxNodesAtLevel = Math.max(...Object.values(levels));

    // Compute max depth for horizontal axis inversion
    let maxDepth = 0;
    root.each(node => { if (node.depth > maxDepth) maxDepth = node.depth; });

    const isHorizontal = layoutOrientation === 'horizontal';

    // In vertical mode: tree spreads horizontally, depth goes down (y axis)
    // In horizontal mode: tree spreads vertically, depth goes rightâ†’left (x axis inverted)
    let treeLayout;
    if (isHorizontal) {
      const calculatedHeight = Math.max(height - 100, maxNodesAtLevel * 220);
      treeLayout = d3.tree()
        .size([calculatedHeight, width - 100])
        .separation((a, b) => {
          if (!a.children && !b.children) return a.parent === b.parent ? 2.0 : 2.5;
          return a.parent === b.parent ? 1.5 : 2.0;
        });
    } else {
      const calculatedWidth = Math.max(width, maxNodesAtLevel * 220);
      treeLayout = d3.tree()
        .size([calculatedWidth, height - 100])
        .separation((a, b) => {
          if (!a.children && !b.children) return a.parent === b.parent ? 2.0 : 2.5;
          return a.parent === b.parent ? 1.5 : 2.0;
        });
    }

    treeLayout(root);

    // Build nodePositions map from D3 layout
    // For horizontal: swap xâ†”y and invert depth axis so roots appear on the right
    const nodePositions = new Map();
    root.descendants().forEach(d => {
      if (d.data.id && d.data.id !== 'virtual-root') {
        if (isHorizontal) {
          // d.x = spread (vertical position), d.y = depth
          // We want roots (depth=0) at right, leaves at left
          // So: px = (width-100) - d.y, py = d.x
          nodePositions.set(d.data.id, { x: (width - 100) - d.y, y: d.x });
        } else {
          nodePositions.set(d.data.id, { x: d.x, y: d.y });
        }
      }
    });

    const treeNodes = root.descendants();
    const treeLinks = root.links();

    // Helper: get position for a D3 node (primary tree node)
    function getPrimaryPos(d) {
      if (isHorizontal) {
        return { x: (width - 100) - d.y, y: d.x };
      }
      return { x: d.x, y: d.y };
    }

    // â”€â”€ Primary links â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    g.selectAll('.link')
      .data(treeLinks.filter(d => d.source.data.id !== 'virtual-root'))
      .enter()
      .append('path')
      .attr('class', d => d.target.data.linkType === 'transport' ? 'link link-transport' : 'link')
      .attr('d', d => {
        const src = getPrimaryPos(d.source);
        const pos = nodePositions.get(d.target.data.id);
        const tx = pos ? pos.x : getPrimaryPos(d.target).x;
        const ty = pos ? pos.y : getPrimaryPos(d.target).y;
        if (isHorizontal) {
          // Horizontal bezier: control points move along x axis
          const mx = (src.x + tx) / 2;
          return `M ${src.x} ${src.y} C ${mx} ${src.y}, ${mx} ${ty}, ${tx} ${ty}`;
        } else {
          return `M ${src.x} ${src.y} C ${src.x} ${(src.y + ty) / 2}, ${tx} ${(src.y + ty) / 2}, ${tx} ${ty}`;
        }
      });

    // BOM quantity labels
    const formatQty = q => {
      if (q === undefined || q === null) return '';
      if (q < 0.01) return q.toPrecision(2);
      if (q % 1 === 0) return String(q);
      return String(parseFloat(q.toFixed(4)));
    };
    const linksWithQty = treeLinks.filter(d => {
      const qty = d.target.data.quantity;
      return d.target.data.linkType !== 'transport' && qty !== undefined && qty !== null && qty !== 1;
    });
    const linkLabelSelection = g.selectAll('.link-label')
      .data(linksWithQty)
      .enter()
      .append('g')
      .attr('class', 'link-label')
      .attr('transform', d => {
        const src = getPrimaryPos(d.source);
        const pos = nodePositions.get(d.target.data.id);
        const tx = pos ? pos.x : getPrimaryPos(d.target).x;
        const ty = pos ? pos.y : getPrimaryPos(d.target).y;
        return `translate(${(src.x + tx) / 2}, ${(src.y + ty) / 2})`;
      });
    linkLabelSelection.each(function(d) {
      const group = d3.select(this);
      const label = formatQty(d.target.data.quantity);
      const fontSize = 11;
      const padding = 5;
      const bw = Math.max(24, label.length * (fontSize * 0.62) + padding * 2);
      const bh = 18;
      group.append('rect')
        .attr('x', -bw / 2).attr('y', -bh / 2)
        .attr('width', bw).attr('height', bh)
        .attr('rx', 6).attr('ry', 6)
        .attr('fill', 'white').attr('stroke', '#6b7280').attr('stroke-width', 1.5);
      group.append('text')
        .attr('class', 'link-quantity')
        .attr('text-anchor', 'middle').attr('dy', '0.35em')
        .style('font-size', `${fontSize}px`).style('font-weight', '600').style('fill', '#374151')
        .text(label);
    });

    // â”€â”€ Secondary links (shared nodes) â€” same style as primary â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    const validSecondaryLinks = secondaryLinks.filter(sl =>
      nodePositions.has(sl.sourceNodeId) && nodePositions.has(sl.targetNodeId)
    );
    g.selectAll('.link-secondary')
      .data(validSecondaryLinks)
      .enter()
      .append('path')
      .attr('class', sl => sl.linkType === 'transport' ? 'link link-transport' : 'link')
      .attr('d', sl => {
        const src = nodePositions.get(sl.sourceNodeId);
        const tgt = nodePositions.get(sl.targetNodeId);
        if (isHorizontal) {
          const mx = (src.x + tgt.x) / 2;
          return `M ${src.x} ${src.y} C ${mx} ${src.y}, ${mx} ${tgt.y}, ${tgt.x} ${tgt.y}`;
        } else {
          const cy = (src.y + tgt.y) / 2;
          return `M ${src.x} ${src.y} C ${src.x} ${cy}, ${tgt.x} ${cy}, ${tgt.x} ${tgt.y}`;
        }
      });

    // â”€â”€ Nodes â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    const nodeSelection = g.selectAll('.node')
      .data(treeNodes.filter(d => d.data.id !== 'virtual-root'))
      .enter()
      .append('g')
      .attr('class', d => {
        const node = d.data.data;
        if (!node) return 'node';
        const classes = ['node'];
        if (node.missingCustomerLeadTime && node.missingCustomerLeadTime > 0) classes.push('has-gap');
        return classes.join(' ');
      })
      // Use nodePositions for placement (accounts for recentered shared nodes)
      .attr('transform', d => {
        const pos = nodePositions.get(d.data.id);
        return pos ? `translate(${pos.x},${pos.y})` : `translate(${d.x},${d.y})`;
      })
      .attr('data-node-id', d => d.data.id)
      .on('click', (event, d) => { if (d.data.data) selectNode(d.data.data.id); })
      .on('mouseover', (event, d) => {
        if (!d.data.data) return;
        tooltip
          .html(buildTooltipHTML(d.data.data))
          .style('left', (event.pageX + 15) + 'px')
          .style('top', (event.pageY - 10) + 'px')
          .classed('visible', true);
      })
      .on('mousemove', (event) => {
        tooltip.style('left', (event.pageX + 15) + 'px').style('top', (event.pageY - 10) + 'px');
      })
      .on('mouseout', () => { tooltip.classed('visible', false); });

    nodeSelection.append('rect')
      .attr('x', -nodeWidth / 2).attr('y', -nodeHeight / 2)
      .attr('width', nodeWidth).attr('height', nodeHeight)
      .attr('fill', d => { const node = d.data.data; return node ? getNodeColor(node) : '#9ca3af'; });

    nodeSelection.append('circle')
      .attr('class', 'leadtime-badge')
      .attr('cx', -nodeWidth / 2).attr('cy', -nodeHeight / 2).attr('r', badgeRadius);
    nodeSelection.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'leadtime-text')
      .attr('x', -nodeWidth / 2).attr('y', -nodeHeight / 2)
      .text(d => { const node = d.data.data; return node ? `${getLeadTimeDisplay(node)}d` : ''; });

    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.data && d.data.data.hasBuffer)
      .each(function() { addBufferIcon(d3.select(this)); });
    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.data && d.data.data.independentADU > 0)
      .each(function(d) { addIndependentADUBadge(d3.select(this), d.data.data); });
    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.data && d.data.data.missingCustomerLeadTime > 0)
      .each(function(d) { addCustomerLeadTimeGapBadge(d3.select(this), d.data.data); });
    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.data && d.data.data.ltExceeding > 0 && d.data.data.independentADU > 0)
      .each(function(d) { addOpportunityBadge(d3.select(this), d.data.data); });

    // Shared badge for multi-parent nodes
    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.data && (parentCounts.get(d.data.data.id) || 0) > 1)
      .each(function(d) { addSharedBadge(d3.select(this), parentCounts.get(d.data.data.id)); });

    nodeSelection.filter(d => d.data.hasBOMChildren)
      .each(function(d) { addCollapseButton(d3.select(this), d.data.id); });

    const showLoc = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.showLocations;
    nodeSelection.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'node-name')
      .attr('dy', showLoc ? '-0.4em' : '0.35em')
      .text(d => {
        if (!d.data.data) return '';
        const name = d.data.data.product || d.data.data.id;
        return name.length > 14 ? name.substring(0, 14) + 'â€¦' : name;
      });

    if (showLoc) {
      nodeSelection.append('text')
        .attr('class', 'node-location')
        .attr('dy', '0.9em')
        .style('font-size', '9px')
        .style('fill', 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)')
        .text(d => {
          if (!d.data.data || !d.data.data.location) return '';
          const loc = d.data.data.location;
          return loc.length > 16 ? loc.substring(0, 16) + 'â€¦' : loc;
        });
    }

    // â”€â”€ Auto-center or restore view â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    if (preserveView && currentTransform) {
      svg.call(zoom.transform, currentTransform);
    } else {
      const bounds = g.node().getBBox();
      const fullWidth = svg.node().clientWidth;
      const fullHeight = svg.node().clientHeight;
      const scale = 0.9 / Math.max(bounds.width / fullWidth, bounds.height / fullHeight);
      const translate = [
        fullWidth / 2 - scale * (bounds.x + bounds.width / 2),
        fullHeight / 2 - scale * (bounds.y + bounds.height / 2)
      ];
      const newTransform = d3.zoomIdentity.translate(translate[0], translate[1]).scale(scale);
      svg.call(zoom.transform, newTransform);
      currentTransform = newTransform;
    }

    renderLegend(renderNetwork);
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Update single node appearance â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function updateNodeAppearance(nodeId) {
    const currentNetwork = window.currentNetwork;
    if (!currentNetwork || !currentNetwork.nodes) return;
    const node = currentNetwork.nodes.get ? currentNetwork.nodes.get(nodeId) : currentNetwork.nodes[nodeId];
    if (!node) return;
    const nodeGroup = g.selectAll('.node').filter(d => d.data.id === nodeId);
    if (nodeGroup.empty()) return;
    const classes = ['node'];
    if (node.missingCustomerLeadTime && node.missingCustomerLeadTime > 0) classes.push('has-gap');
    nodeGroup.attr('class', classes.join(' '));
    nodeGroup.select('rect').attr('fill', getNodeColor(node));
    nodeGroup.select('.buffer-icon').remove();
    if (node.hasBuffer) addBufferIcon(nodeGroup);
    nodeGroup.select('.adu-badge').remove();
    if (node.independentADU > 0) addIndependentADUBadge(nodeGroup, node);
    nodeGroup.select('.alert-badge').remove();
    nodeGroup.select('.opportunity-badge').remove();
    if (node.missingCustomerLeadTime && node.missingCustomerLeadTime > 0) {
      addCustomerLeadTimeGapBadge(nodeGroup, node);
    } else if (node.ltExceeding && node.ltExceeding > 0 && node.independentADU > 0) {
      addOpportunityBadge(nodeGroup, node);
    }
    console.log(`âœ“ Updated visualization for ${node.product || node.id}`);
  }

  function selectNode(nodeId) {
    g.selectAll('.node').classed('selected', false);
    g.selectAll('.node')
      .filter(d => d.data.data && d.data.data.id === nodeId)
      .classed('selected', true);
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect) {
      window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect(nodeId);
    } else {
      console.error('âŒ onNodeSelect handler not found!');
    }
  }

  return { init, render, selectNode, updateNodeAppearance };
})();

window.NetworkRenderer = NetworkRenderer;
console.log('âœ“ NetworkRenderer ready (shared-node recentering enabled)');


=== BLOCK: 0300850 | Focus Manager ===
/**
 * Focus Manager
 * Filters network view to show only ancestors and descendants of a selected node
 */
const FocusManager = (function() {
    'use strict';

    let focusNodeId = null;

    /**
     * Collect all ancestor node IDs (upward: toward finished goods)
     */
    function getAncestors(nodeId, nodes) {
        const visited = new Set();
        const queue = [nodeId];
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            const current = queue.shift();
            if (visited.has(current)) continue;
            visited.add(current);
            const node = nodes.get(current);
            if (!node) continue;
            (node.parents || []).forEach(parentRef => {
                const pid = typeof parentRef === 'string' ? parentRef : parentRef.id;
                if (pid && !visited.has(pid)) queue.push(pid);
            });
        }
        visited.delete(nodeId); // exclude self (added separately)
        return visited;
    }

    /**
     * Collect all descendant node IDs (downward: toward raw materials)
     */
    function getDescendants(nodeId, nodes) {
        const visited = new Set();
        const queue = [nodeId];
        while (queue.length > 0) {
            const current = queue.shift();
            if (visited.has(current)) continue;
            visited.add(current);
            const node = nodes.get(current);
            if (!node) continue;
            (node.children || []).forEach(childRef => {
                const cid = typeof childRef === 'string' ? childRef : childRef.id;
                if (cid && !visited.has(cid)) queue.push(cid);
            });
        }
        visited.delete(nodeId); // exclude self (added separately)
        return visited;
    }

    /**
     * Activate focus mode on a node
     */
    function activate(nodeId) {
        const network = window.currentNetwork;
        if (!network || !network.nodes) {
            console.warn('[FocusManager] No network loaded');
            return;
        }

        const node = network.nodes.get(nodeId);
        if (!node) {
            console.warn('[FocusManager] Node not found:', nodeId);
            return;
        }

        focusNodeId = nodeId;

        // Build full visible set: self + ancestors + descendants
        const ancestors = getAncestors(nodeId, network.nodes);
        const descendants = getDescendants(nodeId, network.nodes);
        const visibleIds = new Set([nodeId, ...ancestors, ...descendants]);

        console.log('[FocusManager] Focus on:', node.product || nodeId,
            '| ancestors:', ancestors.size, '| descendants:', descendants.size,
            '| total visible:', visibleIds.size);

        // Update badge
        updateBadge(node.product || nodeId, true);

        // Re-render with focus filter
        if (window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
            window.NetworkRenderer.render(network, true, visibleIds);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Clear focus mode
     */
    function clear() {
        focusNodeId = null;
        updateBadge('', false);
        const network = window.currentNetwork;
        if (network && window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
            window.NetworkRenderer.render(network, true);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Returns whether focus mode is currently active
     */
    function isActive() {
        return focusNodeId !== null;
    }

    /**
     * Returns current focus node ID
     */
    function getFocusNodeId() {
        return focusNodeId;
    }

    /**
     * Update badge and button state in controls bar
     */
    function updateBadge(nodeName, active) {
        const badge = document.getElementById('focus-mode-badge');
        const nameLabel = document.getElementById('focus-node-name-label');
        if (badge) badge.classList.toggle('active', active);
        if (nameLabel) nameLabel.textContent = nodeName || 'â€”';
    }

    /**
     * Initialize UI event listeners
     */
    function init() {
        // Focus button click
        const focusBtn = document.getElementById('focus-node-btn');
        if (focusBtn) {
            focusBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                const selectedId = window.DDOptim?.selectedNodeId;
                if (selectedId) activate(selectedId);
            });
        }

        // Clear badge button
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('focus-clear-btn');
        if (clearBtn) {
            clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
                clear();
            });
        }

        console.log('[FocusManager] âœ“ Initialized');
    }

    // Expose API
    window.FocusManager = { activate, clear, isActive, getFocusNodeId, init };

    // Auto-init when DOM ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        setTimeout(init, 100);
    }

    return { activate, clear, isActive, getFocusNodeId };
})();

console.log('âœ“ FocusManager ready');
