    // === BUFFER POSITIONING ===
    html += '<div class="detail-section editable-section">';
    html += '<div class="detail-section-title">Buffer Positioning âœï¸</div>';
    
    // Buffer Lock Checkbox
    const bufferLocked = node.bufferLocked || false;
    html += '<div class="detail-field">';
    html += '<label class="checkbox-label">';
    html += `<input type="checkbox" id="edit-buffer-locked" ${bufferLocked ? 'checked' : ''}>`;
    html += ' ðŸ”’ Lock buffer decision (prevent auto-positioning changes)';
    html += '</label>';
    html += '<div class="detail-help">âš¡ When locked, Auto-Position algorithm will preserve this buffer decision</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    
    // Has Buffer Checkbox
    const hasBuffer = node.hasBuffer || false;
    html += '<div class="detail-field">';
    html += '<label class="checkbox-label">';
    html += `<input type="checkbox" id="edit-has-buffer" ${hasBuffer ? 'checked' : ''}>`;
    html += ' Add Strategic Buffer';
    html += '</label>';
    html += '</div>';
    
    // Buffer Rationale
    html += '<div class="detail-field">';
    html += '<label class="detail-label" for="edit-buffer-rationale">Rationale</label>';
    html += `<textarea id="edit-buffer-rationale" class="detail-textarea" rows="3" placeholder="Optional notes">${node.bufferRationale || ''}</textarea>`;
    html += '</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    
    // === CALCULATED VALUES (Read-Only) ===
    html += '<div class="detail-section">';
    html += '<div class="detail-section-title">Calculated Values ðŸ”’</div>';
    
    if (node.calculatedADU !== undefined) {
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Calculated ADU</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value calculated">ðŸ”’ ${node.calculatedADU.toFixed(1)} units/day</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
    }
    
    if (node.clt !== undefined) {
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Cumulative Lead Time (CLT)</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value calculated">ðŸ”’ ${node.clt} days</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
    }
    
    if (node.dlt !== undefined) {
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Decoupled Lead Time (DLT)</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value calculated">ðŸ”’ ${node.dlt} days</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Show buffer impact if CLT is available
      if (node.clt !== undefined && node.clt !== node.dlt) {
        const bufferImpact = node.clt - node.dlt;
        html += '<div class="detail-field">';
        html += '<div class="detail-label">Buffer Impact</div>';
        html += `<div class="detail-value calculated success">ðŸ”’ -${bufferImpact} days (${Math.round(bufferImpact/node.clt*100)}% reduction)</div>`;
        html += '</div>';
      }
      
      // Show LT Exceeding Tolerance if node has customer tolerance
      if (node.independentADU > 0 && node.customerTolerance && node.ltExceeding !== undefined) {
        if (node.ltExceeding > 0) {
          // Show in green bold when there is margin
          html += '<div class="detail-field">';
          html += '<div class="detail-label">LT Exceeding Tolerance</div>';
          html += `<div class="detail-value calculated" style="color: #10b981; font-weight: 600;">ðŸ”’ ${node.ltExceeding.toFixed(1)} days margin</div>`;
          html += '</div>';
        }
      }
    }
    html += '</div>';
    
    // === BUFFER SIZING (if buffered) ===
    if (node.hasBuffer && node.bufferSizing) {
      html += '<div class="detail-section buffer-sizing-section">';
      html += '<div class="detail-section-title">ðŸ“Š DDMRP Buffer Sizing</div>';
      
      const sizing = node.bufferSizing;
      
      // Graphical buffer visualization
      html += '<div class="buffer-visual">';
      
      // Calculate percentages for visual display
      const total = sizing.topOfGreen;
      const redPct = (sizing.red / total * 100).toFixed(1);
      const yellowPct = (sizing.yellow / total * 100).toFixed(1);
      const greenPct = (sizing.green / total * 100).toFixed(1);
      
      html += '<div class="buffer-bar">';
      html += `<div class="buffer-zone red" style="height: ${redPct}%" title="Red Zone: ${sizing.red} units">`;
      html += `<span class="zone-label">Red<br>${sizing.red}</span>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += `<div class="buffer-zone yellow" style="height: ${yellowPct}%" title="Yellow Zone: ${sizing.yellow} units">`;
      html += `<span class="zone-label">Yellow<br>${sizing.yellow}</span>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += `<div class="buffer-zone green" style="height: ${greenPct}%" title="Green Zone: ${sizing.green} units">`;
      html += `<span class="zone-label">Green<br>${sizing.green}</span>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Average stock marker
      const avgStockPct = (sizing.averageStock / total * 100).toFixed(1);
      html += `<div class="avg-stock-marker" style="bottom: ${avgStockPct}%" title="Average Stock: ${sizing.averageStock} units">`;
      html += '<span class="marker-label">â† Avg Stock</span>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Buffer zone details
      html += '<div class="buffer-zones-grid">';
      
      // Red Zone
      html += '<div class="zone-detail red-zone">';
      html += '<div class="zone-header">ðŸ”´ Red Zone (Safety Stock)</div>';
      html += `<div class="zone-value">${sizing.red} units</div>`;
      html += '<div class="zone-breakdown">';
      html += `Base: ${sizing.redBase} units<br>`;
      html += `Security: ${sizing.redSecurity} units (Ã—${sizing.variabilityFactor})`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Yellow Zone
      html += '<div class="zone-detail yellow-zone">';
      html += '<div class="zone-header">ðŸŸ¡ Yellow Zone (Lead Time Demand)</div>';
      html += `<div class="zone-value">${sizing.yellow} units</div>`;
      html += '<div class="zone-breakdown">';
      html += `ADU Ã— DLT = ${node.calculatedADU.toFixed(1)} Ã— ${node.dlt}`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Green Zone
      html += '<div class="zone-detail green-zone">';
      html += '<div class="zone-header">ðŸŸ¢ Green Zone (Order Size)</div>';
      html += `<div class="zone-value">${sizing.green} units</div>`;
      html += '<div class="zone-breakdown">';
      html += `MAX(<br>`;
      html += `  Delay: ${sizing.greenDelay} (Ã—${sizing.leadTimeFactor})<br>`;
      html += `  MOQ: ${sizing.greenMOQ}<br>`;
      html += `  Cycle: ${sizing.greenCycle}<br>`;
      html += `)`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      html += '</div>';
      
      // Buffer levels summary
      html += '<div class="buffer-levels">';
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Top of Red</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value">${sizing.topOfRed} units</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Top of Yellow</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value">${sizing.topOfYellow} units</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<div class="detail-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label">Top of Green</div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value">${sizing.topOfGreen} units</div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<div class="detail-field highlight-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label"><strong>Average Stock</strong></div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value"><strong>${sizing.averageStock} units</strong></div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '<div class="detail-field highlight-field">';
      html += '<div class="detail-label"><strong>Inventory Value</strong></div>';
      html += `<div class="detail-value"><strong>â‚¬${sizing.inventoryValue.toFixed(2)}</strong></div>`;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      
      html += '</div>';
    } else if (node.hasBuffer && !node.bufferSizing) {
      // Buffer is enabled but sizing not calculated
      html += '<div class="detail-section">';
      html += '<div class="detail-section-title">âš ï¸ Buffer Sizing Not Calculated</div>';
      html += '<div class="detail-help">Buffer sizing calculation failed. Check that ADU, DLT, and buffer profile are valid.</div>';
      html += '</div>';
    }
    
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Setting panel content HTML...');
    content.innerHTML = html;
    
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Attaching event listeners...');
    attachEventListeners();
    
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Adding .open class to panel...');
    panel.classList.add('open');
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Panel classes:', panel.className);
    
    console.log('âœ“ Detail panel opened successfully');
  }
  
  // Attach event listeners to editable controls
  function attachEventListeners() {
    const nodeId = UIHandlers.getCurrentNodeId();
    
    // Independent ADU
    const aduInput = document.getElementById('edit-independent-adu');
    if (aduInput) {
      aduInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BasicParams.handleIndependentADUChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Customer Lead Time
    const customerToleranceInput = document.getElementById('edit-customer-lead-time');
    if (customerToleranceInput) {
      customerToleranceInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_CustomerLeadTime.handleCustomerLeadTimeChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Lead Time
    const leadTimeInput = document.getElementById('edit-lead-time');
    if (leadTimeInput) {
      leadTimeInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BasicParams.handleLeadTimeChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Buffer Profile
    const profileSelect = document.getElementById('edit-buffer-profile');
    if (profileSelect) {
      profileSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BasicParams.handleBufferProfileChange(nodeId, e.target.value);
      });
    }
    
    // MOQ
    const moqInput = document.getElementById('edit-moq');
    if (moqInput) {
      moqInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_Constraints.handleMOQChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Order Cycle
    const orderCycleInput = document.getElementById('edit-order-cycle');
    if (orderCycleInput) {
      orderCycleInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_Constraints.handleOrderCycleChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Unit Cost
    const unitCostInput = document.getElementById('edit-unit-cost');
    if (unitCostInput) {
      unitCostInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_Constraints.handleUnitCostChange(nodeId, parseFloat(e.target.value));
      });
    }
    
    // Buffer Locked
    const bufferLockedCheckbox = document.getElementById('edit-buffer-locked');
    if (bufferLockedCheckbox) {
      bufferLockedCheckbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.handleBufferLockedChange(nodeId, e.target.checked);
      });
    }
    
    // Has Buffer
    const hasBufferCheckbox = document.getElementById('edit-has-buffer');
    if (hasBufferCheckbox) {
      hasBufferCheckbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.handleHasBufferToggle(nodeId, e.target.checked);
      });
    }
    
    // Tolerance Mandatory
    const toleranceMandatoryCheckbox = document.getElementById('edit-tolerance-mandatory');
    if (toleranceMandatoryCheckbox) {
      toleranceMandatoryCheckbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_CustomerLeadTime.handleToleranceMandatoryChange(nodeId, e.target.checked);
      });
    }

    // Buffer Rationale
    const rationaleTextarea = document.getElementById('edit-buffer-rationale');
    if (rationaleTextarea) {
      rationaleTextarea.addEventListener('blur', (e) => {
        window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.handleBufferRationaleChange(nodeId, e.target.value);
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Extend UIHandlers with showNodeDetails
  UIHandlers.showNodeDetails = showNodeDetails;
  console.log('âœ“ showNodeDetails registered on UIHandlers');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300910 | Basic Parameter Handlers ===
// UI Event Handlers - Basic Parameter Handlers
window.UIHandlers_BasicParams = (function() {
  
  // Helper: Refresh table view if active
  function refreshTableIfActive() {
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabularView && window.DDOptim.tabularView.active) {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim.renderTable === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ”„ Refreshing table view...');
        window.DDOptim.renderTable();
      }
    }
  }
  
  function handleIndependentADUChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    if (newValue < 0) {
      alert('Independent ADU cannot be negative');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    const oldValue = node.independentADU;
    node.independentADU = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} independentADU: ${oldValue} â†’ ${newValue}`);
    
    // Step 1: Propagate ADU through entire network
    if (window.ADUPropagation && window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Propagating ADU through network...');
      window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU(window.currentNetwork.nodes, 1.0);
    }
    
    // Step 2: Recalculate buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (ADU impacts Yellow zone)
    if (window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions && window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (ADU changed)...');
      window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing();
    }
    
    // Step 3: Recalculate delivery lead time for this node (independentADU affects whether it's calculated)
    if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator && window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.recalculateNode) {
      window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.recalculateNode(node);
    }
    
    // Step 4: Update visualization - refresh ADU badge display (preserves view)
    if (window.NetworkRenderer && window.NetworkRenderer.updateNodeAppearance) {
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Updating node appearance with new ADU badge...');
      window.NetworkRenderer.updateNodeAppearance(nodeId);
      console.log('âœ“ Node visualization updated');
    }
    
    // Step 5: Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Step 6: Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleLeadTimeChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    if (newValue <= 0) {
      alert('Lead time must be greater than 0');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    if (newValue > 365) {
      if (!confirm('Lead time exceeds 1 year. Are you sure?')) {
        UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
        return;
      }
    }
    
    const oldLeadTime = node.leadTime;
    node.leadTime = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} leadTime: ${oldLeadTime} â†’ ${newValue}`);
    
    // Step 1: Recalculate CLT and DLT for entire network
    if (window.CLTCalculator && window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating CLT...');
      window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
    }
    if (window.DLTCalculator && window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating DLT...');
      window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
    }
    
    // Step 2: Recalculate buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (DLT impacts Yellow zone)
    if (window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions && window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (Lead time changed)...');
      window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing();
    }
    
    // Step 3: Recalculate delivery lead time for all nodes (DLT changed affects deliveryLeadTime)
    if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator && window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime) {
      window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
    }
    
    // Step 4: Update visualization - re-render entire network with preserved view
    if (window.NetworkRenderer && window.NetworkRenderer.render) {
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Re-rendering network with updated lead times (preserving view)...');
      window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork, true); // preserveView = true
      console.log('âœ“ Network visualization updated');
    }
    
    // Step 5: Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Step 6: Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleBufferProfileChange(nodeId, newProfile) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    const currentNetwork = window.currentNetwork;
    const profileExists = currentNetwork.profiles && 
      (currentNetwork.profiles.get ? currentNetwork.profiles.has(newProfile) : currentNetwork.profiles[newProfile]);
    
    if (!profileExists) {
      alert(`Profile '${newProfile}' not found`);
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    const oldProfile = node.bufferProfile;
    node.bufferProfile = newProfile;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} bufferProfile: ${oldProfile} â†’ ${newProfile}`);
    
    // Step 1: Recalculate buffer sizing for all buffered nodes
    // (Profile change affects variability factor and lead time factor)
    if (window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions && window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (Buffer profile changed)...');
      window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions.recalculateAllBufferSizing();
    }
    
    // Step 2: Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Step 3: Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  return {
    handleIndependentADUChange,
    handleLeadTimeChange,
    handleBufferProfileChange
  };
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300915 | Constraint Handlers ===
// UI Event Handlers - Constraint Handlers
window.UIHandlers_Constraints = (function() {
  
  // Helper: Refresh table view if active
  function refreshTableIfActive() {
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabularView && window.DDOptim.tabularView.active) {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim.renderTable === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ”„ Refreshing table view...');
        window.DDOptim.renderTable();
      }
    }
  }
  
  function handleMOQChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    if (newValue < 0) {
      alert('MOQ cannot be negative');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    node.moq = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} moq to ${newValue}`);
    
    // Recalculate buffer sizing if buffered (affects green zone)
    if (node.hasBuffer && typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
      window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
      console.log('âœ“ Buffer sizing recalculated');
    }
    
    // Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleOrderCycleChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    if (newValue < 0) {
      alert('Order cycle cannot be negative');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    node.orderCycle = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} orderCycle to ${newValue}`);
    
    // Recalculate buffer sizing if buffered (affects green zone)
    if (node.hasBuffer && typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
      window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
      console.log('âœ“ Buffer sizing recalculated');
    }
    
    // Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleUnitCostChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    if (newValue < 0) {
      alert('Unit cost cannot be negative');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    node.unitCost = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} unitCost to â‚¬${newValue.toFixed(2)}`);
    
    // Recalculate inventory value if buffered
    if (node.hasBuffer && typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
      window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
      console.log('âœ“ Buffer sizing recalculated');
    }
    
    // Update metrics display (total inventory value)
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  return {
    handleMOQChange,
    handleOrderCycleChange,
    handleUnitCostChange
  };
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300920 | Buffer Decision Handlers ===
// UI Event Handlers - Buffer Decision Handlers
window.UIHandlers_BufferDecisions = (function() {
  
  // Helper: Refresh table view if active
  function refreshTableIfActive() {
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabularView && window.DDOptim.tabularView.active) {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim.renderTable === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ”„ Refreshing table view...');
        window.DDOptim.renderTable();
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Helper: Recalculate buffer sizing for all buffered nodes
  function recalculateAllBufferSizing() {
    if (!window.currentNetwork || !window.currentNetwork.nodes) return;
    
    if (typeof window.calculateBufferSizing !== 'function') {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ calculateBufferSizing function not available');
      return;
    }
    
    let recalculatedCount = 0;
    window.currentNetwork.nodes.forEach(node => {
      if (node.hasBuffer) {
        window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
        recalculatedCount++;
      }
    });
    
    console.log(`âœ“ Recalculated buffer sizing for ${recalculatedCount} buffered nodes`);
    return recalculatedCount;
  }
  
  function handleHasBufferToggle(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    const oldValue = node.hasBuffer;
    node.hasBuffer = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} hasBuffer: ${oldValue} â†’ ${newValue}`);
    
    // Step 1: Recalculate DLT for entire network (buffer status affects parent DLT calculations)
    if (window.DLTCalculator && window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating DLT (buffer status changed)...');
      window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
    }
    
    // Step 2: Recalculate buffer sizing for ALL buffered nodes
    // This is CRITICAL because:
    // - Parent nodes' DLT may have changed (impacts their Yellow zone)
    // - Parent nodes' buffer sizing depends on their children's DLT
    // - Example: Buffering "Rayons" reduces "Roue" DLT, which changes "Roue" buffer sizing
    console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating buffer sizing for all buffered nodes (DLT changed)...');
    const count = recalculateAllBufferSizing();
    
    if (count > 0) {
      console.log(`âœ“ Buffer sizing updated for ${count} nodes due to DLT cascade`);
    }
    
    // Step 3: Recalculate delivery lead time for all nodes (buffer status affects deliveryLeadTime)
    if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator && window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime) {
      window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
    }
    
    // Step 4: Re-render network to update node visuals and badges
    if (window.NetworkRenderer && window.NetworkRenderer.render) {
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Re-rendering network (preserving view)...');
      window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork, true); // preserveView = true
      console.log('âœ“ Network updated with new buffer status');
    }
    
    // Step 5: Update metrics display (total inventory value may have changed)
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    // Step 6: Refresh table view if active
    refreshTableIfActive();
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleBufferRationaleChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    node.bufferRationale = newValue.trim();
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} bufferRationale`);
    
    // Rationale doesn't affect calculations, but update table if visible
    refreshTableIfActive();
  }
  
  function handleBufferLockedChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    node.bufferLocked = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} bufferLocked: ${newValue}`);
    
    // No recalculations needed - this just sets a flag for RLT algorithm
    // Update table to show lock icon change
    refreshTableIfActive();
  }
  
  return {
    handleHasBufferToggle,
    handleBufferRationaleChange,
    handleBufferLockedChange,
    recalculateAllBufferSizing, // Expose for use by other handlers if needed
    refreshTableIfActive // Expose for use by other handlers
  };
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300925 | Customer Lead Time Handler ===
// UI Event Handlers - Customer Lead Time Handler
window.UIHandlers_CustomerLeadTime = (function() {
  
  function handleCustomerLeadTimeChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;
    
    // Validation: Only allow editing if node has independentADU > 0
    if (!node.independentADU || node.independentADU <= 0) {
      alert('Customer Lead Time can only be set for nodes with independent ADU (customer-facing products)');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    if (newValue < 0) {
      alert('Customer Lead Time cannot be negative');
      UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
      return;
    }
    
    if (newValue > 365) {
      if (!confirm('Customer Lead Time exceeds 1 year. Are you sure?')) {
        UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
        return;
      }
    }
    
    const oldValue = node.customerTolerance;
    node.customerTolerance = newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} customerTolerance: ${oldValue} â†’ ${newValue}`);
    
    // Step 1: Recalculate delivery lead time for this node
    // (customerTolerance affects deliveryLeadTime and missingCustomerLeadTime)
    if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator && window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.recalculateNode) {
      window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.recalculateNode(node);
    }
    
    // Step 2: Update visualization - refresh alert badge display
    if (window.NetworkRenderer && window.NetworkRenderer.updateNodeAppearance) {
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Updating node appearance with new alert badge...');
      window.NetworkRenderer.updateNodeAppearance(nodeId);
      console.log('âœ“ Node visualization updated');
    }
    
    // Step 3: Update metrics display
    if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
      window.updateMetricsDisplay();
    }
    
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }
  
  function handleToleranceMandatoryChange(nodeId, newValue) {
    const node = UIHandlers.getNode(nodeId);
    if (!node) return;

    // Only meaningful on customer-facing nodes
    if (!node.independentADU || node.independentADU <= 0) return;

    node.toleranceMandatory = !!newValue;
    console.log(`Updated ${node.product || node.id} toleranceMandatory: ${node.toleranceMandatory}`);

    // No recalculation needed â€” toleranceMandatory is an OPT constraint only
    UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
  }

  return {
    handleCustomerLeadTimeChange,
    handleToleranceMandatoryChange
  };
})();


=== BLOCK: 0300930 | Auto-Position UI Handlers ===
// ============================================================================
// SCRIPT 930: Auto-Position UI Handlers
// ============================================================================
// PURPOSE: UI controls for Required Lead Time auto-positioning workflow.
// WORKFLOW (mirrors OPT):
//   1. Run algorithm â†’ modifies network in-place for preview
//   2. Show modal with proposed changes
//   3. Save as new scenario â†’ diff engine captures mods, scenario created
//   4. Discard â†’ restore network to pre-algorithm state
// ============================================================================

(function() {
  'use strict';

  console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Loading...');

  // Snapshot taken BEFORE algorithm runs â€” used to restore on discard
  let _preAlgoSnapshot = null;
  // Result returned by the RLT engine
  let _pendingResult = null;

  // =========================================================================
  // MODULE: AutoPositionUI
  // =========================================================================

  window.AutoPositionUI = {

    // =======================================================================
    // BUTTON CLICK: Run Algorithm and Show Preview
    // =======================================================================

    runAlgorithm: function() {
      console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Running algorithm...');

      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (!network || !network.nodes) {
        alert('Please load a model first.');
        return;
      }

      try {
        // â”€â”€ 1. Snapshot full buffer state before touching anything â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        _preAlgoSnapshot = new Map();
        for (const [id, node] of network.nodes) {
          _preAlgoSnapshot.set(id, {
            hasBuffer:      node.hasBuffer,
            bufferLocked:   node.bufferLocked,
            bufferRationale: node.bufferRationale || ''
          });
        }
        console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Pre-algo snapshot captured (' + _preAlgoSnapshot.size + ' nodes)');

        // â”€â”€ 2. Run algorithm (modifies network in-place for preview) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        const result = window.RequiredLeadTimePropagation.autoPositionBuffers({
          respectLockedBuffers: true,
          logIterations: true
        });

        // â”€â”€ 3. Tag new buffers with automatic rationale â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        for (const bufferedNode of result.buffersSet) {
          const node = network.nodes.get(bufferedNode.nodeId);
          if (node && !node.bufferLocked) {
            node.bufferRationale = 'For customer tolerance (automatic)';
          }
        }

        _pendingResult = result;

        // â”€â”€ 4. Recalculate so the network preview is accurate â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        this._recalcAll();

        // â”€â”€ 5. Pre-fill scenario name â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        const nameInput = document.getElementById('rlt-scenario-name');
        if (nameInput) {
          nameInput.value = 'RLT Auto-Position';
        }

        // â”€â”€ 6. Render preview and open modal â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        this._renderPreview(result);
        this._openModal();

      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[Auto-Position UI] Error:', err);
        // Restore network if error occurred mid-run
        if (_preAlgoSnapshot) this._restoreSnapshot();
        alert('Error running algorithm: ' + err.message);
      }
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // SAVE AS SCENARIO
    // =======================================================================

    saveAsScenario: function() {
      const nameInput = document.getElementById('rlt-scenario-name');
      const name = nameInput ? nameInput.value.trim() : '';

      if (!name) {
        alert('Please enter a scenario name.');
        return;
      }

      // Check for duplicate name
      const scenarios = (window.currentNetwork && window.currentNetwork.scenarios) || [];
      if (scenarios.some(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
        alert('A scenario with this name already exists. Please choose a different name.');
        return;
      }

      // Network is already in the post-RLT state â€” use diff engine to capture mods
      const diffEngine = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.diffEngine;
      if (!diffEngine || typeof diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState !== 'function') {
        alert('Scenario system not available.');
        return;
      }

      const modifications = diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState();

      // Calculate metrics
      const scenarioUI = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarioUI;
      const metrics = scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.calculateCurrentMetrics === 'function'
        ? scenarioUI.calculateCurrentMetrics()
        : {};

      const newScenario = {
        id: 'scenario_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),
        name: name,
        description: 'RLT Auto-Position result created on ' + new Date().toLocaleString(),
        isBaseline: false,
        budgetMax: null,
        modifications: modifications,
        metrics: metrics,
        created: new Date().toISOString(),
        lastModified: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      if (!window.currentNetwork.scenarios) window.currentNetwork.scenarios = [];
      window.currentNetwork.scenarios.push(newScenario);

      // Sync dropdown and switch to new scenario
      if (scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.syncScenarios === 'function') scenarioUI.syncScenarios();
      if (scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown === 'function') scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown();

      window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
      window.DDOptim.scenarios = window.DDOptim.scenarios || {};
      window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;
      window.currentNetwork.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;

      const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
      if (dropdown) dropdown.value = newScenario.id;

      this._closeModal();
      _pendingResult    = null;
      _preAlgoSnapshot  = null;

      console.log('[Auto-Position UI] âœ“ Scenario saved:', name);

      // Refresh all active views
      if (window.NetworkRenderer && typeof window.NetworkRenderer.render === 'function') {
        window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
      }
      if (window.DDOptim.metricsUpdater && typeof window.DDOptim.metricsUpdater.update === 'function') {
        window.DDOptim.metricsUpdater.update();
      }
      const activeView = document.querySelector('.view-container.active');
      if (activeView) {
        if (activeView.id === 'table-view' && window.TableRenderer && typeof window.TableRenderer.render === 'function') {
          window.TableRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
        } else if (activeView.id === 'scenarios-view' && window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer && typeof window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer.render === 'function') {
          window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer.render();
        }
      }
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // DISCARD: Restore network to pre-algorithm state
    // =======================================================================

    discardResults: function() {
      if (_pendingResult) {
        console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Discarding â€” restoring pre-algo state...');
        this._restoreSnapshot();
        this._recalcAll();
        console.log('[Auto-Position UI] âœ“ Network restored');
      }
      this._closeModal();
      _pendingResult   = null;
      _preAlgoSnapshot = null;
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // PRIVATE: Restore snapshot
    // =======================================================================

    _restoreSnapshot: function() {
      if (!_preAlgoSnapshot) return;
      const nodes = window.currentNetwork.nodes;
      for (const [id, saved] of _preAlgoSnapshot) {
        const node = nodes.get(id);
        if (node) {
          node.hasBuffer       = saved.hasBuffer;
          node.bufferLocked    = saved.bufferLocked;
          node.bufferRationale = saved.bufferRationale;
        }
      }
      console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Snapshot restored (' + _preAlgoSnapshot.size + ' nodes)');
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // PRIVATE: Recalculate full pipeline
    // =======================================================================

    _recalcAll: function() {
      const nodes = window.currentNetwork.nodes;

      window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(nodes);

      if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator && window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime) {
        window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime(nodes);
      }

      if (typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
        for (const [id, node] of nodes) {
          if (node.hasBuffer) window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
        }
      }

      if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
        window.updateMetricsDisplay();
      }

      if (window.NetworkRenderer && window.NetworkRenderer.render) {
        window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
      }
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // PRIVATE: Render preview content
    // =======================================================================

    _renderPreview: function(result) {
      // Summary counts
      document.getElementById('buffers-add-count').textContent    = result.changes.added.length;
      document.getElementById('buffers-remove-count').textContent = result.changes.removed.length;
      document.getElementById('buffers-locked-count').textContent = result.changes.unchanged.filter(b => b.locked).length;

      // Changes list
      const changesList = document.getElementById('buffer-changes-list');
      changesList.innerHTML = '';

      if (result.changes.added.length === 0 && result.changes.removed.length === 0) {
        changesList.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--b2w-text-muted); font-style: italic;">No changes proposed. All buffers are optimally positioned.</p>';
      } else {
        result.changes.added.forEach(function(buffer) {
          const bufferInfo = result.buffersSet.find(b => b.nodeId === buffer.nodeId);
          const reasonText = bufferInfo
            ? (bufferInfo.reason === 'mandatory'
                ? 'Mandatory (remaining time: ' + bufferInfo.remainingTime.toFixed(2) + ' days)'
                : 'Strategic positioning')
            : 'Auto-positioned';
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.style.cssText = 'padding: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--b2w-success); background: var(--b2w-surface); border-radius: 4px;';
          item.innerHTML = '<strong style="color: var(--b2w-success);">\u2795 Add Buffer:</strong> ' + buffer.nodeName + ' (' + buffer.id + ')<br>' +
            '<small style="color: var(--b2w-text-muted);">Reason: ' + reasonText + '</small>';
          changesList.appendChild(item);
        });

        result.changes.removed.forEach(function(buffer) {
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.style.cssText = 'padding: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--b2w-error); background: var(--b2w-surface); border-radius: 4px;';
          item.innerHTML = '<strong style="color: var(--b2w-error);">\u2796 Remove Buffer:</strong> ' + buffer.nodeName + ' (' + buffer.id + ')<br>' +
            '<small style="color: var(--b2w-text-muted);">No longer needed for customer requirements</small>';
          changesList.appendChild(item);
        });

        const lockedBuffers = result.changes.unchanged.filter(b => b.locked);
        if (lockedBuffers.length > 0) {
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.style.cssText = 'padding: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--b2w-warning); background: var(--b2w-surface); border-radius: 4px;';
          item.innerHTML = '<strong style="color: var(--b2w-warning);">\ud83d\udd12 Locked Buffers (Preserved):</strong><br>' +
            '<small style="color: var(--b2w-text-muted);">' + lockedBuffers.map(b => b.nodeName).join(', ') + '</small>';
          changesList.appendChild(item);
        }
      }

      // Unsolvable constraints
      if (result.unsolvableConstraints && result.unsolvableConstraints.length > 0) {
        const warningItem = document.createElement('div');
        warningItem.style.cssText = 'padding: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--b2w-error); background: var(--b2w-surface); border-radius: 4px;';
        warningItem.innerHTML = '<strong style="color: var(--b2w-error);">\u26a0 Unsolvable Constraints:</strong><br>' +
          '<small style="color: var(--b2w-text-muted);">' +
          result.unsolvableConstraints.map(c =>
            c.nodeName + ': deficit of ' + c.deficit.toFixed(2) + ' days (locked without buffer)'
          ).join('<br>') + '</small>';
        changesList.appendChild(warningItem);
      }

      // Execution log
      const logText = 'Single-pass topological sort completed:\n' +
        '- Buffers set: ' + result.buffersSet.length + '\n' +
        '- Unsolvable constraints: ' + (result.unsolvableConstraints ? result.unsolvableConstraints.length : 0);
      document.getElementById('algorithm-log-text').textContent = logText;

      // Disable save button when no changes
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('btn-save-auto-position');
      if (saveBtn) {
        const hasChanges = result.changes.added.length > 0 || result.changes.removed.length > 0;
        saveBtn.disabled = !hasChanges;
        saveBtn.style.opacity  = hasChanges ? '1' : '0.5';
        saveBtn.style.cursor   = hasChanges ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed';
      }
    },

    // =======================================================================
    // PRIVATE: Modal open / close
    // =======================================================================

    _openModal: function() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('auto-position-modal');
      if (modal) {
        modal.style.display = 'block';
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      }
    },

    _closeModal: function() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('auto-position-modal');
      if (modal) {
        modal.style.display = 'none';
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
      }
    }
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // EVENT LISTENER: Wire Auto-Position button
  // =========================================================================

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('autoPositionBtn');
    if (btn) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        window.AutoPositionUI.runAlgorithm();
      });
      console.log('[Auto-Position UI] Auto-position button wired up');
    } else {
      console.warn('[Auto-Position UI] autoPositionBtn not found');
    }
  });

  console.log('[Auto-Position UI] âœ“ Module loaded');

})();


=== BLOCK: 0300935 | OPT UI Handler ===
/**
 * OPT UI Handler
 * Connects the Optimize button to window.DDOptim.OPT.run()
 * and manages the three OPT modal dialogs.
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';

    console.log('[OPT UI] Loading...');

    // â”€â”€ State â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    let _lastOPTResult = null; // holds result until user saves or discards

    // â”€â”€ Helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function showModal(id)  { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.style.display = 'flex'; }
    function hideModal(id)  { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; }
    function fmtCurrency(v) { return 'â‚¬' + (v || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }); }
    function fmtDays(v)     { return (v || 0).toFixed(1) + ' d'; }

    // â”€â”€ Launch Modal â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function openLaunchModal() {
        const network = window.currentNetwork;
        if (!network || !network.nodes) {
            alert('Please load a model first.');
            return;
        }

        // Pre-fill budget from current scenario if set
        const scenarioState = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarios;
        const currentScenario = scenarioState && scenarioState.list
            ? scenarioState.list.find(s => s.id === scenarioState.currentScenarioId)
            : null;
        const savedBudget = currentScenario ? currentScenario.budgetMax : null;

        const budgetInput   = document.getElementById('opt-budget-input');
        const unlimitedCb   = document.getElementById('opt-budget-unlimited');
        if (savedBudget !== null && savedBudget !== undefined) {
            budgetInput.value = savedBudget;
            unlimitedCb.checked = false;
            budgetInput.disabled = false;
        } else {
            budgetInput.value = '';
            unlimitedCb.checked = true;
            budgetInput.disabled = true;
        }

        showModal('opt-launch-modal');
    }

    function initLaunchModal() {
        const unlimitedCb = document.getElementById('opt-budget-unlimited');
        const budgetInput = document.getElementById('opt-budget-input');

        if (unlimitedCb) {
            unlimitedCb.addEventListener('change', () => {
                budgetInput.disabled = unlimitedCb.checked;
                if (unlimitedCb.checked) budgetInput.value = '';
            });
        }

        document.getElementById('opt-launch-close')  && document.getElementById('opt-launch-close').addEventListener('click',  () => hideModal('opt-launch-modal'));
        document.getElementById('opt-launch-overlay') && document.getElementById('opt-launch-overlay').addEventListener('click', () => hideModal('opt-launch-modal'));
        document.getElementById('opt-launch-cancel') && document.getElementById('opt-launch-cancel').addEventListener('click',  () => hideModal('opt-launch-modal'));
        document.getElementById('opt-launch-run')    && document.getElementById('opt-launch-run').addEventListener('click',    runOPT);
    }

    // â”€â”€ Run OPT â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function runOPT() {
        const unlimitedCb = document.getElementById('opt-budget-unlimited');
        const budgetInput = document.getElementById('opt-budget-input');
        const runBtn      = document.getElementById('opt-launch-run');

        const budgetMax = unlimitedCb && unlimitedCb.checked
            ? null
            : (budgetInput && budgetInput.value !== '' ? parseFloat(budgetInput.value) : null);

        hideModal('opt-launch-modal');

        // Show spinner on Optimize button
        const optimizeBtn = document.getElementById('optimizeBtn');
        if (optimizeBtn) {
            optimizeBtn.disabled = true;
            optimizeBtn.textContent = 'ðŸ” Runningâ€¦';
        }

        // Run async to let UI update first
        setTimeout(() => {
            try {
                const network = window.currentNetwork;
                const result = window.DDOptim.OPT.run({ network, budgetMax });
                _lastOPTResult = result;

                if (optimizeBtn) {
                    optimizeBtn.disabled = false;
                    optimizeBtn.textContent = 'ðŸ”¬ Optimize';
                }

                if (!result.feasible) {
                    showErrorModal(result);
                } else {
                    showResultsModal(result, budgetMax);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('[OPT UI] Error running OPT:', err);
                if (optimizeBtn) {
                    optimizeBtn.disabled = false;
                    optimizeBtn.textContent = 'ðŸ”¬ Optimize';
                }
                alert('OPT algorithm error: ' + err.message);
            }
        }, 50);
    }

    // â”€â”€ Error Modal â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function showErrorModal(result) {
        const tbody   = document.getElementById('opt-error-tbody');
        const sugBox  = document.getElementById('opt-error-suggestions');
        if (!tbody) return;

        const report = result.feasibilityReport;
        const infeasNodes = report && report.structural ? report.structural.infeasibleNodes : [];
        const budgetInfo  = report && report.budget;

        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const suggestions = [];

        infeasNodes.forEach(item => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            const causeTag = `<span class="opt-cause-tag opt-cause-locked">ðŸ”’ Locked buffer</span>`;
            const blockers = item.blockingNodes && item.blockingNodes.length
                ? item.blockingNodes.map(b => b.product || b.nodeId).join(', ')
                : 'â€”';
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td><strong>${item.product}</strong>${item.location ? `<br><small style="color:var(--b2w-text-muted)">${item.location}</small>` : ''}</td>
                <td>${item.minDlt} d</td>
                <td>${item.tolerance} d</td>
                <td>${causeTag}</td>
                <td><small>${blockers}</small></td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
            suggestions.push(`Unlock buffer on <strong>${blockers}</strong> to allow the algorithm to buffer it.`);
        });

        if (budgetInfo && !budgetInfo.feasible) {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td colspan="3"><em>Budget constraint</em></td>
                <td><span class="opt-cause-tag opt-cause-budget">ðŸ’° Budget</span></td>
                <td>Required: ${fmtCurrency(budgetInfo.requiredBudget)}<br>Available: ${fmtCurrency(budgetInfo.availableBudget)}</td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
            suggestions.push(`Increase budget to at least ${fmtCurrency(budgetInfo.requiredBudget)} or remove budget constraint.`);
        }

        if (sugBox) {
            sugBox.innerHTML = `<strong>Suggestions:</strong><ul>${suggestions.map(s => `<li>${s}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`;
        }

        document.getElementById('opt-error-close')     && document.getElementById('opt-error-close').addEventListener('click',     () => hideModal('opt-error-modal'));
        document.getElementById('opt-error-overlay')   && document.getElementById('opt-error-overlay').addEventListener('click',   () => hideModal('opt-error-modal'));
        document.getElementById('opt-error-close-btn') && document.getElementById('opt-error-close-btn').addEventListener('click', () => hideModal('opt-error-modal'));

        showModal('opt-error-modal');
    }

    // â”€â”€ Results Modal â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function showResultsModal(result, budgetMax) {
        const { before, after, changes, log } = result;

        // â€” KPI cards â€”
        const kpisEl = document.getElementById('opt-results-kpis');
        if (kpisEl) {
            const kpis = [
                { label: 'Inventory Value (f2)',       before: fmtCurrency(before.f2), after: fmtCurrency(after.f2), worse: after.f2 > before.f2 },
                { label: 'Coverage gap (f1, days)',    before: fmtDays(before.f1),     after: fmtDays(after.f1),     worse: after.f1 > before.f1 },
                { label: 'Mandatory gap (f0, days)',   before: fmtDays(before.f0),     after: fmtDays(after.f0),     worse: after.f0 > before.f0 }
            ];
            kpisEl.innerHTML = kpis.map(k => `
                <div class="opt-kpi-card">
                    <div class="kpi-label">${k.label}</div>
                    <div class="kpi-values">
                        <span class="kpi-before">${k.before}</span>
                        <span class="kpi-arrow">â†’</span>
                        <span class="kpi-after${k.worse ? ' worse' : ''}">${k.after}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>`).join('');
        }

        // â€” Coverage status â€”
        const covEl = document.getElementById('opt-results-coverage');
        if (covEl) {
            const f0Ok = after.f0 === 0;
            const f1Ok = after.f1 === 0;
            covEl.innerHTML = `
                <p style="font-size:13px; margin:4px 0;">${f0Ok ? 'âœ…' : 'âš ï¸'} Mandatory constraints: ${f0Ok ? 'All satisfied' : 'Gap of ' + fmtDays(after.f0) + ' remaining'}</p>
                <p style="font-size:13px; margin:4px 0;">${f1Ok ? 'âœ…' : 'âš ï¸'} All demand nodes: ${f1Ok ? 'All satisfied' : 'Gap of ' + fmtDays(after.f1) + ' remaining'}</p>`;
        }

        // â€” Change list â€”
        const changesEl = document.getElementById('opt-results-changes');
        if (changesEl && changes) {
            const items = [];
            (changes.added || []).forEach(c => {
                items.push(`<li class="added"><span class="opt-change-tag added">+</span> ${c.product}${c.location ? ' / ' + c.location : ''} <small style="margin-left:auto; color:#15803d;">${fmtCurrency(c.inventoryValue)}</small></li>`);
            });
            (changes.removed || []).forEach(c => {
                items.push(`<li class="removed"><span class="opt-change-tag removed">âˆ’</span> ${c.product}${c.location ? ' / ' + c.location : ''}</li>`);
            });
            (changes.swaps || []).forEach(s => {
                const added = s.added.map(a => a.product).join(' + ');
                items.push(`<li class="swap"><span class="opt-change-tag swap">â‡„</span> ${s.removed.product} â†’ ${added} <small style="margin-left:auto; color:#1d4ed8;">âˆ’${fmtCurrency(s.deltaF2)}</small></li>`);
            });
            changesEl.innerHTML = items.length ? items.join('') : '<li style="color:var(--b2w-text-muted); font-size:13px;">No changes from current state.</li>';
        }

        // â€” Execution log â€”
        const logEl     = document.getElementById('opt-log-content');
        const logToggle = document.getElementById('opt-log-toggle-btn');
        if (logEl && log) {
            logEl.textContent = log.join('\n');
        }
        if (logToggle) {
            logToggle.onclick = () => {
                const visible = logEl.classList.toggle('visible');
                logToggle.textContent = visible ? 'â–¼ Hide execution log' : 'â–¶ Show execution log';
            };
        }

        // â€” Scenario name pre-fill â€”
        const nameInput = document.getElementById('opt-scenario-name');
        if (nameInput) {
            const budget = budgetMax !== null ? ` â€“ Budget ${fmtCurrency(budgetMax)}` : ' â€“ No budget';
            nameInput.value = `OPT${budget}`;
        }

        // â€” Footer & save wiring â€”
        document.getElementById('opt-results-close')   && (document.getElementById('opt-results-close').onclick   = discardResults);
        document.getElementById('opt-results-overlay') && (document.getElementById('opt-results-overlay').onclick = discardResults);
        document.getElementById('opt-results-discard') && (document.getElementById('opt-results-discard').onclick  = discardResults);
        document.getElementById('opt-save-btn')        && (document.getElementById('opt-save-btn').onclick         = saveAsScenario);

        showModal('opt-results-modal');
    }

    // â”€â”€ Save & Apply â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function saveAsScenario() {
        const name = (document.getElementById('opt-scenario-name').value || '').trim();
        if (!name) {
            alert('Please enter a scenario name.');
            return;
        }

        // The network already reflects the OPT result (modified in-place).
        // Use the diff engine to capture changes vs baseline, then create scenario.
        const diffEngine = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.diffEngine;
        if (!diffEngine || typeof diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState !== 'function') {
            alert('Scenario system not available.');
            return;
        }

        const modifications = diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState();

        // Check for duplicate name
        const scenarios = window.currentNetwork && window.currentNetwork.scenarios || [];
        if (scenarios.some(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
            alert('A scenario with this name already exists. Please choose a different name.');
            return;
        }

        // Calculate metrics for the new scenario
        const scenarioUI = window.DDOptim.scenarioUI;
        const metrics = scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.calculateCurrentMetrics === 'function'
            ? scenarioUI.calculateCurrentMetrics()
            : {};

        const budgetMax = _lastOPTResult ? (_lastOPTResult.budgetMax || null) : null;

        const newScenario = {
            id: `scenario_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`,
            name,
            description: `OPT result created on ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
            isBaseline: false,
            budgetMax,
            modifications,
            metrics,
            created: new Date().toISOString(),
            lastModified: new Date().toISOString()
        };

        if (!window.currentNetwork.scenarios) window.currentNetwork.scenarios = [];
        window.currentNetwork.scenarios.push(newScenario);

        // Sync and switch to the new scenario
        if (scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.syncScenarios === 'function') scenarioUI.syncScenarios();
        if (scenarioUI && typeof scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown === 'function') scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown();

        window.DDOptim.scenarios = window.DDOptim.scenarios || {};
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;
        window.currentNetwork.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;

        const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (dropdown) dropdown.value = newScenario.id;

        hideModal('opt-results-modal');
        _lastOPTResult = null;
        console.log('[OPT UI] âœ“ Scenario saved:', name);

        // Refresh all active views
        if (window.NetworkRenderer && typeof window.NetworkRenderer.render === 'function') {
            window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
        }
        if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
            window.updateMetricsDisplay();
        }
        const activeView = document.querySelector('.view-container.active');
        if (activeView) {
            if (activeView.id === 'table-view' && window.TableRenderer && typeof window.TableRenderer.render === 'function') {
                window.TableRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
            } else if (activeView.id === 'scenarios-view' && window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer && typeof window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer.render === 'function') {
                window.DDOptim.scenariosRenderer.render();
            }
        }
    }

    function discardResults() {
        // Restore network to pre-OPT state by reloading the current scenario
        if (_lastOPTResult) {
            const loader = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarioLoader;
            if (loader && typeof loader.loadScenario === 'function' && window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
                loader.loadScenario(window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId);
            }
        }
        hideModal('opt-results-modal');
        _lastOPTResult = null;
        console.log('[OPT UI] Results discarded, network restored.');
    }

    // â”€â”€ Init â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function init() {
        initLaunchModal();

        const btn = document.getElementById('optimizeBtn');
        if (btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', openLaunchModal);
        } else {
            console.warn('[OPT UI] #optimizeBtn not found â€” will bind on button creation');
        }
        console.log('[OPT UI] âœ“ Initialized');
    }

    // Export
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.optUI = { openLaunchModal };

    // Init after DOM ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }

    console.log('[OPT UI] âœ“ Loaded');
})();
