=== BLOCK: 0301090 | Unified Model Loader ===
// Unified Model Loader
// Shared by model selector and JSON file import

window.ModelLoader = {
    /**
     * Load a network model from JSON data.
     * After loading:
     *   1. Builds node Map and parent index
     *   2. Initialises / validates scenarios array
     *   3. Captures baseline snapshot (clean network state)
     *   4. Runs full calculation pipeline (ADU, CLT, DLT, buffer sizing, delivery LT)
     *   5. Populates scenario dropdown
     *   6. If currentScenarioId != 'baseline', applies that scenario
     *   7. Refreshes the currently active view (network, table, or scenarios)
     *
     * @param {Object} modelData - JSON object with metadata and nodes array
     * @param {string} source    - Description of source ("Model Selector", "JSON Import")
     * @returns {Object} Result with success flag and message
     */
    loadModel: function(modelData, source) {
        console.log(`ðŸ“¦ ModelLoader: Loading from ${source}...`);

        // --- Validation ---
        if (!modelData) {
            return { success: false, message: 'No model data provided' };
        }
        if (!modelData.metadata || !modelData.metadata.name) {
            return { success: false, message: 'Invalid model: missing metadata.name' };
        }
        if (!modelData.nodes || !Array.isArray(modelData.nodes)) {
            return { success: false, message: 'Invalid model: nodes must be an array' };
        }

        try {
            // Update model name display
            if (typeof window.updateModelNameDisplay === 'function') {
                window.updateModelNameDisplay(modelData.metadata.name);
                console.log(`ðŸ“ Model name updated to: ${modelData.metadata.name}`);
            }

            // --- Build nodes Map ---
            const nodesMap = new Map();
            modelData.nodes.forEach(node => {
                if (!node.id) {
                    throw new Error('Node missing required field: id');
                }

                const normalizedNode = {
                    id: node.id,
                    product: node.product || node.name || node.id,
                    description: node.description || '',
                    location: node.location || '',
                    type: node.type || 'unknown',

                    independentADU: node.independentADU || 0,
                    calculatedADU: null,
                    leadTime: node.leadTime || 0,
                    customerTolerance: node.customerTolerance || null,

                    hasBuffer: node.hasBuffer || false,
                    bufferLocked: node.bufferLocked || false,
                    bufferRationale: node.bufferRationale || '',
                    toleranceMandatory: node.toleranceMandatory || false,

                    children: (node.children || []).map(child => ({
                        id: child.id,
                        quantity: child.quantity || 1,
                        linkType: child.linkType || 'bom'
                    })),
                    parents: [],

                    bufferProfile: node.bufferProfile || 'I',
                    moq: node.moq || node.MOQ || 0,
                    orderCycle: node.orderCycle || 0,
                    unitCost: node.unitCost || 0,

                    clt: null,
                    dlt: null,
                    redZone: null,
                    yellowZone: null,
                    greenZone: null,
                    averageStock: null
                };

                nodesMap.set(node.id, normalizedNode);
            });

            // Build parent relationships (reverse BOM)
            nodesMap.forEach(node => { node.parents = []; });
            nodesMap.forEach(parent => {
                parent.children.forEach(child => {
                    const childNode = nodesMap.get(child.id);
                    if (childNode) {
                        childNode.parents.push({ id: parent.id, quantity: child.quantity });
                    }
                });
            });

            // --- Scenario initialisation ---
            if (!modelData.scenarios || modelData.scenarios.length === 0) {
                console.log('[ModelLoader] No scenarios defined - creating baseline');
                modelData.scenarios = [{
                    id: 'baseline',
                    name: 'Baseline',
                    description: 'Original network configuration',
                    isBaseline: true,
                    budgetMax: null,
                    modifications: [],
                    created: new Date().toISOString(),
                    lastModified: new Date().toISOString()
                }];
            }

            const hasBaseline = modelData.scenarios.some(s => s.id === 'baseline');
            if (!hasBaseline) {
                console.log('[ModelLoader] Adding missing baseline scenario');
                modelData.scenarios.unshift({
                    id: 'baseline',
                    name: 'Baseline',
                    description: 'Original network configuration',
                    isBaseline: true,
                    budgetMax: null,
                    modifications: [],
                    created: new Date().toISOString(),
                    lastModified: new Date().toISOString()
                });
            }

            // Remember which scenario was active at export time
            const savedScenarioId = modelData.currentScenarioId || 'baseline';

            // Always start at baseline while we set up the network
            modelData.currentScenarioId = 'baseline';

            // --- Build network object ---
            const network = {
                metadata: {
                    name: modelData.metadata.name,
                    description: modelData.metadata.description || '',
                    version: modelData.metadata.version || '1.0',
                    nodeCount: nodesMap.size,
                    loadedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                    source: source
                },
                nodes: nodesMap,
                filterPresets: modelData.filterPresets || [],
                scenarios: modelData.scenarios,
                currentScenarioId: 'baseline'
            };

            window.currentNetwork = network;

            // --- Sync to DDOptim.scenarios ---
            if (!window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim = {};
            if (!window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
                window.DDOptim.scenarios = {
                    list: [],
                    currentId: null,
                    hasUnsavedChanges: false,
                    selectedForComparison: []
                };
            }
            window.DDOptim.scenarios.list = network.scenarios;
            window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId = 'baseline';
            window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId = 'baseline';
            window.DDOptim.scenarios.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
            console.log(`[ModelLoader] âœ… Synced ${network.scenarios.length} scenarios to DDOptim.scenarios.list`);

            // --- Capture BASELINE snapshot (clean state, no scenario applied) ---
            console.log('[ModelLoader] Capturing baseline snapshot...');
            if (window.DDOptim?.baselineManager?.captureBaseline) {
                const ok = window.DDOptim.baselineManager.captureBaseline();
                if (ok) {
                    console.log('[ModelLoader] âœ… Baseline snapshot captured');
                } else {
                    console.warn('[ModelLoader] âš ï¸ Baseline snapshot : Baseline snapshot not captured');
                }
            } else {
                // baselineManager not yet loaded (position 1305 > 1090) - schedule retry
                console.warn('[ModelLoader] Baseline manager not available yet - scheduling retry...');
                setTimeout(function() {
                    if (window.DDOptim?.baselineManager?.captureBaseline) {
                        const ok = window.DDOptim.baselineManager.captureBaseline();
                        console.log(ok
                            ? '[ModelLoader] âœ… Baseline snapshot captured (delayed)'
                            : '[ModelLoader] âŒ Baseline snapshot : Baseline snapshot not captured (delayed)');
                    } else {
                        console.error('[ModelLoader] âŒ Baseline manager still not available - baseline not captured');
                    }
                }, 500);
            }

            // --- Full calculation pipeline on baseline state ---
            console.log('ðŸ“ Recalculating ADU...');
            if (window.ADUPropagation?.propagateADU) {
                window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU(network.nodes, 1.0);
            }

            console.log('ðŸ“ Calculating CLT...');
            if (window.CLTCalculator?.calculateCLT) {
                window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT(network.nodes);
            }

            console.log('ðŸ“ Calculating DLT...');
            if (window.DLTCalculator?.calculateDLT) {
                window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(network.nodes);
            }

            console.log('ðŸ“ Calculating buffer zones...');
            if (typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
                let bufferCount = 0;
                network.nodes.forEach(node => {
                    if (node.hasBuffer) {
                        window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
                        bufferCount++;
                    }
                });
                console.log(`âœ“ Buffer sizing calculated for ${bufferCount} buffered nodes`);
            }

            console.log('ðŸ“ Calculating delivery lead time...');
            if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator?.calculateDeliveryLeadTime) {
                window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime(network.nodes);
            }

            // Update baseline scenario metrics (calculated after full pipeline)
            console.log('[ModelLoader] Calculating baseline metrics...');
            const baselineScenario = network.scenarios.find(s => s.id === 'baseline');
            if (baselineScenario && window.DDOptim?.scenarioUI?.calculateCurrentMetrics) {
                baselineScenario.metrics = window.DDOptim.scenarioUI.calculateCurrentMetrics();
                console.log('[ModelLoader] âœ… Baseline metrics updated:', baselineScenario.metrics);
            } else {
                console.warn('[ModelLoader] Could not update baseline metrics - scenarioUI not ready');
            }

            // Update metrics display
            console.log('ðŸ“ Updating metrics...');
            if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
                window.updateMetricsDisplay();
            }

            // Notify table integration that a new model is available
            if (typeof window.DDOptim?.onModelLoaded === 'function') {
                window.DDOptim.onModelLoaded();
            }

            // --- Render network or defer if container is hidden ---
            console.log('ðŸ“ Rendering network...');
            if (!window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
                throw new Error('NetworkRenderer not available');
            }

            const activeView = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView || 'network';

            const renderNetworkAndRefreshView = function() {
                const networkContainer = document.getElementById('network-container');
                const containerVisible = networkContainer &&
                    networkContainer.style.display !== 'none' &&
                    networkContainer.offsetParent !== null;

                if (containerVisible) {
                    window.NetworkRenderer.render(network);
                    if (window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim._networkNeedsRender = false;
                } else {
                    console.log('[ModelLoader] Network container hidden - deferring render');
                    if (window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim._networkNeedsRender = true;
                }

                // Refresh the active non-network view
                const view = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView || 'network';
                if (view === 'table' && typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') {
                    console.log('[ModelLoader] Refreshing table view...');
                    window.DDOptim.renderTable();
                } else if (view === 'scenarios' && typeof window.DDOptim?.renderScenariosView === 'function') {
                    console.log('[ModelLoader] Refreshing scenarios view...');
                    window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
                }
            };

            if (typeof d3 === 'undefined') {
                console.warn('[ModelLoader] D3 not ready yet, scheduling render retry...');
                setTimeout(function() {
                    if (typeof d3 !== 'undefined' && window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
                        console.log('[ModelLoader] D3 now available, rendering...');
                        renderNetworkAndRefreshView();
                    } else {
                        console.error('[ModelLoader] D3 still not available after retry - render failed');
                    }
                }, 300);
            } else {
                renderNetworkAndRefreshView();
            }

            // --- Populate scenario dropdown ---
            if (window.DDOptim?.scenarioUI?.populateScenarioDropdown) {
                window.DDOptim.scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown();
                console.log('[ModelLoader] âœ… Scenario dropdown populated');
            } else {
                console.warn('[ModelLoader] scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown not available');
            }

            // --- Apply saved scenario if it differs from baseline ---
            if (savedScenarioId && savedScenarioId !== 'baseline') {
                console.log(`[ModelLoader] Restoring saved scenario: ${savedScenarioId}`);

                const savedScenario = network.scenarios.find(s => s.id === savedScenarioId);
                if (savedScenario) {
                    if (window.DDOptim?.scenarioLoader?.loadScenario) {
                        const ok = window.DDOptim.scenarioLoader.loadScenario(savedScenarioId);
                        if (ok) {
                            console.log(`[ModelLoader] âœ… Restored scenario: ${savedScenario.name}`);
                        } else {
                            console.warn(`[ModelLoader] âš ï¸ Failed to restore scenario ${savedScenarioId}, staying on baseline`);
                        }
                    } else {
                        console.warn('[ModelLoader] scenarioLoader not ready, scheduling scenario restore...');
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (window.DDOptim?.scenarioLoader?.loadScenario) {
                                window.DDOptim.scenarioLoader.loadScenario(savedScenarioId);
                                console.log(`[ModelLoader] âœ… Delayed restore of scenario: ${savedScenarioId}`);
                            } else {
                                console.warn('[ModelLoader] scenarioLoader still not available - scenario not restored');
                            }
                        }, 800);
                    }
                } else {
                    console.warn(`[ModelLoader] Saved scenario "${savedScenarioId}" not found in scenarios list - staying on baseline`);
                }
            } else {
                console.log('[ModelLoader] Saved scenario is baseline - no restore needed');
            }

            console.log(`âœ“ Model loaded successfully: ${network.metadata.name} (${network.nodes.size} nodes)`);
            console.log(`âœ“ Scenarios: ${network.scenarios.length} total, active: ${savedScenarioId}`);

            return {
                success: true,
                message: `âœ“ Loaded: ${network.metadata.name} (${network.nodes.size} nodes)`,
                network: network
            };

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('âŒ Error loading model:', error);
            console.error('Stack:', error.stack);
            return { success: false, message: `Error: ${error.message}` };
        }
    },

    /**
     * Load a model from the MODEL_LIBRARY by key
     * @param {string} modelKey - Key from MODEL_LIBRARY (e.g., 'simple_chain')
     * @returns {Object} Result from loadModel()
     */
    loadFromLibrary: function(modelKey) {
        if (!window.MODEL_LIBRARY) {
            return { success: false, message: 'Model library not available' };
        }

        const modelData = window.MODEL_LIBRARY[modelKey];
        if (!modelData) {
            return { success: false, message: `Model '${modelKey}' not found in library` };
        }

        return this.loadModel(modelData, 'Model Selector');
    }
};

console.log('âœ“ ModelLoader: Unified loader initialized');


=== BLOCK: 0301100 | Load Model Modal Controller ===
// Load Model Modal Controller
(function() {
    console.log('ðŸ”§ Load Model Modal: Initializing...');
    
    const modal = document.getElementById('loadModelModal');
    const loadModelBtn = document.getElementById('loadModelBtn');
    const modelSelectModal = document.getElementById('modelSelectModal');
    const loadModelConfirmBtn = document.getElementById('loadModelConfirmBtn');
    
    if (!modal || !loadModelBtn || !modelSelectModal || !loadModelConfirmBtn) {
        console.error('âŒ Load Model Modal: Required elements not found!');
        return;
    }
    
    // Modal controller object
    window.LoadModelModal = {
        open: function() {
            console.log('ðŸ”“ Opening Load Model modal...');
            modal.style.display = 'flex';
            // Reset selection and disable Load button
            modelSelectModal.value = '';
            loadModelConfirmBtn.disabled = true;
        },
        
        close: function() {
            console.log('ðŸ”’ Closing Load Model modal...');
            modal.style.display = 'none';
        },
        
        loadSelected: function() {
            const selectedModel = modelSelectModal.value;
            
            if (!selectedModel) {
                alert('Please select a model first');
                return;
            }
            
            // Check if ModelLoader is available
            if (!window.ModelLoader || !window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary) {
                alert('ModelLoader not available');
                console.error('âŒ ModelLoader not found');
                return;
            }
            
            console.log(`ðŸ“¥ Loading model: ${selectedModel}`);
            
            // Use unified loader
            const result = window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary(selectedModel);
            
            if (result.success) {
                console.log(`âœ“ Model loaded: ${selectedModel}`);
                this.close();
            } else {
                alert(`Failed to load model: ${result.message}`);
                console.error(`âŒ Failed to load model: ${selectedModel}`, result.message);
            }
        }
    };
    
    // Event: Open modal on button click
    loadModelBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        window.LoadModelModal.open();
    });
    
    // Event: Close modal on overlay click
    modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (e.target === modal) {
            window.LoadModelModal.close();
        }
    });
    
    // Event: Enable/disable Load button based on selection
    modelSelectModal.addEventListener('change', function() {
        const hasSelection = modelSelectModal.value !== '';
        loadModelConfirmBtn.disabled = !hasSelection;
        console.log(`ðŸ”˜ Load button ${hasSelection ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}`);
    });
    
    // Event: Load on Enter key in select
    modelSelectModal.addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && modelSelectModal.value) {
            window.LoadModelModal.loadSelected();
        }
    });
    
    console.log('âœ“ Load Model Modal: Initialized');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301150 | JSON Export Module ===
// JSON Export Module - Captures complete model state
// Exports: nodes (BASELINE values), BOM, scenarios, filterPresets
// ONLY SAVES USER INPUT - calculated values are recomputed on import
// IMPORTANT: nodes are serialized from the baseline snapshot, NOT the current
// scenario state, so that scenarios can be correctly replayed on re-import.

window.JSONExport = (function() {
  'use strict';

  /**
   * Export current network state to JSON file.
   * Steps:
   *   1. Check for unsaved scenario changes (offer to save first)
   *   2. Serialize baseline node values (not current scenario state)
   *   3. Include scenarios array + currentScenarioId
   *   4. Download JSON file
   */
  function exportToFile() {
    try {
      const network = window.currentNetwork;
      if (!network || !network.nodes) {
        alert('No network loaded. Please load a model first.');
        return;
      }

      // === STEP 1: Check for unsaved scenario changes ===
      if (window.DDOptim?.scenarioUI?.saveCurrentIfNeeded) {
        const proceed = window.DDOptim.scenarioUI.saveCurrentIfNeeded();
        if (!proceed) return;
      }

      const actualModelName = getActualModelName();

      // === STEP 2: Serialize nodes from BASELINE snapshot ===
      const serializedNodes = serializeNodesFromBaseline(network);

      const exportData = {
        metadata: {
          name: actualModelName,
          description: network.metadata?.description || '',
          version: network.metadata?.version || '1.0',
          exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          exportedBy: 'DDoptim v1.0'
        },
        nodes: serializedNodes,
        bufferProfiles: window.BUFFER_PROFILES || {}
      };

      if (network.filterPresets && network.filterPresets.length > 0) {
        exportData.filterPresets = network.filterPresets;
      }

      // === STEP 3: Include scenarios + current scenario pointer ===
      if (network.scenarios && network.scenarios.length > 0) {
        exportData.scenarios = network.scenarios;
        console.log(`âœ“ Exporting ${network.scenarios.length} scenario(s)`);
      }

      if (network.currentScenarioId) {
        exportData.currentScenarioId = network.currentScenarioId;
      }

      const jsonString = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);

      // === STEP 4: Download ===
      downloadJSON(jsonString, generateFilename(actualModelName));

      console.log('âœ… Network exported successfully:', actualModelName);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('âŒ Export failed:', error);
      alert('Export failed: ' + error.message);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Serialize nodes using the BASELINE snapshot values.
   * Falls back to current network node values if no snapshot exists.
   */
  function serializeNodesFromBaseline(network) {
    const snapshot = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot;

    if (!snapshot) {
      console.warn('[JSONExport] No baseline snapshot found - serializing current node values');
      return serializeNodes(network.nodes);
    }

    console.log('[JSONExport] Serializing nodes from baseline snapshot');
    const nodesArray = [];

    network.nodes.forEach((node, nodeId) => {
      const base = snapshot[nodeId] || {};

      const serialized = {
        // Structural identity (never modified by scenarios)
        id: node.id,
        product: node.product || node.id,
        description: node.description || '',
        location: node.location || '',
        type: base.type !== undefined ? base.type : node.type,

        // BOM relationships - always from current node
        children: node.children || [],

        // User-editable parameters - from BASELINE
        independentADU: base.independentADU !== undefined ? base.independentADU : (node.independentADU || 0),
        leadTime:       base.leadTime      !== undefined ? base.leadTime      : (node.leadTime || 0),
        bufferProfile:  base.bufferProfile !== undefined ? base.bufferProfile : (node.bufferProfile || null),
        customerTolerance: base.customerTolerance !== undefined ? base.customerTolerance : (node.customerTolerance || null),
        moq:            base.moq           !== undefined ? base.moq           : (node.moq || 0),
        orderCycle:     base.orderCycle    !== undefined ? base.orderCycle    : (node.orderCycle || 0),
        unitCost:       base.unitCost      !== undefined ? base.unitCost      : (node.unitCost || 0),

        // Buffer decisions - from BASELINE
        hasBuffer:       base.hasBuffer      !== undefined ? base.hasBuffer      : (node.hasBuffer || false),
        bufferLocked:    base.bufferLocked   !== undefined ? base.bufferLocked   : (node.bufferLocked || false),
        bufferRationale: base.bufferRationale !== undefined ? base.bufferRationale : (node.bufferRationale || ''),
        toleranceMandatory: base.toleranceMandatory !== undefined ? base.toleranceMandatory : (node.toleranceMandatory || false)
      };

      if (!serialized.bufferProfile) delete serialized.bufferProfile;
      if (serialized.customerTolerance === null || serialized.customerTolerance === undefined) delete serialized.customerTolerance;
      if (!serialized.description) delete serialized.description;

      nodesArray.push(serialized);
    });

    return nodesArray;
  }

  /**
   * Fallback: serialize nodes directly from the Map (current state).
   */
  function serializeNodes(nodesMap) {
    const nodesArray = [];

    nodesMap.forEach((node, nodeId) => {
      const serialized = {
        id: node.id,
        product: node.product || node.id,
        description: node.description || '',
        location: node.location || '',
        type: node.type,
        children: node.children || [],
        independentADU: node.independentADU || 0,
        leadTime: node.leadTime || 0,
        bufferProfile: node.bufferProfile || null,
        customerTolerance: node.customerTolerance || null,
        moq: node.moq || 0,
        orderCycle: node.orderCycle || 0,
        unitCost: node.unitCost || 0,
        hasBuffer: node.hasBuffer || false,
        bufferLocked: node.bufferLocked || false,
        bufferRationale: node.bufferRationale || '',
        toleranceMandatory: node.toleranceMandatory || false
      };

      if (!serialized.bufferProfile) delete serialized.bufferProfile;
      if (!serialized.customerTolerance) delete serialized.customerTolerance;
      if (!serialized.description) delete serialized.description;

      nodesArray.push(serialized);
    });

    return nodesArray;
  }

  function getActualModelName() {
    const displayElement = document.getElementById('modelNameDisplay');
    if (displayElement && displayElement.textContent.trim()) return displayElement.textContent.trim();
    if (window.currentModelName) return window.currentModelName;
    if (window.currentNetwork?.metadata?.name) return window.currentNetwork.metadata.name;
    return 'Custom Model';
  }

  function generateFilename(modelName) {
    const cleanName = (modelName || 'model').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/:/g, '-');
    return `ddoptim_${cleanName}_${timestamp}.json`;
  }

  function downloadJSON(jsonString, filename) {
    const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  return { exportToFile: exportToFile };
})();

console.log('âœ… JSON Export module loaded');


=== BLOCK: 0301160 | JSON Import Module ===
// JSON Import Module
// Import network configuration from JSON file

window.JSONImport = {
    /**
     * Trigger file picker and import network from JSON file
     */
    importFromFile: function() {
        console.log('ðŸ“¥ JSON Import: Opening file picker...');
        
        // Create file input element
        const fileInput = document.createElement('input');
        fileInput.type = 'file';
        fileInput.accept = '.json';
        
        fileInput.onchange = function(e) {
            const file = e.target.files[0];
            if (!file) {
                console.log('No file selected');
                return;
            }
            
            console.log('ðŸ“¥ JSON Import: Reading file...', file.name);
            
            const reader = new FileReader();
            
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                try {
                    const jsonData = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
                    console.log('âœ“ JSON parsed successfully');
                    
                    // Use unified loader
                    if (!window.ModelLoader || !window.ModelLoader.loadModel) {
                        alert('Error: ModelLoader not available');
                        console.error('âŒ ModelLoader not found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const result = window.ModelLoader.loadModel(jsonData, 'JSON Import');
                    
                    if (result.success) {
                        alert(`Import successful!\n${result.message}`);
                        console.log('âœ“ Import complete:', result.message);
                    } else {
                        alert(`Import failed:\n${result.message}`);
                        console.error('âŒ Import failed:', result.message);
                    }
                    
                } catch (error) {
                    alert(`Error parsing JSON file:\n${error.message}`);
                    console.error('âŒ JSON parse error:', error);
                }
            };
            
            reader.onerror = function() {
                alert('Error reading file');
                console.error('âŒ File read error');
            };
            
            reader.readAsText(file);
        };
        
        // Trigger file picker
        fileInput.click();
    }
};

console.log('âœ“ JSON Import: Module initialized');

// Export/Import Button Handlers
// Wires up UI buttons to JSONExport and JSONImport modules

(function() {
  'use strict';

  // Wait for DOM to be ready
  function initExportImportHandlers() {
    // Export button
    const exportBtn = document.getElementById('exportJsonBtn');
    if (exportBtn) {
      exportBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        console.log('ðŸ“¤ Export button clicked');
        
        if (window.JSONExport && window.JSONExport.exportToFile) {
          window.JSONExport.exportToFile();
        } else {
          console.error('âŒ JSONExport module not loaded');
          alert('Export functionality not available. Please refresh the page.');
        }
      });
      console.log('âœ… Export button handler attached');
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Export button not found in DOM');
    }

    // Import button
    const importBtn = document.getElementById('importJsonBtn');
    if (importBtn) {
      importBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        console.log('ðŸ“¥ Import button clicked');
        
        if (window.JSONImport && window.JSONImport.importFromFile) {
          window.JSONImport.importFromFile();
        } else {
          console.error('âŒ JSONImport module not loaded');
          alert('Import functionality not available. Please refresh the page.');
        }
      });
      console.log('âœ… Import button handler attached');
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Import button not found in DOM');
    }
  }

  // Initialize when DOM is ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initExportImportHandlers);
  } else {
    initExportImportHandlers();
  }
})();
