=== BLOCK: 0301170 | JS: Datamart Import Dialog ===
// AUDITOR:LARGE_BLOCK_JUSTIFIED - Single cohesive dialog module
window.DatamartImportDialog = (function () {

    var _selectedLocations = [];
    var _locations = [];
    var _onLoadCallback = null;

    var LOCATIONS_SQL = [
        "WITH latest AS (",
        "  SELECT *,",
        "         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY wwarehousepartid, wimportdateid ORDER BY importdatetime ASC) AS rn",
        "  FROM vvwarehouseparts",
        "  WHERE wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvwarehouseparts)",
        "),",
        "warehouses AS (",
        "  SELECT warehousecode,",
        "         warehousedescription,",
        "         COUNT(*) AS part_count",
        "  FROM latest",
        "  WHERE rn = 1",
        "    AND warehousecode IS NOT NULL",
        "    AND warehousecode <> ''",
        "    AND COALESCE(isproductactive, 1) = 1",
        "  GROUP BY warehousecode, warehousedescription",
        ")",
        "SELECT warehousecode, warehousedescription, CAST(part_count AS INTEGER) AS part_count",
        "FROM warehouses",
        "ORDER BY part_count DESC"
    ].join(" ");

    function _el(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

    function _showLoading(msg) {
        _el('datamartModalBody').innerHTML =
            '<div class="dm-loading">' +
            '  <div class="dm-spinner"></div>' +
            '  <span>' + (msg || 'Fetching available locations&hellip;') + '</span>' +
            '</div>';
    }

    function _showError(msg) {
        _el('datamartModalBody').innerHTML =
            '<div class="dm-error">' +
            '  <span style="font-size:16px;">&#9888;</span>' +
            '  <span>' + _escHtml(msg) + '</span>' +
            '</div>';
        _updateFooter();
    }

    function _renderLocations(locations) {
        if (!locations || locations.length === 0) {
            _showError('No warehouse locations found in the Datamart.');
            return;
        }

        var html = '<div class="dm-select-all" id="dmSelectAllRow">' +
            '  <input type="checkbox" id="dmSelectAllChk"> ' +
            '  <label for="dmSelectAllChk" style="cursor:pointer;">Select all locations</label>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="dm-location-list">';

        locations.forEach(function (loc) {
            var desc = (loc.description && loc.description !== loc.code) ? loc.description : '';
            var descHtml = desc ? '<div class="dm-location-desc">' + _escHtml(desc) + '</div>' : '';
            var badge = loc.partCount > 0 ? '<span class="dm-location-badge">' + loc.partCount + ' parts</span>' : '';
            html +=
                '<label class="dm-location-item" data-code="' + _escHtml(loc.code) + '">' +
                '  <input type="checkbox" class="dm-loc-chk" value="' + _escHtml(loc.code) + '">' +
                '  <div class="dm-location-info">' +
                '    <div class="dm-location-code">' + _escHtml(loc.code) + '</div>' +
                '    ' + descHtml +
                '  </div>' +
                '  ' + badge +
                '</label>';
        });
        html += '</div>';
        _el('datamartModalBody').innerHTML = html;

        document.querySelectorAll('.dm-loc-chk').forEach(function (chk) {
            chk.addEventListener('change', _onLocationCheckChange);
        });
        var selectAllChk = _el('dmSelectAllChk');
        if (selectAllChk) {
            selectAllChk.addEventListener('change', function () {
                document.querySelectorAll('.dm-loc-chk').forEach(function (c) {
                    c.checked = selectAllChk.checked;
                    var item = c.closest('.dm-location-item');
                    if (item) item.classList.toggle('is-selected', c.checked);
                });
                _syncSelection();
            });
        }
        _syncSelection();
    }

    function _onLocationCheckChange(e) {
        var item = e.target.closest('.dm-location-item');
        if (item) item.classList.toggle('is-selected', e.target.checked);
        _syncSelectAll();
        _syncSelection();
    }

    function _syncSelectAll() {
        var all = document.querySelectorAll('.dm-loc-chk');
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('.dm-loc-chk:checked');
        var selectAllChk = _el('dmSelectAllChk');
        if (!selectAllChk) return;
        selectAllChk.indeterminate = checked.length > 0 && checked.length < all.length;
        selectAllChk.checked = checked.length === all.length && all.length > 0;
    }

    function _syncSelection() {
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('.dm-loc-chk:checked');
        _selectedLocations = Array.from(checked).map(function (c) { return c.value; });
        _updateFooter();
    }

    function _updateFooter() {
        var info = _el('datamartSelectionInfo');
        var loadBtn = _el('datamartModalLoad');
        var count = _selectedLocations.length;
        if (info) info.textContent = count > 0 ? count + ' location' + (count > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' selected' : 'No location selected';
        if (loadBtn) loadBtn.disabled = count === 0;
    }

    // â”€â”€ Async Datamart query: start â†’ poll â†’ fetch â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    function _fetchLocations() {
        _showLoading();
        _updateFooter();

        function doFetch() {
            if (typeof window.commwiseConfig === 'undefined' || !window.commwiseConfig.nonce) {
                setTimeout(doFetch, 100);
                return;
            }

            // Step 1: start query
            commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
                action: 'start-query',
                query: LOCATIONS_SQL
            })
            .then(function (response) {
                if (!response.body || !response.body.success) {
                    throw new Error(response.body ? response.body.error : 'Failed to start query');
                }
                var queryId = response.body.queryId;
                _showLoading('Query running&hellip;');
                return _pollStatus(queryId);
            })
            .then(function (results) {
                _locations = results.map(function (r) {
                    return {
                        code: r.warehousecode || '',
                        description: r.warehousedescription || '',
                        partCount: parseInt(r.part_count || 0, 10)
                    };
                }).filter(function (l) { return l.code; });
                _renderLocations(_locations);
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
                console.error('[DatamartImportDialog] Error:', err);
                _showError('Failed to load locations: ' + (err.message || String(err)));
            });
        }

        doFetch();
    }

    // Step 2: poll until SUCCEEDED, then fetch results
    function _pollStatus(queryId) {
        return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
            var attempts = 0;
            var maxAttempts = 60;
            var interval = setInterval(function () {
                attempts++;
                if (attempts > maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    reject(new Error('Query timeout'));
                    return;
                }
                commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
                    action: 'check-status',
                    queryId: queryId
                })
                .then(function (res) {
                    if (!res.body || !res.body.success) throw new Error('Status check failed');
                    var status = res.body.status;
                    if (status === 'SUCCEEDED') {
                        clearInterval(interval);
                        _fetchResults(queryId).then(resolve).catch(reject);
                    } else if (status === 'FAILED' || status === 'CANCELLED') {
                        clearInterval(interval);
                        reject(new Error('Query ' + status + ': ' + (res.body.error || '')));
                    }
                })
                .catch(function (err) { clearInterval(interval); reject(err); });
            }, 2000);
        });
    }

    // Step 3: get results
    function _fetchResults(queryId) {
        return commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
            action: 'get-results',
            queryId: queryId
        })
        .then(function (res) {
            if (!res.body || !res.body.success) throw new Error('Failed to get results');
            return res.body.results || [];
        });
    }

    function _escHtml(str) {
        return String(str)
            .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
            .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
            .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
            .replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
    }

    function open(onLoadCallback) {
        _onLoadCallback = onLoadCallback || null;
        _selectedLocations = [];
        _locations = [];
        var modal = _el('datamartModal');
        if (!modal) { console.error('[DatamartImportDialog] Modal element not found'); return; }
        modal.classList.add('is-open');
        _fetchLocations();
    }

    function close() {
        var modal = _el('datamartModal');
        if (modal) modal.classList.remove('is-open');
        _selectedLocations = [];
    }

    function getSelectedLocations() { return _selectedLocations.slice(); }

    return { open: open, close: close, getSelectedLocations: getSelectedLocations };

}());


=== BLOCK: 0301175 | JS: Load from Datamart Button Handler ===
(function () {

    function initDatamartButtonHandlers() {
        // Guard: dialog module must be available
        if (typeof window.DatamartImportDialog === 'undefined') {
            setTimeout(initDatamartButtonHandlers, 100);
            return;
        }
        // Guard: importer module must be available
        if (typeof window.DatamartImporter === 'undefined') {
            setTimeout(initDatamartButtonHandlers, 100);
            return;
        }

        // Guard: DOM elements must be present
        var openBtn   = document.getElementById('loadFromDatamartBtn');
        var closeBtn  = document.getElementById('datamartModalClose');
        var cancelBtn = document.getElementById('datamartModalCancel');
        var loadBtn   = document.getElementById('datamartModalLoad');
        var overlay   = document.getElementById('datamartModal');

        if (!openBtn || !closeBtn || !cancelBtn || !loadBtn || !overlay) {
            setTimeout(initDatamartButtonHandlers, 100);
            return;
        }

        // Open modal
        openBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            window.DatamartImportDialog.open();
        });

        // Close / Cancel
        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            window.DatamartImportDialog.close();
        });
        cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            window.DatamartImportDialog.close();
        });

        // Click overlay backdrop to close
        overlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            if (e.target === overlay) {
                window.DatamartImportDialog.close();
            }
        });

        // Keyboard: Escape closes modal
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                window.DatamartImportDialog.close();
            }
        });

        // Load Selected button: fire real import
        loadBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            var selected = window.DatamartImportDialog.getSelectedLocations();
            if (selected.length === 0) return; // Safety guard (button should be disabled)

            // Disable button to prevent double-click during async import
            loadBtn.disabled = true;

            console.log('[DDOptim] Loading network from locations:', selected);

            window.DatamartImporter.loadNetwork(selected)
                .catch(function (err) {
                    // Error already shown inside modal body by DatamartImporter
                    console.error('[DDOptim] DatamartImporter.loadNetwork failed:', err);
                    // Re-enable button so user can retry
                    loadBtn.disabled = false;
                });
        });

        console.log('[DDOptim] Datamart button handlers initialised (with real import wired up)');
    }

    initDatamartButtonHandlers();

}());


=== BLOCK: 0301177 | DatamartImporter â€” loadNetwork(selectedLocations) ===
// AUDITOR:LARGE_BLOCK_JUSTIFIED - Single cohesive import module with three sequential async queries
window.DatamartImporter = (function () {

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // SQL Queries
    // isproductactive filter removed: raw materials (RM-xx) have
    // isproductactive=0 but are valid BOM components.
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    var NODES_SQL_TEMPLATE = [
        "SELECT",
        "  CAST(wp.wwarehousepartid AS VARCHAR)                             AS id,",
        "  wp.partcode                                                      AS product,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.partdescription, wp.partcode)                        AS description,",
        "  wp.warehousecode                                                 AS location,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.itemtypecode, 'unknown')                             AS type,",
        "  COALESCE(CAST(wp.supplierleadtimedaysdefault AS DOUBLE), 0.0)     AS lead_time,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.adusales, 0.0)                                       AS independent_adu,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.unitcoststandard, 0.0)                               AS unit_cost,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.bufferprofile, wp.partbufferprofileprefix, '')        AS buffer_profile,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.minimumorderquantity, 0.0)                           AS moq,",
        "  COALESCE(wp.desiredordercycledaysactual, 0.0)                    AS order_cycle,",
        "  CASE WHEN wp.planningmodename LIKE '%DDMRP%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS has_buffer",
        "FROM vvwarehouseparts wp",
        "WHERE wp.wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvwarehouseparts)",
        "  AND wp.warehousecode IN (__WH_LIST__)",
        "ORDER BY product"
    ].join(" ");

    var BOM_SQL_TEMPLATE = [
        "SELECT",
        "  CAST(bom.wwarehousepartidparent AS VARCHAR)            AS parent_id,",
        "  CAST(bom.wwarehousepartidchild  AS VARCHAR)            AS child_id,",
        "  COALESCE(bom.bomqty, 1.0)                             AS quantity",
        "FROM vvbillofmaterials bom",
        "JOIN vvwarehouseparts wp_parent",
        "  ON bom.wwarehousepartidparent = wp_parent.wwarehousepartid",
        "  AND wp_parent.wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvwarehouseparts)",
        "  AND wp_parent.warehousecode IN (__WH_LIST__)",
        "JOIN vvwarehouseparts wp_child",
        "  ON bom.wwarehousepartidchild = wp_child.wwarehousepartid",
        "  AND wp_child.wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvwarehouseparts)",
        "  AND wp_child.warehousecode IN (__WH_LIST__)",
        "WHERE bom.wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvbillofmaterials)",
        "  AND COALESCE(bom.phantom, 'N') <> 'Y'",
        "  AND COALESCE(bom.bomqty, 0.0) > 0",
        "ORDER BY parent_id, child_id"
    ].join(" ");

    // Transfer links: wp_child.suppliercode = source warehouse (= supplier/parent in DDoptim BOM)
    // DDoptim convention: children[] = sources/components, parents[] = consumers
    // So: source warehouse (supplier) is the CHILD, destination warehouse is the PARENT
    var TRANSFER_SQL_TEMPLATE = [
        "SELECT",
        "  CAST(wp_dest.wwarehousepartid   AS VARCHAR)  AS parent_id,",
        "  CAST(wp_source.wwarehousepartid AS VARCHAR)  AS child_id",
        "FROM vvwarehouseparts wp_dest",
        "JOIN vvwarehouseparts wp_source",
        "  ON  wp_source.partcode       = wp_dest.partcode",
        "  AND wp_source.warehousecode  = wp_dest.suppliercode",
        "  AND wp_source.wimportdateid  = wp_dest.wimportdateid",
        "WHERE wp_dest.wimportdateid = (SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) FROM vvwarehouseparts)",
        "  AND wp_dest.warehousecode   IN (__WH_LIST__)",
        "  AND wp_dest.suppliercode    IN (__WH_LIST__)",
        "  AND wp_dest.suppliercode IS NOT NULL",
        "  AND wp_dest.suppliercode   <> ''",
        "ORDER BY parent_id, child_id"
    ].join(" ");

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Athena async helper: start â†’ poll â†’ fetch
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function _buildWhList(locations) {
        return locations.map(function (l) { return "'" + l.replace(/'/g, "''") + "'"; }).join(',');
    }

    function _startQuery(sql) {
        return commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
            action: 'start-query',
            query: sql
        }).then(function (response) {
            if (!response.body || !response.body.success) {
                throw new Error(response.body ? response.body.error : 'Failed to start query');
            }
            return response.body.queryId;
        });
    }

    function _pollStatus(queryId) {
        return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
            var attempts = 0;
            var maxAttempts = 90;
            var interval = setInterval(function () {
                attempts++;
                if (attempts > maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    reject(new Error('Query timeout after 3 minutes'));
                    return;
                }
                commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
                    action: 'check-status',
                    queryId: queryId
                })
                .then(function (res) {
                    if (!res.body || !res.body.success) throw new Error('Status check failed');
                    var status = res.body.status;
                    if (status === 'SUCCEEDED') {
                        clearInterval(interval);
                        _fetchResults(queryId).then(resolve).catch(reject);
                    } else if (status === 'FAILED' || status === 'CANCELLED') {
                        clearInterval(interval);
                        reject(new Error('Query ' + status + ': ' + (res.body.error || '')));
                    }
                })
                .catch(function (err) { clearInterval(interval); reject(err); });
            }, 2000);
        });
    }

    function _fetchResults(queryId) {
        return commwiseConfigClient.secureRequest('C3', 'DATAMART_PROD', {
            action: 'get-results',
            queryId: queryId
        }).then(function (res) {
            if (!res.body || !res.body.success) throw new Error('Failed to get results');
            return res.body.results || [];
        });
    }

    function _runQuery(sql, label) {
        console.log('[DatamartImporter] Starting query: ' + label);
        return _startQuery(sql)
            .then(function (queryId) {
                console.log('[DatamartImporter] ' + label + ' queryId: ' + queryId);
                return _pollStatus(queryId);
            })
            .then(function (rows) {
                console.log('[DatamartImporter] ' + label + ' returned ' + rows.length + ' rows');
                return rows;
            });
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Buffer profile normalisation
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    var VALID_PROFILES = ['F', 'I', 'U', 'AL', 'AI'];

    function _normaliseProfile(raw) {
        if (!raw) return 'I';
        var upper = String(raw).toUpperCase().trim();
        if (VALID_PROFILES.indexOf(upper) !== -1) return upper;
        for (var i = 0; i < VALID_PROFILES.length; i++) {
            if (upper.indexOf(VALID_PROFILES[i]) === 0) return VALID_PROFILES[i];
        }
        return 'I';
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Transform raw SQL rows â†’ DDoptim node objects
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function _buildNodes(rows) {
        var nodes = [];
        rows.forEach(function (r) {
            nodes.push({
                id:               String(r.id || ''),
                product:          String(r.product || r.id || ''),
                description:      String(r.description || ''),
                location:         String(r.location || ''),
                type:             String(r.type || 'unknown'),
                leadTime:         parseFloat(r.lead_time) || 0,
                independentADU:   parseFloat(r.independent_adu) || 0,
                unitCost:         parseFloat(r.unit_cost) || 0,
                bufferProfile:    _normaliseProfile(r.buffer_profile),
                moq:              parseFloat(r.moq) || 0,
                orderCycle:       parseFloat(r.order_cycle) || 0,
                hasBuffer:        (parseInt(r.has_buffer, 10) === 1),
                bufferLocked:     false,
                bufferRationale:  '',
                customerTolerance: null,
                children:         []
            });
        });
        return nodes;
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Attach BOM + transport links to nodes as children[] arrays
    // Each child entry: { id, quantity, linkType: "bom" | "transport" }
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function _attachLinks(nodes, bomRows, transferRows) {
        var nodeIds = new Set(nodes.map(function (n) { return n.id; }));
        var childrenByParent = {};
        var skippedBom = 0;
        var skippedTransfer = 0;

        // Helper: add a child entry (deduplicates by parentId+childId+linkType)
        function _addChild(parentId, childId, quantity, linkType) {
            if (!nodeIds.has(parentId) || !nodeIds.has(childId)) return false;
            if (!childrenByParent[parentId]) childrenByParent[parentId] = [];
            var existing = childrenByParent[parentId].find(function (c) {
                return c.id === childId && c.linkType === linkType;
            });
            if (!existing) {
                childrenByParent[parentId].push({ id: childId, quantity: quantity, linkType: linkType });
            }
            return true;
        }

        // BOM links
        bomRows.forEach(function (r) {
            var ok = _addChild(
                String(r.parent_id || ''),
                String(r.child_id  || ''),
                parseFloat(r.quantity) || 1,
                'bom'
            );
            if (!ok) skippedBom++;
        });

        // Transport links (quantity always 1:1)
        transferRows.forEach(function (r) {
            var ok = _addChild(
                String(r.parent_id || ''),
                String(r.child_id  || ''),
                1,
                'transport'
            );
            if (!ok) skippedTransfer++;
        });

        if (skippedBom > 0) {
            console.warn('[DatamartImporter] Skipped ' + skippedBom + ' BOM rows referencing nodes outside selected locations');
        }
        if (skippedTransfer > 0) {
            console.warn('[DatamartImporter] Skipped ' + skippedTransfer + ' transfer rows referencing nodes outside selected locations');
        }

        nodes.forEach(function (node) {
            node.children = childrenByParent[node.id] || [];
        });

        return nodes;
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Progress feedback
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function _updateProgress(msg) {
        var body = document.getElementById('datamartModalBody');
        if (body) {
            body.innerHTML = '<div class="dm-loading"><div class="dm-spinner"></div><span>' + msg + '</span></div>';
        }
        console.log('[DatamartImporter] ' + msg);
    }

    function _updateProgressDone(msg) {
        var body = document.getElementById('datamartModalBody');
        if (body) {
            body.innerHTML = '<div class="dm-loading"><span style="font-size:16px;">&#10003;&nbsp;</span><span>' + msg + '</span></div>';
        }
        console.log('[DatamartImporter] ' + msg);
    }

    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    // Public API
    // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    function loadNetwork(selectedLocations) {
        if (!selectedLocations || selectedLocations.length === 0) {
            return Promise.reject(new Error('No locations selected'));
        }

        var whList       = _buildWhList(selectedLocations);
        var nodesSql     = NODES_SQL_TEMPLATE.replace(/__WH_LIST__/g, whList);
        var bomSql       = BOM_SQL_TEMPLATE.replace(/__WH_LIST__/g, whList);
        var transferSql  = TRANSFER_SQL_TEMPLATE.replace(/__WH_LIST__/g, whList);

        _updateProgress('Fetching parts from DataMart&hellip; (1/3)');

        return _runQuery(nodesSql, 'Nodes')
            .then(function (nodeRows) {
                if (nodeRows.length === 0) {
                    throw new Error('No parts found for: ' + selectedLocations.join(', '));
                }
                _updateProgress('Fetching BOM relationships&hellip; (2/3)');
                return _runQuery(bomSql, 'BOM').then(function (bomRows) {
                    return { nodeRows: nodeRows, bomRows: bomRows };
                });
            })
            .then(function (data) {
                _updateProgress('Fetching transport links&hellip; (3/3)');
                return _runQuery(transferSql, 'Transfer').then(function (transferRows) {
                    data.transferRows = transferRows;
                    return data;
                });
            })
            .then(function (data) {
                _updateProgress('Building network model&hellip;');

                var nodes = _buildNodes(data.nodeRows);
                nodes     = _attachLinks(nodes, data.bomRows, data.transferRows);

                var bomEdges      = nodes.reduce(function (s, n) { return s + n.children.filter(function (c) { return (c.linkType || 'bom') !== 'transport'; }).length; }, 0);
                var transferEdges = nodes.reduce(function (s, n) { return s + n.children.filter(function (c) { return c.linkType === 'transport'; }).length; }, 0);

                var modelData = {
                    metadata: {
                        name:            'DataMart \u2014 ' + selectedLocations.join(', '),
                        description:     'Imported from B2WISE DataMart. Locations: ' + selectedLocations.join(', '),
                        version:         '1.0',
                        importedAt:      new Date().toISOString(),
                        sourceLocations: selectedLocations
                    },
                    nodes:         nodes,
                    scenarios:     [],
                    filterPresets: []
                };

                console.log('[DatamartImporter] Model: ' + nodes.length + ' nodes, ' + bomEdges + ' BOM edges, ' + transferEdges + ' transport links');

                var result = window.ModelLoader.loadModel(modelData, 'DataMart Import');

                if (result.success) {
                    _updateProgressDone(
                        nodes.length + ' parts loaded &mdash; ' +
                        bomEdges + ' BOM links, ' + transferEdges + ' transport links &mdash; ' +
                        selectedLocations.length + ' location(s).' +
                        '<br><small style="color:#888;">Set customer tolerance manually on finished products.</small>'
                    );
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        if (window.DatamartImportDialog) window.DatamartImportDialog.close();
                    }, 2500);
                } else {
                    throw new Error(result.message);
                }
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
                console.error('[DatamartImporter] Error:', err);
                var body = document.getElementById('datamartModalBody');
                if (body) {
                    body.innerHTML =
                        '<div class="dm-error">' +
                        '<span style="font-size:16px;">&#9888;</span>' +
                        '<span>' + String(err.message || err) + '</span>' +
                        '</div>';
                }
                var loadBtn = document.getElementById('datamartModalLoad');
                if (loadBtn) loadBtn.disabled = false;
                throw err;
            });
    }

    return { loadNetwork: loadNetwork };

}());

console.log('\u2713 DatamartImporter: module initialized');


=== BLOCK: 0301200 | Initialization - Test Network ===
// DDoptim Initialization
// Load Simple Chain model by default on startup

(function() {
    console.log('DDoptim initialization starting...');
    
    function tryInitialize() {
        // Check dependencies
        if (typeof d3 === 'undefined') {
            console.log('Waiting for D3.js...');
            setTimeout(tryInitialize, 100);
            return;
        }
        
        if (typeof NetworkRenderer === 'undefined') {
            console.log('Waiting for NetworkRenderer...');
            setTimeout(tryInitialize, 100);
            return;
        }
        
        if (!window.ModelLoader || !window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary) {
            console.log('Waiting for ModelLoader...');
            setTimeout(tryInitialize, 100);
            return;
        }
        
        if (!window.MODEL_LIBRARY || !window.MODEL_LIBRARY.simple_chain) {
            console.log('Waiting for MODEL_LIBRARY...');
            setTimeout(tryInitialize, 100);
            return;
        }
        
        // All dependencies ready!
        console.log('All modules loaded, initializing...');
        console.log('D3.js version:', d3.version);
        
        try {
            // Initialize network renderer
            NetworkRenderer.init();
            console.log('âœ“ NetworkRenderer initialized');
            
            // Load Simple Chain model by default on startup
            console.log('Loading default model: Simple Chain...');
            const result = window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary('simple_chain');
            
            if (result.success) {
                console.log('âœ“ Initialization complete:', result.message);
                
                // Update model selector UI to reflect loaded model
                const modelSelect = document.getElementById('modelSelect');
                const modelStatus = document.getElementById('modelStatus');
                if (modelSelect) {
                    modelSelect.value = 'simple_chain';
                }
                if (modelStatus) {
                    modelStatus.textContent = result.message;
                    modelStatus.style.color = '#10b981';
                }
            } else {
                console.error('âŒ Initialization failed:', result.message);
            }
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('âœ— Error during initialization:', error);
            console.error('Stack trace:', error.stack);
        }
    }
    
    // Start initialization
    setTimeout(tryInitialize, 100);
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301210 | Filter Engine ===
/**
 * Filter Engine Module
 * Applies filter criteria to nodes array
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Filter Engine] Initializing...');
    
    // Column type definitions for proper filtering
    const COLUMN_TYPES = {
        // Text columns
        name: 'text',
        bufferRationale: 'text',
        toleranceMandatory: 'boolean',
        
        // Enum columns
        type: 'enum',
        bufferProfile: 'enum',
        
        // Number columns
        leadTime: 'number',
        clt: 'number',
        dlt: 'number',
        independentADU: 'number',
        calculatedADU: 'number',
        customerTolerance: 'number',
        missingCustomerLeadTime: 'number',
        ltExceeding: 'number',
        inventoryValue: 'number',
        moq: 'number',
        orderCycle: 'number',
        unitCost: 'number',
        
        // Boolean columns
        hasBuffer: 'boolean',
        bufferLocked: 'boolean'
    };
    
    // Enum options for multi-select filters
    const ENUM_OPTIONS = {
        type: [
            'finished_product',
            'intermediate',
            'machined',
            'purchased_local',
            'purchased_international'
        ],
        bufferProfile: ['F', 'I', 'U', 'AL', 'AI']
    };
    
    /**
     * Get column type
     */
    function getColumnType(column) {
        return COLUMN_TYPES[column] || 'text';
    }
    
    /**
     * Get enum options for a column
     * For 'type' and 'bufferProfile', dynamically extract distinct values from loaded network
     */
    function getEnumOptions(column) {
        if (column === 'type' || column === 'bufferProfile') {
            const network = window.currentNetwork;
            if (network && network.nodes) {
                const nodes = network.nodes instanceof Map
                    ? Array.from(network.nodes.values())
                    : Object.values(network.nodes);
                const values = [...new Set(
                    nodes.map(n => n[column]).filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '')
                )].sort();
                if (values.length > 0) return values;
            }
        }
        return ENUM_OPTIONS[column] || [];
    }
    
    /**
     * Match a single node against a filter
     */
    function matchFilter(node, filter) {
        const { column, operator, value } = filter;
        const nodeValue = node[column];
        
        // Handle null/undefined values
        if (nodeValue === null || nodeValue === undefined) {
            // For boolean filters, treat null as false
            if (operator === 'equals' && COLUMN_TYPES[column] === 'boolean') {
                return value === false || value === null;
            }
            // For other operators, null doesn't match
            return false;
        }
        
        switch (operator) {
            case 'contains':
                // Case-insensitive text search
                return String(nodeValue)
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .includes(String(value).toLowerCase());
            
            case 'equals':
                if (COLUMN_TYPES[column] === 'boolean') {
                    return nodeValue === value;
                }
                return nodeValue === value;
            
            case 'in':
                // Multi-select: check if value is in array
                return Array.isArray(value) && value.includes(nodeValue);
            
            case 'range':
                // Numeric range: {min, max}
                if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
                const numValue = Number(nodeValue);
                if (isNaN(numValue)) return false;
                
                const hasMin = value.min !== null && value.min !== undefined && value.min !== '';
                const hasMax = value.max !== null && value.max !== undefined && value.max !== '';
                
                if (hasMin && hasMax) {
                    return numValue >= Number(value.min) && numValue <= Number(value.max);
                } else if (hasMin) {
                    return numValue >= Number(value.min);
                } else if (hasMax) {
                    return numValue <= Number(value.max);
                }
                return true; // No constraints
            
            default:
                console.warn('[Filter Engine] Unknown operator:', operator);
                return true;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Apply all filters to nodes array
     * Returns filtered array
     */
    function applyFilters(nodes, filters) {
        if (!filters || filters.length === 0) {
            return nodes;
        }
        
        console.log('[Filter Engine] Applying', filters.length, 'filter(s) to', nodes.length, 'nodes');
        
        // AND logic: node must match ALL filters
        const filtered = nodes.filter(node => {
            return filters.every(filter => matchFilter(node, filter));
        });
        
        console.log('[Filter Engine] Result:', filtered.length, 'nodes match');
        return filtered;
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate filter structure
     */
    function validateFilter(filter) {
        if (!filter.column) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Column is required' };
        }
        
        if (!filter.operator) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Operator is required' };
        }
        
        if (filter.value === null || filter.value === undefined || filter.value === '') {
            // Range can have empty min/max
            if (filter.operator !== 'range') {
                return { valid: false, error: 'Value is required' };
            }
        }
        
        // Validate operator for column type
        const columnType = getColumnType(filter.column);
        
        if (columnType === 'boolean' && filter.operator !== 'equals') {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Boolean columns only support "equals" operator' };
        }
        
        if (columnType === 'number' && !['equals', 'range'].includes(filter.operator)) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Number columns only support "equals" or "range" operators' };
        }
        
        return { valid: true };
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.filterEngine = {
        applyFilters,
        matchFilter,
        validateFilter,
        getColumnType,
        getEnumOptions,
        COLUMN_TYPES,
        ENUM_OPTIONS
    };
    
    console.log('[Filter Engine] âœ“ Initialized');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301220 | Sort Engine ===
// SCRIPT 1220: Sort Engine

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('â¬†ï¸ Loading Sort Engine...');
  
  /**
   * Sort nodes by a column
   * @param {Array} nodes - Array of node objects
   * @param {string|null} sortColumn - Column name to sort by (null = no sort)
   * @param {string} sortOrder - 'asc' or 'desc'
   * @returns {Array} Sorted array (new array, does not mutate input)
   */
  function sortNodes(nodes, sortColumn, sortOrder = 'asc') {
    if (!sortColumn || !nodes || nodes.length === 0) {
      return nodes;
    }
    
    // Create copy to avoid mutating input
    const sorted = [...nodes];
    
    sorted.sort((a, b) => {
      const aVal = a[sortColumn];
      const bVal = b[sortColumn];
      
      // Null/undefined handling - always last
      if (aVal == null && bVal == null) return 0;
      if (aVal == null) return 1;
      if (bVal == null) return -1;
      
      // Type-specific comparison
      let result;
      
      if (typeof aVal === 'string' && typeof bVal === 'string') {
        // String comparison (case-insensitive)
        result = aVal.toLowerCase().localeCompare(bVal.toLowerCase());
      } else if (typeof aVal === 'boolean' && typeof bVal === 'boolean') {
        // Boolean comparison (false < true)
        result = aVal === bVal ? 0 : (aVal ? 1 : -1);
      } else {
        // Numeric comparison (handles numbers and numeric strings)
        const aNum = Number(aVal);
        const bNum = Number(bVal);
        
        if (isNaN(aNum) && isNaN(bNum)) {
          // Both non-numeric, compare as strings
          result = String(aVal).localeCompare(String(bVal));
        } else if (isNaN(aNum)) {
          return 1; // a is non-numeric, goes after
        } else if (isNaN(bNum)) {
          return -1; // b is non-numeric, goes after
        } else {
          result = aNum - bNum;
        }
      }
      
      // Apply sort order
      return sortOrder === 'asc' ? result : -result;
    });
    
    return sorted;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get the next sort state for a column
   * @param {string} column - Column name
   * @param {string|null} currentColumn - Currently sorted column
   * @param {string} currentOrder - Current sort order ('asc' or 'desc')
   * @returns {Object} {column, order} - New sort state (null column = clear sort)
   */
  function getNextSortState(column, currentColumn, currentOrder) {
    if (column !== currentColumn) {
      // New column - start with ascending
      return { column: column, order: 'asc' };
    }
    
    // Same column - cycle through: asc â†’ desc â†’ clear
    if (currentOrder === 'asc') {
      return { column: column, order: 'desc' };
    } else {
      // desc â†’ clear (no sort)
      return { column: null, order: 'asc' };
    }
  }
  
  // Expose functions globally
  if (!window.DDOptim) {
    window.DDOptim = {};
  }
  
  window.DDOptim.sortNodes = sortNodes;
  window.DDOptim.getNextSortState = getNextSortState;
  
  console.log('âœ… Sort Engine loaded');
  
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301230 | Filter UI - Init & Events ===
/**
 * Filter UI Controller - Initialization & Event Binding
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    // Wait for dependencies (max 10 attempts = 1 second)
    function waitForDependencies(attemptsLeft) {
        if (!window.DDOptim || 
            !window.DDOptim.filterEngine || 
            !window.DDOptim.renderTable ||
            !window.DDOptim.tabularView) {
            if (attemptsLeft > 0) {
                setTimeout(function() { waitForDependencies(attemptsLeft - 1); }, 100);
            }
            // Phase 2 not yet loaded - stop silently
            return;
        }
        init();
    }
    
    function init() {
        // Get DOM elements
        const addFilterBtn = document.getElementById('add-filter-btn');
        const clearFiltersBtn = document.getElementById('clear-filters-btn');
        const cancelFilterBtn = document.getElementById('cancel-filter-btn');
        const applyFilterBtn = document.getElementById('apply-filter-btn');
        const filterColumnSelect = document.getElementById('filter-column');
        const filterOperatorSelect = document.getElementById('filter-operator');
        
        if (!addFilterBtn || !clearFiltersBtn) {
            // Filter panel DOM not yet implemented (Phase 2) - stop silently
            return;
        }
        
        // Attach event listeners
        addFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.showAddFilterForm();
        });
        
        clearFiltersBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.clearAllFilters();
        });
        
        cancelFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.hideAddFilterForm();
        });
        
        applyFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.applyFilter();
        });
        
        filterColumnSelect.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.onColumnChange(e);
        });
        
        filterOperatorSelect.addEventListener('change', function() {
            window.DDOptim.filterUI.onOperatorChange();
        });
        
        console.log('[Filter UI Init] âœ“ Event listeners attached');
        
        // Render initial state
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.renderActiveFilters();
    }
    
    // Create namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.filterUI = window.DDOptim.filterUI || {};
    
    // Start initialization
    waitForDependencies();
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Init] Module loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301232 | Filter UI - Form Management ===
/**
 * Filter UI - Form Management
 * Handle show/hide form and dynamic input generation
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Form] Loading...');
    
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.filterUI = window.DDOptim.filterUI || {};
    
    /**
     * Show add filter form
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.showAddFilterForm = function() {
        const form = document.getElementById('add-filter-form');
        form.classList.remove('hidden');
        
        // Reset form
        document.getElementById('filter-column').value = '';
        document.getElementById('filter-operator-row').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('filter-value-row').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('apply-filter-btn').disabled = true;
        
        console.log('[Filter UI Form] Add filter form shown');
    };
    
    /**
     * Hide add filter form
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.hideAddFilterForm = function() {
        const form = document.getElementById('add-filter-form');
        form.classList.add('hidden');
        console.log('[Filter UI Form] Add filter form hidden');
    };
    
    /**
     * Handle column selection change
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.onColumnChange = function(e) {
        const column = e.target.value;
        
        if (!column) {
            document.getElementById('filter-operator-row').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('filter-value-row').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('apply-filter-btn').disabled = true;
            return;
        }
        
        // Show operator row
        document.getElementById('filter-operator-row').classList.remove('hidden');
        
        // Update operator options based on column type
        const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(column);
        const operatorSelect = document.getElementById('filter-operator');
        
        operatorSelect.innerHTML = '';
        
        if (columnType === 'text') {
            operatorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="contains">Contains</option>';
        } else if (columnType === 'enum') {
            operatorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="in">In (select multiple)</option>';
        } else if (columnType === 'number') {
            operatorSelect.innerHTML = `
                <option value="range">Range</option>
                <option value="equals">Equals</option>
            `;
        } else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
            operatorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="equals">Is</option>';
        }
        
        // Trigger operator change to build value input
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.onOperatorChange();
    };
    
    /**
     * Handle operator change - build value input
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.onOperatorChange = function() {
        const column = document.getElementById('filter-column').value;
        const operator = document.getElementById('filter-operator').value;
        
        if (!column || !operator) {
            document.getElementById('filter-value-row').classList.add('hidden');
            return;
        }
        
        const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(column);
        const valueRow = document.getElementById('filter-value-row');
        valueRow.classList.remove('hidden');
        
        // Build value input based on type and operator
        let html = '<label for="filter-value">Value:</label>';
        
        if (columnType === 'text') {
            html += '<input type="text" id="filter-value" class="filter-input" placeholder="Enter text...">';
        } else if (columnType === 'enum') {
            const options = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getEnumOptions(column);
            html += '<select id="filter-value" class="filter-select" multiple size="5">';
            options.forEach(opt => {
                html += `<option value="${opt}">${formatEnumLabel(opt)}</option>`;
            });
            html += '</select>';
        } else if (columnType === 'number') {
            if (operator === 'range') {
                html += `
                    <div class="range-inputs">
                        <input type="number" id="filter-value-min" class="filter-input" placeholder="Min" step="any">
                        <span class="range-separator">to</span>
                        <input type="number" id="filter-value-max" class="filter-input" placeholder="Max" step="any">
                    </div>
                `;
            } else {
                html += '<input type="number" id="filter-value" class="filter-input" placeholder="Enter number..." step="any">';
            }
        } else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
            html += `
                <select id="filter-value" class="filter-select">
                    <option value="true">Yes</option>
                    <option value="false">No</option>
                </select>
            `;
        }
        
        valueRow.innerHTML = html;
        
        // Enable apply button
        document.getElementById('apply-filter-btn').disabled = false;
    };
    
    /**
     * Format enum label for display
     */
    function formatEnumLabel(value) {
        const labels = {
            'finished_product': 'Finished Product',
            'intermediate': 'Intermediate',
            'machined': 'Machined',
            'purchased_local': 'Purchased Local',
            'purchased_international': 'Purchased International'
        };
        return labels[value] || value;
    }
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Form] Module loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301234 | Filter UI - Operations ===
/**
 * Filter UI - Filter Operations
 * Handle apply, remove, clear operations
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Operations] Loading...');
    
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.filterUI = window.DDOptim.filterUI || {};
    
    /**
     * Apply filter
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.applyFilter = function() {
        const column = document.getElementById('filter-column').value;
        const operator = document.getElementById('filter-operator').value;
        const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(column);
        
        let value;
        
        // Get value based on column type
        if (columnType === 'enum') {
            const select = document.getElementById('filter-value');
            value = Array.from(select.selectedOptions).map(opt => opt.value);
            if (value.length === 0) {
                alert('Please select at least one value');
                return;
            }
        } else if (columnType === 'number' && operator === 'range') {
            const min = document.getElementById('filter-value-min').value;
            const max = document.getElementById('filter-value-max').value;
            if (!min && !max) {
                alert('Please enter at least a minimum or maximum value');
                return;
            }
            value = {
                min: min ? Number(min) : null,
                max: max ? Number(max) : null
            };
        } else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
            value = document.getElementById('filter-value').value === 'true';
        } else if (columnType === 'number') {
            value = Number(document.getElementById('filter-value').value);
        } else {
            value = document.getElementById('filter-value').value;
        }
        
        // Create filter object
        const filter = { column, operator, value };
        
        // Validate
        const validation = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.validateFilter(filter);
        if (!validation.valid) {
            alert('Invalid filter: ' + validation.error);
            return;
        }
        
        // Add to active filters
        window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.push(filter);
        
        console.log('[Filter UI Operations] Filter applied:', filter);
        console.log('[Filter UI Operations] Active filters:', window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.length);
        
        // Hide form and re-render
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.hideAddFilterForm();
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.renderActiveFilters();
        
        // Re-render table with filters
        if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
            window.DDOptim.renderTable();
        }
    };
    
    /**
     * Remove a filter by index
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.removeFilter = function(index) {
        window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.splice(index, 1);
        console.log('[Filter UI Operations] Filter removed, remaining:', window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.length);
        
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.renderActiveFilters();
        
        // Re-render table
        if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
            window.DDOptim.renderTable();
        }
    };
    
    /**
     * Clear all filters
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.clearAllFilters = function() {
        window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = [];
        console.log('[Filter UI Operations] All filters cleared');
        
        window.DDOptim.filterUI.renderActiveFilters();
        
        // Re-render table
        if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
            window.DDOptim.renderTable();
        }
    };
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Operations] Module loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301236 | Filter UI - Display ===
/**
 * Filter UI - Display & Rendering
 * Render active filters with badges
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Display] Loading...');
    
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.filterUI = window.DDOptim.filterUI || {};
    
    /**
     * Render active filters display
     */
    window.DDOptim.filterUI.renderActiveFilters = function() {
        const container = document.getElementById('active-filters-list');
        const filters = window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters;
        
        if (!container) {
            console.warn('[Filter UI Display] active-filters-list container not found');
            return;
        }
        
        if (!filters || filters.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="no-filters-message">No filters applied</div>';
            return;
        }
        
        let html = '';
        filters.forEach((filter, index) => {
            html += `
                <div class="filter-badge">
                    <span class="filter-text">${formatFilter(filter)}</span>
                    <button class="filter-remove-btn" data-index="${index}" title="Remove filter">Ã—</button>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        
        container.innerHTML = html;
        
        // Attach remove handlers
        container.querySelectorAll('.filter-remove-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.DDOptim.filterUI.removeFilter(parseInt(this.dataset.index));
            });
        });
        
        console.log('[Filter UI Display] Rendered', filters.length, 'filter badge(s)');
    };
    
    /**
     * Format filter for display
     */
    function formatFilter(filter) {
        const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(filter.column);
        let text = `${formatColumnName(filter.column)}: `;
        
        if (filter.operator === 'contains') {
            text += `contains "${filter.value}"`;
        } else if (filter.operator === 'in') {
            text += filter.value.map(v => formatEnumLabel(v)).join(', ');
        } else if (filter.operator === 'range') {
            if (filter.value.min !== null && filter.value.max !== null) {
                text += `${filter.value.min} - ${filter.value.max}`;
            } else if (filter.value.min !== null) {
                text += `â‰¥ ${filter.value.min}`;
            } else {
                text += `â‰¤ ${filter.value.max}`;
            }
        } else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
            text += filter.value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
        } else {
            text += filter.value;
        }
        
        return text;
    }
    
    /**
     * Format column name for display
     */
    function formatColumnName(column) {
        const labels = {
            name: 'Name',
            type: 'Type',
            leadTime: 'Lead Time',
            clt: 'CLT',
            dlt: 'DLT',
            independentADU: 'Independent ADU',
            calculatedADU: 'Calculated ADU',
            hasBuffer: 'Has Buffer',
            bufferLocked: 'Buffer Locked',
            bufferProfile: 'Buffer Profile',
            toleranceMandatory: 'Tolerance Mandatory',
            customerTolerance: 'Customer Tolerance',
            missingCustomerLeadTime: 'Missing Tolerance',
            ltExceeding: 'LT Exceeding',
            inventoryValue: 'Inventory Value',
            moq: 'MOQ',
            unitCost: 'Unit Cost'
        };
        return labels[column] || column;
    }
    
    /**
     * Format enum label for display
     */
    function formatEnumLabel(value) {
        const labels = {
            'finished_product': 'Finished Product',
            'intermediate': 'Intermediate',
            'machined': 'Machined',
            'purchased_local': 'Purchased Local',
            'purchased_international': 'Purchased International'
        };
        return labels[value] || value;
    }
    
    console.log('[Filter UI Display] Module loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301240 | Table Event Handlers ===
// SCRIPT 1240: Table Event Handlers

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('ðŸ“Œ Loading Table Event Handlers...');
  
  // Initialize on DOM ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }
  
  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ“Œ Initializing table event handlers...');
    
    // Use event delegation for row clicks (tbody level)
    const tableBody = document.getElementById('table-body');
    if (tableBody) {
      tableBody.addEventListener('click', handleRowClick);
      console.log('âœ… Row click handler attached');
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Table body not found');
    }
    
    // Attach handlers to all sortable headers
    const sortableHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.ddoptim-table thead th.sortable');
    sortableHeaders.forEach(header => {
      header.addEventListener('click', handleHeaderClick);
    });
    
    if (sortableHeaders.length > 0) {
      console.log(`âœ… Sort handlers attached to ${sortableHeaders.length} columns`);
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ No sortable headers found');
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Handle row click - open detail panel for clicked node
   * @param {Event} event - Click event
   */
  function handleRowClick(event) {
    // Find the row (in case user clicked a cell)
    const row = event.target.closest('tr');
    if (!row || !row.dataset.nodeId) return;
    
    const nodeId = row.dataset.nodeId;
    console.log(`ðŸ‘ï¸ Row clicked: ${nodeId}`);
    
    // Highlight selected row
    highlightSelectedRow(nodeId);
    
    // Open detail panel using existing handler
    if (window.UIHandlers && typeof window.UIHandlers.showNodeDetails === 'function') {
      window.UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
    } else if (window.DDOptim && typeof window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect === 'function') {
      // Fallback to DDOptim handler
      window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect(nodeId);
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ No detail panel handler available');
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Highlight the selected row in the table
   * @param {string} nodeId - ID of the selected node
   */
  function highlightSelectedRow(nodeId) {
    console.log(`ðŸ‘‰ Highlighting row: ${nodeId}`);
    
    // Remove existing selection
    const allRows = document.querySelectorAll('.ddoptim-table tbody tr');
    console.log(`   Found ${allRows.length} rows`);
    allRows.forEach(r => r.classList.remove('selected'));
    
    // Add selection to clicked row
    const selectedRow = document.querySelector(`.ddoptim-table tbody tr[data-node-id="${nodeId}"]`);
    if (selectedRow) {
      selectedRow.classList.add('selected');
      console.log(`   âœ… Added 'selected' class to row ${nodeId}`);
    } else {
      console.warn(`   âš ï¸ Could not find row with data-node-id="${nodeId}"`);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Handle header click - toggle sort
   * @param {Event} event - Click event
   */
  function handleHeaderClick(event) {
    const header = event.currentTarget;
    const column = header.dataset.column;
    
    if (!column) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Header has no data-column attribute');
      return;
    }
    
    console.log(`â¬†ï¸â¬‡ï¸ Header clicked: ${column}`);
    
    // Get current sort state
    const currentColumn = window.DDOptim?.tabularView?.sortColumn;
    const currentOrder = window.DDOptim?.tabularView?.sortOrder || 'asc';
    
    // Get next sort state
    if (typeof window.DDOptim?.getNextSortState !== 'function') {
      console.error('âŒ getNextSortState function not available');
      return;
    }
    
    const nextState = window.DDOptim.getNextSortState(column, currentColumn, currentOrder);
    
    // Update state
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabularView) {
      window.DDOptim.tabularView.sortColumn = nextState.column;
      window.DDOptim.tabularView.sortOrder = nextState.order;
    }
    
    console.log(`ðŸ”„ Sort state: column=${nextState.column}, order=${nextState.order}`);
    
    // Re-render table
    if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.renderTable();
    } else {
      console.error('âŒ renderTable function not available');
    }
  }
  
  // Expose highlight function for external use
  if (!window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim = {};
  window.DDOptim.highlightSelectedRow = highlightSelectedRow;
  
  console.log('âœ… Table Event Handlers loaded');
  
})();

=== BLOCK: 0301260 | View Toggle Handler ===
// SCRIPT 1260: View Toggle Handler (Extended for 3 views)

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('ðŸ”„ Loading View Toggle Handler...');
  
  // Wait for both DOM and DDOptim to be ready
  function waitForReady(callback) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const maxAttempts = 50;
    
    const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
      attempts++;
      
      if (window.DDOptim && document.getElementById('btn-network-view')) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        callback();
      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        console.error('âŒ View toggle initialization timeout');
      }
    }, 100);
  }
  
  // Initialize when ready
  waitForReady(init);
  
  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ“Œ Initializing view toggle (3 views)...');
    
    const btnNetwork = document.getElementById('btn-network-view');
    const btnTable = document.getElementById('btn-table-view');
    const btnScenarios = document.getElementById('btn-scenarios-view');
    
    if (!btnNetwork || !btnTable) {
      console.error('âŒ View toggle buttons not found');
      return;
    }
    
    console.log('âœ… View toggle buttons found');
    
    // Attach event listeners
    btnNetwork.addEventListener('click', () => {
      console.log('ðŸ–±ï¸ Network button clicked');
      switchView('network');
    });
    
    btnTable.addEventListener('click', () => {
      console.log('ðŸ–±ï¸ Table button clicked');
      switchView('table');
    });
    
    // Scenarios button (may not exist yet)
    if (btnScenarios) {
      btnScenarios.addEventListener('click', () => {
        console.log('ðŸ–±ï¸ Scenarios button clicked');
        switchView('scenarios');
      });
      console.log('âœ… Scenarios button listener attached');
    } else {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Scenarios button not found (will be added in Phase 2)');
    }

    // Profiles button
    const btnProfilesInit = document.getElementById('btn-profiles-view');
    if (btnProfilesInit) {
      btnProfilesInit.addEventListener('click', () => {
        console.log('ðŸ–±ï¸ Profiles button clicked');
        switchView('profiles');
      });
      console.log('âœ… Profiles button listener attached');
    }
    
    console.log('âœ… Event listeners attached');
    
    // Apply initial view state from DDOptim.viewState (always network, no persistence)
    const initialView = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView || 'network';
    console.log(`ðŸ“ Initial view: ${initialView}`);
    switchView(initialView, false); // false = don't persist
    
    console.log('âœ… View toggle initialized');
  }
  
  /**
   * Switch between network, table, and scenarios views
   * @param {string} viewName - 'network' | 'table' | 'scenarios'
   * @param {boolean} persist - Whether to save to localStorage (DISABLED - always false)
   */
  function switchView(viewName, persist = false) {
    console.log(`ðŸ”„ Switching to ${viewName} view (persist: ${persist})`);
    
    // Validate view name
    if (!['network', 'table', 'scenarios', 'profiles'].includes(viewName)) {
      console.error(`âŒ Invalid view name: ${viewName}`);
      return;
    }
    
    // Update DDOptim state
    if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.viewState) {
      window.DDOptim.viewState.currentView = viewName;
      console.log(`ðŸ“Š DDOptim.viewState.currentView = ${viewName}`);
    }
    
    // Get containers
    const networkContainer = document.getElementById('network-container');
    const tableContainer = document.getElementById('table-view-container');
    const scenariosContainer = document.getElementById('scenarios-container');
    
    if (!networkContainer || !tableContainer) {
      console.error('âŒ View containers not found');
      console.log('networkContainer:', networkContainer);
      console.log('tableContainer:', tableContainer);
      return;
    }
    
    console.log('âœ… Containers found');
    
    // Hide all containers using style.display for consistency
    networkContainer.style.display = 'none';
    tableContainer.classList.add('hidden');
    if (scenariosContainer) {
      scenariosContainer.style.display = 'none';
    }
    const profilesContainer = document.getElementById('profiles-container');
    if (profilesContainer) {
      profilesContainer.style.display = 'none';
    }
    
    // Show selected container
    if (viewName === 'network') {
      networkContainer.style.display = 'block';
      console.log('ðŸ‘ï¸ Network view visible');
    } else if (viewName === 'table') {
      tableContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
      console.log('ðŸ‘ï¸ Table view visible');
    } else if (viewName === 'scenarios') {
      if (scenariosContainer) {
        scenariosContainer.style.display = 'block';
        console.log('ðŸ‘ï¸ Scenarios view visible');
      } else {
        console.warn('âš ï¸ Scenarios container not found, showing network instead');
        networkContainer.style.display = 'block';
        viewName = 'network'; // Fallback
      }
    } else if (viewName === 'profiles') {
      const pc = document.getElementById('profiles-container');
      if (pc) {
        pc.style.display = 'block';
        console.log('ðŸ‘ï¸ Profiles view visible');
      } else {
        console.warn('âš ï¸ Profiles container not found, showing network instead');
        networkContainer.style.display = 'block';
        viewName = 'network';
      }
    }
    
    // Update button states
    const btnNetwork = document.getElementById('btn-network-view');
    const btnTable = document.getElementById('btn-table-view');
    const btnScenarios = document.getElementById('btn-scenarios-view');
    const btnProfiles = document.getElementById('btn-profiles-view');

    if (btnNetwork) btnNetwork.classList.remove('active');
    if (btnTable) btnTable.classList.remove('active');
    if (btnScenarios) btnScenarios.classList.remove('active');
    if (btnProfiles) btnProfiles.classList.remove('active');

    if (viewName === 'network' && btnNetwork) {
      btnNetwork.classList.add('active');
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Network button active');
    } else if (viewName === 'table' && btnTable) {
      btnTable.classList.add('active');
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Table button active');
    } else if (viewName === 'scenarios' && btnScenarios) {
      btnScenarios.classList.add('active');
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Scenarios button active');
    } else if (viewName === 'profiles' && btnProfiles) {
      btnProfiles.classList.add('active');
      console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Profiles button active');
    }
    
    // Render active view
    if (viewName === 'table') {
      // Render table if renderTable function exists
      if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ“‹ Rendering table...');
        window.DDOptim.renderTable();
      } else {
        console.warn('âš ï¸ renderTable function not yet available');
      }
    } else if (viewName === 'network') {
      // Render network: either deferred (flag set by ModelLoader) or on-demand
      if (window.DDOptim?._networkNeedsRender && window.currentNetwork && window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
        console.log('ðŸ•¸ï¸ Rendering deferred network...');
        window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
        window.DDOptim._networkNeedsRender = false;
      } else if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderNetwork === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ•¸ï¸ Rendering network...');
        window.DDOptim.renderNetwork();
      }
    } else if (viewName === 'scenarios') {
      // Render scenarios view if function exists
      if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderScenariosView === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸ“¦ Rendering scenarios view...');
        window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
      } else {
        console.warn('âš ï¸ renderScenariosView function not yet available');
      }
    } else if (viewName === 'profiles') {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderProfilesView === 'function') {
        console.log('ðŸŽ›ï¸ Rendering profiles view...');
        window.DDOptim.renderProfilesView();
      } else {
        console.warn('âš ï¸ renderProfilesView function not yet available');
      }
    }
    
    // NOTE: View preference is NOT persisted - always start with network view on page load
    // The persist parameter is kept for API compatibility but ignored
    
    console.log(`âœ… View switch complete: ${viewName}`);
  }
  
  // Expose switchView globally for external access
  if (!window.DDOptim) {
    window.DDOptim = {};
  }
  window.DDOptim.switchView = switchView;
  
  console.log('âœ… View Toggle Handler loaded');
  
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301270 | Table Renderer [REPLACED by SCRIPT 1271] ===
// SCRIPT 1270: REPLACED by SCRIPT 1271 (Tabulator Table Integration)
// This block is intentionally empty. Do not add code here.
// renderTable() is now exposed by SCRIPT 1271.


=== BLOCK: 0301271 | Tabulator Table Integration ===
// SCRIPT 1271: Tabulator Table Integration

(function() {
  'use strict';

  console.log('ðŸ“‹ Loading Tabulator Table Integration...');

  var tableInstance = null;

  // â”€â”€â”€ Formatters â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function fmtNumber(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue();
    if (v == null || v === '') return '';
    return Number(v).toFixed(1);
  }

  function fmtCurrency(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue();
    if (v == null || v === '') return '';
    return 'â‚¬' + Math.round(v).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
  }

  function fmtType(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue() || '';
    var labels = {
      'finished_product': 'Finished', 'intermediate': 'Intermediate',
      'machined': 'Machined', 'purchased_local': 'Local', 'purchased_international': 'Intl'
    };
    return '<span class="ddopt-type-badge ddopt-type-' + v + '">' + (labels[v] || v) + '</span>';
  }

  function fmtHasBuffer(cell) {
    return cell.getValue()
      ? '<span class="ddopt-buffer-icon ddopt-has-buffer">âœ“</span>'
      : '<span class="ddopt-buffer-icon ddopt-no-buffer">âœ—</span>';
  }

  function fmtLocked(cell) {
    return cell.getValue() ? '<span class="ddopt-lock-icon">ðŸ”’</span>' : '';
  }

  function fmtProfile(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue();
    return v ? '<span class="ddopt-profile-badge">' + v + '</span>' : '';
  }

  function fmtMissing(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue();
    if (v == null || v === '') return '';
    var f = Number(v).toFixed(1);
    return Number(v) > 0 ? '<span class="ddopt-missing-warning">' + f + '</span>' : f;
  }

  function fmtExceeding(cell) {
    var v = cell.getValue();
    if (v == null || v === '') return '';
    var f = Number(v).toFixed(1);
    return Number(v) > 0 ? '<span class="ddopt-exceeding-positive">' + f + '</span>' : f;
  }

  function fmtInventoryValue(cell) {
    var d = cell.getRow().getData();
    var v = d.bufferSizing && d.bufferSizing.inventoryValue != null ? d.bufferSizing.inventoryValue : null;
    return v != null ? 'â‚¬' + Math.round(v).toLocaleString('fr-FR') : '';
  }

  function fmtToleranceMandatory(cell) {
    return cell.getValue() ? '<span title="Hard constraint for OPT">ðŸ”’ Hard</span>' : '';
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Filters â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function boolFilterFunc(headerValue, rowValue) {
    if (headerValue === '') return true;
    if (headerValue === 'true') return rowValue === true;
    if (headerValue === 'false') return rowValue === false;
    return true;
  }

  function makeSelectFilter(values) {
    return function(cell, onRendered, success) {
      var sel = document.createElement('select');
      sel.style.cssText = 'width:100%;font-size:12px;border:1px solid #ccc;background:white;';
      values.forEach(function(opt) {
        var el = document.createElement('option');
        el.value = opt.value; el.textContent = opt.label;
        sel.appendChild(el);
      });
      sel.addEventListener('change', function() { success(sel.value); });
      return sel;
    };
  }

  var TYPE_VALUES = [
    {value:'',label:'Tous'},{value:'finished_product',label:'Finished'},
    {value:'intermediate',label:'Intermediate'},{value:'machined',label:'Machined'},
    {value:'purchased_local',label:'Local'},{value:'purchased_international',label:'Intl'}
  ];
  var BOOL_VALUES = [{value:'',label:'Tous'},{value:'true',label:'Oui'},{value:'false',label:'Non'}];
  var PROFILE_VALUES = [
    {value:'',label:'Tous'},{value:'F',label:'F'},{value:'I',label:'I'},
    {value:'U',label:'U'},{value:'AL',label:'AL'},{value:'AI',label:'AI'}
  ];

  // â”€â”€â”€ Columns â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function buildColumns() {
    return [
      { title:'Location', field:'location', width:110, headerFilter:'input', visible:true },
      { title:'Product', field:'product', width:160, headerFilter:'input', visible:true },
      { title:'Description', field:'description', width:180, headerFilter:'input', visible:false },
      { title:'Type', field:'type', width:130, formatter:fmtType,
        headerFilter:makeSelectFilter(TYPE_VALUES),
        headerFilterFunc:function(hv,rv){return hv===''||rv===hv;}, visible:true },
      { title:'Lead Time', field:'leadTime', width:90, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'CLT', field:'clt', width:80, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'DLT', field:'dlt', width:80, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'Indep. ADU', field:'independentADU', width:100, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'Calc. ADU', field:'calculatedADU', width:100, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'Buffer', field:'hasBuffer', width:80, formatter:fmtHasBuffer, hozAlign:'center',
        headerFilter:makeSelectFilter(BOOL_VALUES), headerFilterFunc:boolFilterFunc, visible:true },
      { title:'Locked', field:'bufferLocked', width:75, formatter:fmtLocked, hozAlign:'center',
        headerFilter:makeSelectFilter(BOOL_VALUES), headerFilterFunc:boolFilterFunc, visible:true },
      { title:'Profile', field:'bufferProfile', width:80, formatter:fmtProfile, hozAlign:'center',
        headerFilter:makeSelectFilter(PROFILE_VALUES),
        headerFilterFunc:function(hv,rv){return hv===''||rv===hv;}, visible:true },
      { title:'Cust. Tol.', field:'customerTolerance', width:90, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:true },
      { title:'Missing Tol.', field:'missingCustomerLeadTime', width:100, formatter:fmtMissing,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>', visible:true },
      { title:'LT Exceed.', field:'ltExceeding', width:95, formatter:fmtExceeding,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>', visible:true },
