      { title:'Inv. Value', field:'inventoryValue', width:110, formatter:fmtInventoryValue,
        hozAlign:'right', visible:true },
      { title:'MOQ', field:'moq', width:80, formatter:fmtNumber,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>', visible:false },
      { title:'Unit Cost', field:'unitCost', width:100, formatter:fmtCurrency,
        hozAlign:'right', headerHozAlign:'right', headerFilter:'input', headerFilterFunc:'>=', visible:false },
      { title:'Tol. Mandatory', field:'toleranceMandatory', width:120, formatter:fmtToleranceMandatory,
        hozAlign:'center', headerFilter:makeSelectFilter(BOOL_VALUES),
        headerFilterFunc:boolFilterFunc, visible:false }
    ];
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Row click â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleRowClick(e, row) {
    var nodeId = row.getData().id;
    if (!nodeId) return;
    tableInstance.getRows().forEach(function(r) { r.getElement().classList.remove('ddopt-row-selected'); });
    row.getElement().classList.add('ddopt-row-selected');
    if (window.UIHandlers && typeof window.UIHandlers.showNodeDetails === 'function') {
      window.UIHandlers.showNodeDetails(nodeId);
    } else if (window.DDOptim && typeof window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.onNodeSelect(nodeId);
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Data helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function getCurrentNodesArray() {
    if (!window.currentNetwork || !window.currentNetwork.nodes) return [];
    return Array.from(window.currentNetwork.nodes.values());
  }

  function updateRowCount() {
    if (!tableInstance) return;
    setTimeout(function() {
      var visible = tableInstance.getDataCount('active');
      var total = tableInstance.getDataCount();
      var el = document.getElementById('ddoptim-table-row-count');
      if (el) el.textContent = visible === total ? total + ' noeuds' : visible + ' / ' + total + ' noeuds';
    }, 0);
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Init â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  // initialData is passed directly to the Tabulator constructor.
  // This avoids all tableBuilt callback timing issues.

  function initTabulator(initialData) {
    if (tableInstance) return;

    var container = document.getElementById('ddoptim-tabulator-container');
    if (!container) { console.error('âŒ #ddoptim-tabulator-container not found'); return; }
    if (typeof Tabulator === 'undefined') { console.error('âŒ Tabulator not loaded'); return; }

    var columns = buildColumns();
    applyColumnConfig(columns);

    tableInstance = new Tabulator('#ddoptim-tabulator-container', {
      data: initialData || [],
      columns: columns,
      layout: 'fitDataFill',
      movableColumns: true,
      height: 'calc(100vh - 220px)',
      placeholder: 'Aucun modele charge',
      rowClick: handleRowClick,
      columnMoved: function() { saveColumnConfig(); },
      tableBuilt: function() { updateRowCount(); },
      dataLoaded: function() { updateRowCount(); },
      dataFiltered: function(filters, rows) {
        var el = document.getElementById('ddoptim-table-row-count');
        if (el) {
          var total = tableInstance.getDataCount();
          var visible = rows.length;
          el.textContent = visible === total ? total + ' noeuds' : visible + ' / ' + total + ' noeuds';
        }
      }
    });

    console.log('âœ… Tabulator created with', (initialData || []).length, 'rows');
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Public API â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  // Called by switchView('table') â€” container is visible at this point.
  function renderTable() {
    var nodes = getCurrentNodesArray();
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ renderTable called, nodes:', nodes.length);

    if (!tableInstance) {
      // First visit: pass data directly to Tabulator constructor.
      if (typeof Tabulator !== 'undefined') {
        initTabulator(nodes);
      } else {
        window.addEventListener('tabulator-loaded', function() { initTabulator(nodes); }, { once: true });
      }
      return;
    }

    // Already initialised â€” refresh data.
    tableInstance.setData(nodes);
    updateRowCount();
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Table refreshed:', tableInstance.getDataCount(), 'rows');
  }

  // Called by ModelLoader after every model load.
  function onModelLoaded() {
    var nodes = getCurrentNodesArray();
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ onModelLoaded:', nodes.length, 'nodes, tableInstance:', !!tableInstance);
    if (!tableInstance) return; // not yet visited â€” renderTable() will pass data on first visit
    tableInstance.setData(nodes);
    updateRowCount();
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Table updated after model load:', tableInstance.getDataCount(), 'rows');
  }

  function highlightSelectedRow(nodeId) {
    if (!tableInstance) return;
    tableInstance.getRows().forEach(function(r) { r.getElement().classList.remove('ddopt-row-selected'); });
    var rows = tableInstance.getRows();
    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      if (rows[i].getData().id === nodeId) { rows[i].getElement().classList.add('ddopt-row-selected'); break; }
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Column config (localStorage) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  var LS_KEY = 'ddoptim_column_config';

  function saveColumnConfig() {
    if (!tableInstance) return;
    var config = { order: tableInstance.getColumnDefinitions().map(function(c){return c.field;}), visibility:{} };
    tableInstance.getColumns().forEach(function(col) { config.visibility[col.getField()] = col.isVisible(); });
    try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(config)); } catch(e) {}
  }

  function applyColumnConfig(columns) {
    try {
      var saved = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY);
      if (!saved) return;
      var config = JSON.parse(saved);
      if (config.visibility) {
        columns.forEach(function(col) {
          if (col.field && config.visibility.hasOwnProperty(col.field)) col.visible = config.visibility[col.field];
        });
      }
      if (config.order && config.order.length > 0) {
        var orderMap = {};
        config.order.forEach(function(f,i){orderMap[f]=i;});
        columns.sort(function(a,b){
          var ia = orderMap.hasOwnProperty(a.field)?orderMap[a.field]:999;
          var ib = orderMap.hasOwnProperty(b.field)?orderMap[b.field]:999;
          return ia-ib;
        });
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Expose â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  if (!window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim = {};
  window.DDOptim.renderTable = renderTable;
  window.DDOptim.onModelLoaded = onModelLoaded;
  window.DDOptim.highlightSelectedRow = highlightSelectedRow;
  window.DDOptim.saveColumnConfig = saveColumnConfig;
  Object.defineProperty(window.DDOptim, 'tabulatorInstance', { get: function(){ return tableInstance; } });

  console.log('âœ… Tabulator Table Integration loaded');

}());


=== BLOCK: 0301272 | Column Manager ===
// SCRIPT 1272: Column Manager
// Panneau de configuration colonnes (visibilitÃ© + ordre)

(function() {
  'use strict';

  console.log('âš™ï¸ Loading Column Manager...');

  var panelVisible = false;
  var dragSrcField = null;

  var COLUMN_LABELS = {
    location:                'Location',
    product:                 'Product',
    description:             'Description',
    type:                    'Type',
    leadTime:                'Lead Time',
    clt:                     'CLT',
    dlt:                     'DLT',
    independentADU:          'Indep. ADU',
    calculatedADU:           'Calc. ADU',
    hasBuffer:               'Buffer',
    bufferLocked:            'Locked',
    bufferProfile:           'Profile',
    customerTolerance:       'Cust. Tol.',
    missingCustomerLeadTime: 'Missing Tol.',
    ltExceeding:             'LT Exceed.',
    inventoryValue:          'Inv. Value',
    moq:                     'MOQ',
    unitCost:                'Unit Cost',
    toleranceMandatory:      'Tol. Mandatory'
  };

  // â”€â”€â”€ Inject toolbar button â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function injectToolbarButton() {
    var toolbar = document.getElementById('ddoptim-table-toolbar');
    if (!toolbar) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ #ddoptim-table-toolbar not found, cannot inject column manager button');
      return;
    }

    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.id = 'ddoptim-col-manager-btn';
    btn.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-btn';
    btn.innerHTML = 'âš™ï¸ Colonnes';
    btn.addEventListener('click', togglePanel);
    toolbar.appendChild(btn);

    // Click outside â†’ close panel
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var panel = document.getElementById('ddoptim-col-manager-panel');
      var btn = document.getElementById('ddoptim-col-manager-btn');
      if (panel && !panel.contains(e.target) && e.target !== btn) {
        closePanel();
      }
    });

    console.log('âœ… Column manager button injected');
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Toggle panel â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function togglePanel(e) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (panelVisible) {
      closePanel();
    } else {
      openPanel();
    }
  }

  function closePanel() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('ddoptim-col-manager-panel');
    if (panel) { panel.remove(); }
    panelVisible = false;
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Build and open panel â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function openPanel() {
    var table = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabulatorInstance;
    if (!table) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Tabulator not ready');
      return;
    }

    closePanel();

    var columns = table.getColumns();
    var panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.id = 'ddoptim-col-manager-panel';
    panel.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-panel';

    // Header
    var header = document.createElement('div');
    header.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-header';
    header.innerHTML = '<span>Colonnes</span><button class="ddoptim-col-manager-close">Ã—</button>';
    header.querySelector('.ddoptim-col-manager-close').addEventListener('click', closePanel);
    panel.appendChild(header);

    // Column list
    var list = document.createElement('ul');
    list.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-list';
    list.id = 'ddoptim-col-manager-list';

    columns.forEach(function(col) {
      var field = col.getField();
      if (!field) return; // skip row-num columns etc.

      var item = document.createElement('li');
      item.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-item';
      item.dataset.field = field;
      item.draggable = true;

      var handle = document.createElement('span');
      handle.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-handle';
      handle.textContent = 'â˜°';

      var checkbox = document.createElement('input');
      checkbox.type = 'checkbox';
      checkbox.checked = col.isVisible();
      checkbox.id = 'ddoptim-col-chk-' + field;
      checkbox.addEventListener('change', function() {
        toggleColumnVisibility(field, checkbox.checked);
      });

      var label = document.createElement('label');
      label.htmlFor = 'ddoptim-col-chk-' + field;
      label.textContent = COLUMN_LABELS[field] || field;

      item.appendChild(handle);
      item.appendChild(checkbox);
      item.appendChild(label);

      // Drag & drop for reorder
      item.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e) {
        dragSrcField = field;
        item.classList.add('ddoptim-col-dragging');
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragend', function() {
        item.classList.remove('ddoptim-col-dragging');
        document.querySelectorAll('.ddoptim-col-manager-item').forEach(function(el) {
          el.classList.remove('ddoptim-col-drag-over');
        });
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragover', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
        item.classList.add('ddoptim-col-drag-over');
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragleave', function() {
        item.classList.remove('ddoptim-col-drag-over');
      });
      item.addEventListener('drop', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        item.classList.remove('ddoptim-col-drag-over');
        if (dragSrcField && dragSrcField !== field) {
          applyColumnReorder(dragSrcField, field);
        }
      });

      list.appendChild(item);
    });

    panel.appendChild(list);

    // Footer: Reset button
    var footer = document.createElement('div');
    footer.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-footer';
    var resetBtn = document.createElement('button');
    resetBtn.className = 'ddoptim-col-manager-reset';
    resetBtn.textContent = 'RÃ©initialiser';
    resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetColumnConfig);
    footer.appendChild(resetBtn);
    panel.appendChild(footer);

    // Position below button
    var btnEl = document.getElementById('ddoptim-col-manager-btn');
    var rect = btnEl ? btnEl.getBoundingClientRect() : { left: 0, bottom: 0 };
    panel.style.top = (rect.bottom + window.scrollY + 4) + 'px';
    panel.style.left = (rect.left + window.scrollX) + 'px';

    document.body.appendChild(panel);
    panelVisible = true;
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Toggle column visibility â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function toggleColumnVisibility(field, visible) {
    var table = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabulatorInstance;
    if (!table) return;
    var col = table.getColumn(field);
    if (!col) return;
    if (visible) {
      col.show();
    } else {
      col.hide();
    }
    if (window.DDOptim && typeof window.DDOptim.saveColumnConfig === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.saveColumnConfig();
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Apply column reorder via drag & drop â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function applyColumnReorder(srcField, targetField) {
    var table = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabulatorInstance;
    if (!table) return;
    table.moveColumn(srcField, targetField, false); // false = insert before target
    if (window.DDOptim && typeof window.DDOptim.saveColumnConfig === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.saveColumnConfig();
    }
    // Rebuild panel to reflect new order
    openPanel();
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Reset to default config â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function resetColumnConfig() {
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem('ddoptim_column_config');
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ Could not clear localStorage:', e);
    }
    closePanel();
    // Re-init table with default config
    var table = window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.tabulatorInstance;
    if (table) {
      table.destroy();
      window.DDOptim._tabulatorInstance = null;
    }
    if (typeof window.DDOptim.renderTable === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.renderTable();
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€ Init on view switch to table â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  // Inject button once DOM is ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', injectToolbarButton);
  } else {
    injectToolbarButton();
  }

  console.log('âœ… Column Manager loaded');

}());


=== BLOCK: 0301280 | Column Filter Dropdown Controller ===
/**
 * Column Filter Dropdown Controller
 * Excel-style filtering UI
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    // Get filter engine
    function waitForFilterEngine() {
        if (window.DDOptim?.filterEngine) {
            initColumnFilterDropdown();
        } else {
            setTimeout(waitForFilterEngine, 50);
        }
    }
    
    function initColumnFilterDropdown() {
        const dropdown = document.getElementById('column-filter-dropdown');
        const title = document.getElementById('filter-dropdown-title');
        const content = document.getElementById('filter-dropdown-content');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('filter-clear-btn');
        const applyBtn = document.getElementById('filter-apply-btn');
        
        if (!dropdown || !title || !content || !clearBtn || !applyBtn) {
            console.warn('[Column Filter Dropdown] Required elements not found');
            return;
        }
        
        // State
        let currentColumn = null;
        let currentFilter = null;
        
        // Create namespace
        window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
        window.DDOptim.columnFilter = {
            show: showDropdown,
            hide: hideDropdown,
            getCurrentColumn: () => currentColumn,
            getCurrentFilter: () => currentFilter
        };
        
        /**
         * Show dropdown for a specific column
         */
        function showDropdown(column, iconElement) {
            currentColumn = column;
            
            // Find existing filter for this column
            const existingFilter = window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.find(
                f => f.column === column
            );
            currentFilter = existingFilter || null;
            
            // Update title
            title.textContent = `Filter: ${formatColumnName(column)}`;
            
            // Generate content based on column type
            const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(column);
            content.innerHTML = generateDropdownContent(column, columnType, currentFilter);
            
            // Attach input listeners
            attachInputListeners();
            
            // Position dropdown under the icon
            positionDropdown(iconElement);
            
            // Show dropdown
            dropdown.classList.add('show');
            
            // Update buttons
            updateButtons();
        }
        
        /**
         * Hide dropdown
         */
        function hideDropdown() {
            dropdown.classList.remove('show');
            currentColumn = null;
            currentFilter = null;
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate dropdown content based on column type
         */
        function generateDropdownContent(column, columnType, existingFilter) {
            let html = '';
            
            if (columnType === 'text') {
                html = generateTextFilter(column, existingFilter);
            } else if (columnType === 'enum') {
                html = generateEnumFilter(column, existingFilter);
            } else if (columnType === 'number') {
                html = generateNumberFilter(column, existingFilter);
            } else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
                html = generateBooleanFilter(column, existingFilter);
            }
            
            return html;
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate text filter (contains)
         */
        function generateTextFilter(column, existingFilter) {
            const value = existingFilter?.value || '';
            return `
                <div class="filter-dropdown-row">
                    <label class="filter-dropdown-label">Contains text:</label>
                    <input 
                        type="text" 
                        id="filter-input-value" 
                        class="filter-dropdown-input"
                        placeholder="Enter search text..."
                        value="${escapeHtml(value)}"
                    />
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate enum filter (multi-select checkboxes)
         */
        function generateEnumFilter(column, existingFilter) {
            const options = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getEnumOptions(column);
            const selectedValues = existingFilter?.value || [];
            
            let html = '<div class="filter-dropdown-row">';
            html += '<label class="filter-dropdown-label">Select values:</label>';
            html += '<div class="filter-checkbox-list">';
            
            options.forEach(option => {
                const checked = selectedValues.includes(option) ? 'checked' : '';
                const displayName = formatEnumValue(column, option);
                html += `
                    <div class="filter-checkbox-item">
                        <input 
                            type="checkbox" 
                            id="filter-enum-${option}" 
                            value="${option}"
                            ${checked}
                        />
                        <label for="filter-enum-${option}">${displayName}</label>
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate number filter (range)
         */
        function generateNumberFilter(column, existingFilter) {
            const min = existingFilter?.value?.min ?? '';
            const max = existingFilter?.value?.max ?? '';
            
            return `
                <div class="filter-dropdown-row">
                    <label class="filter-dropdown-label">Range:</label>
                    <div class="filter-range-inputs">
                        <input 
                            type="number" 
                            id="filter-range-min" 
                            class="filter-dropdown-input"
                            placeholder="Min"
                            value="${min}"
                        />
                        <span class="filter-range-separator">to</span>
                        <input 
                            type="number" 
                            id="filter-range-max" 
                            class="filter-dropdown-input"
                            placeholder="Max"
                            value="${max}"
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate boolean filter (true/false/any)
         */
        function generateBooleanFilter(column, existingFilter) {
            const currentValue = existingFilter?.value;
            const selectedTrue = currentValue === true ? 'selected' : '';
            const selectedFalse = currentValue === false ? 'selected' : '';
            const selectedAny = currentValue === null ? 'selected' : '';
            
            return `
                <div class="filter-dropdown-row">
                    <label class="filter-dropdown-label">Value:</label>
                    <select id="filter-boolean-value" class="filter-dropdown-select">
                        <option value="any" ${selectedAny}>Any</option>
                        <option value="true" ${selectedTrue}>Yes / True</option>
                        <option value="false" ${selectedFalse}>No / False</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        /**
         * Position dropdown under filter icon
         */
        function positionDropdown(iconElement) {
            const iconRect = iconElement.getBoundingClientRect();
            const tableContainer = document.getElementById('table-view-container');
            const containerRect = tableContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
            
            // Position relative to table container
            dropdown.style.position = 'absolute';
            dropdown.style.left = `${iconRect.left - containerRect.left}px`;
            dropdown.style.top = `${iconRect.bottom - containerRect.top + 4}px`;
            
            // Adjust if dropdown would go off-screen
            setTimeout(() => {
                const dropdownRect = dropdown.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (dropdownRect.right > window.innerWidth - 20) {
                    const offset = dropdownRect.right - window.innerWidth + 20;
                    dropdown.style.left = `${parseFloat(dropdown.style.left) - offset}px`;
                }
            }, 0);
        }
        
        /**
         * Attach input listeners for validation
         */
        function attachInputListeners() {
            const inputs = content.querySelectorAll('input, select');
            inputs.forEach(input => {
                input.addEventListener('input', updateButtons);
                input.addEventListener('change', updateButtons);
            });
        }
        
        /**
         * Update button states
         */
        function updateButtons() {
            const hasValue = checkHasValue();
            applyBtn.disabled = !hasValue;
            clearBtn.disabled = !currentFilter; // Only enable if filter exists
        }
        
        /**
         * Check if dropdown has valid input
         */
        function checkHasValue() {
            const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(currentColumn);
            
            if (columnType === 'text') {
                const input = document.getElementById('filter-input-value');
                return input && input.value.trim().length > 0;
            }
            
            if (columnType === 'enum') {
                const checkboxes = content.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
                return checkboxes.length > 0;
            }
            
            if (columnType === 'number') {
                const minInput = document.getElementById('filter-range-min');
                const maxInput = document.getElementById('filter-range-max');
                return (minInput && minInput.value) || (maxInput && maxInput.value);
            }
            
            if (columnType === 'boolean') {
                const select = document.getElementById('filter-boolean-value');
                return select && select.value !== 'any';
            }
            
            return false;
        }
        
        /**
         * Build filter object from current inputs
         */
        function buildFilterFromInputs() {
            const columnType = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.getColumnType(currentColumn);
            let filter = { column: currentColumn };
            
            if (columnType === 'text') {
                const input = document.getElementById('filter-input-value');
                filter.operator = 'contains';
                filter.value = input.value.trim();
            }
            
            else if (columnType === 'enum') {
                const checkboxes = content.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
                const values = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
                filter.operator = 'in';
                filter.value = values;
            }
            
            else if (columnType === 'number') {
                const minInput = document.getElementById('filter-range-min');
                const maxInput = document.getElementById('filter-range-max');
                filter.operator = 'range';
                filter.value = {
                    min: minInput.value ? Number(minInput.value) : null,
                    max: maxInput.value ? Number(maxInput.value) : null
                };
            }
            
            else if (columnType === 'boolean') {
                const select = document.getElementById('filter-boolean-value');
                filter.operator = 'equals';
                filter.value = select.value === 'true' ? true : false;
            }
            
            return filter;
        }
        
        /**
         * Apply filter button handler
         */
        applyBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const filter = buildFilterFromInputs();
            
            // Validate
            const validation = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.validateFilter(filter);
            if (!validation.valid) {
                alert('Invalid filter: ' + validation.error);
                return;
            }
            
            // Remove existing filter for this column
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = 
                window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.filter(f => f.column !== currentColumn);
            
            // Add new filter
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.push(filter);
            
            // Update UI
            updateFilterIcon(currentColumn, true);
            updateActiveFiltersBar();
            
            // Re-render table
            if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
                window.DDOptim.renderTable();
            }
            
            // Close dropdown
            hideDropdown();
        });
        
        /**
         * Clear filter button handler
         */
        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            // Remove filter for this column
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = 
                window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.filter(f => f.column !== currentColumn);
            
            // Update UI
            updateFilterIcon(currentColumn, false);
            updateActiveFiltersBar();
            
            // Re-render table
            if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
                window.DDOptim.renderTable();
            }
            
            // Close dropdown
            hideDropdown();
        });
        
        /**
         * Close dropdown when clicking outside
         */
        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (!dropdown.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('.filter-icon')) {
                hideDropdown();
            }
        });
        
        /**
         * Attach click handlers to filter icons
         * Uses event delegation to avoid duplicate handlers
         */
        function attachFilterIconHandlers() {
            // Remove old handler if exists
            if (window.DDOptim.columnFilter._iconClickHandler) {
                document.removeEventListener('click', window.DDOptim.columnFilter._iconClickHandler);
            }
            
            // Create new handler with event delegation
            const iconClickHandler = function(e) {
                const filterIcon = e.target.closest('.filter-icon');
                if (!filterIcon) return;
                
                e.stopPropagation();
                const column = filterIcon.dataset.column;
                
                // Toggle: close if already open for this column
                if (dropdown.classList.contains('show') && currentColumn === column) {
                    hideDropdown();
                } else {
                    showDropdown(column, filterIcon);
                }
            };
            
            // Store handler reference for cleanup
            window.DDOptim.columnFilter._iconClickHandler = iconClickHandler;
            
            // Attach to document (event delegation)
            document.addEventListener('click', iconClickHandler);
        }
        
        /**
         * Update filter icon visual state
         */
        function updateFilterIcon(column, isActive) {
            const icon = document.querySelector(`.filter-icon[data-column="${column}"]`);
            if (icon) {
                if (isActive) {
                    icon.classList.add('active');
                } else {
                    icon.classList.remove('active');
                }
            }
        }
        
        /**
         * Update all filter icons based on active filters
         */
        function updateAllFilterIcons() {
            // Clear all active states
            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-icon').forEach(icon => {
                icon.classList.remove('active');
            });
            
            // Set active state for filtered columns (guard: tabularView may not be initialized yet)
            const activeFilters = window.DDOptim?.tabularView?.activeFilters;
            if (!activeFilters) return;
            activeFilters.forEach(filter => {
                updateFilterIcon(filter.column, true);
            });
        }
        
        /**
         * Update active filters bar
         */
        function updateActiveFiltersBar() {
            if (window.DDOptim.updateActiveFiltersBar) {
                window.DDOptim.updateActiveFiltersBar();
            }
        }
        
        // Utility functions
        function formatColumnName(column) {
            const names = {
                name: 'Name',
                type: 'Type',
                leadTime: 'Lead Time',
                clt: 'CLT',
                dlt: 'DLT',
                independentADU: 'Independent ADU',
                calculatedADU: 'Calculated ADU',
                hasBuffer: 'Has Buffer',
                bufferLocked: 'Buffer Locked',
                bufferProfile: 'Buffer Profile',
                    toleranceMandatory: 'Tolerance Mandatory',
                customerTolerance: 'Customer Tolerance',
                missingCustomerLeadTime: 'Missing Tolerance',
                ltExceeding: 'LT Exceeding',
                inventoryValue: 'Inventory Value',
                moq: 'MOQ',
                unitCost: 'Unit Cost'
            };
            return names[column] || column;
        }
        
        function formatEnumValue(column, value) {
            if (column === 'type') {
                const typeNames = {
                    finished_product: 'Finished Product',
                    intermediate: 'Intermediate',
                    machined: 'Machined',
                    purchased_local: 'Purchased Local',
                    purchased_international: 'Purchased International'
                };
                return typeNames[value] || value;
            }
            return value;
        }
        
        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }
        
        // Export for external use
        window.DDOptim.columnFilter.attachFilterIconHandlers = attachFilterIconHandlers;
        window.DDOptim.columnFilter.updateAllFilterIcons = updateAllFilterIcons;
    }
    
    // Start initialization
    waitForFilterEngine();
    
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301285 | Active Filters Bar Manager ===
/**
 * Active Filters Bar Manager
 * Displays and manages active filter badges
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Active Filters Bar] Loading...');
    
    // Wait for dependencies
    function waitForDependencies() {
        if (window.DDOptim?.tabularView && window.DDOptim?.filterEngine) {
            initActiveFiltersBar();
        } else {
            setTimeout(waitForDependencies, 50);
        }
    }
    
    function initActiveFiltersBar() {
        const bar = document.getElementById('active-filters-bar');
        const badgesContainer = document.getElementById('active-filter-badges');
        const clearAllBtn = document.getElementById('clear-all-filters-btn');
        const savePresetBtn = document.getElementById('save-preset-btn');
        
        if (!bar || !badgesContainer || !clearAllBtn || !savePresetBtn) {
            console.warn('[Active Filters Bar] Required elements not found');
            return;
        }
        
        /**
         * Update the active filters bar display
         */
        function updateActiveFiltersBar() {
            const filters = window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters;
            const hasFilters = filters && filters.length > 0;
            
            // Show/hide bar
            if (hasFilters) {
                bar.classList.remove('empty');
            } else {
                bar.classList.add('empty');
            }
            
            // Enable/disable buttons
            clearAllBtn.disabled = !hasFilters;
            savePresetBtn.disabled = !hasFilters;
            
            // Render badges
            renderFilterBadges(filters);
            
            console.log('[Active Filters Bar] Updated:', filters.length, 'filters');
        }
        
        /**
         * Render filter badges
         */
        function renderFilterBadges(filters) {
            if (!filters || filters.length === 0) {
                badgesContainer.innerHTML = '';
                return;
            }
            
            const badges = filters.map(filter => createFilterBadge(filter)).join('');
            badgesContainer.innerHTML = badges;
            
            // Attach click handlers to badges
            attachBadgeHandlers();
        }
        
        /**
         * Create HTML for a single filter badge
         */
        function createFilterBadge(filter) {
            const columnName = formatColumnName(filter.column);
            const valueText = formatFilterValue(filter);
            
            return `
                <div class="filter-badge" data-column="${filter.column}">
                    <span class="filter-badge-column">${columnName}:</span>
                    <span class="filter-badge-value">${valueText}</span>
                    <span class="filter-badge-remove" title="Remove this filter">Ã—</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        /**
         * Format filter value for display
         */
        function formatFilterValue(filter) {
            const { operator, value } = filter;
            
            if (operator === 'contains') {
                return `contains "${value}"`;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'equals') {
                if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
                    return value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
                }
                return `= ${value}`;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'in') {
                if (Array.isArray(value)) {
                    const formatted = value.map(v => formatEnumValue(filter.column, v));
                    if (formatted.length <= 2) {
                        return formatted.join(', ');
                    }
                    return `${formatted.slice(0, 2).join(', ')}, +${formatted.length - 2} more`;
                }
                return value;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'range') {
                if (value.min !== null && value.max !== null) {
                    return `${value.min} - ${value.max}`;
                } else if (value.min !== null) {
                    return `â‰¥ ${value.min}`;
                } else if (value.max !== null) {
                    return `â‰¤ ${value.max}`;
                }
                return 'range';
            }
            
            return String(value);
        }
        
        /**
         * Attach click handlers to badges
         */
        function attachBadgeHandlers() {
            // Click badge to edit filter
            const badges = badgesContainer.querySelectorAll('.filter-badge');
            badges.forEach(badge => {
                // Click badge body to edit
                badge.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    if (e.target.classList.contains('filter-badge-remove')) {
                        return; // Let remove handler handle it
                    }
                    
                    const column = this.dataset.column;
                    const icon = document.querySelector(`.filter-icon[data-column="${column}"]`);
                    
                    if (icon && window.DDOptim.columnFilter) {
                        window.DDOptim.columnFilter.show(column, icon);
                    }
                });
                
                // Click X to remove
                const removeBtn = badge.querySelector('.filter-badge-remove');
                if (removeBtn) {
                    removeBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        const column = badge.dataset.column;
                        removeFilter(column);
                    });
                }
            });
        }
        
        /**
         * Remove a specific filter
         */
        function removeFilter(column) {
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = 
                window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters.filter(f => f.column !== column);
            
            console.log('[Active Filters Bar] Removed filter for:', column);
            
            // Update filter icon
            const icon = document.querySelector(`.filter-icon[data-column="${column}"]`);
            if (icon) {
                icon.classList.remove('active');
            }
            
            // Update UI
            updateActiveFiltersBar();
            
            // Re-render table
            if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
                window.DDOptim.renderTable();
            }
        }
        
        /**
         * Clear all filters
         */
        function clearAllFilters() {
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = [];
            
            console.log('[Active Filters Bar] Cleared all filters');
            
            // Clear all filter icon states
            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-icon').forEach(icon => {
                icon.classList.remove('active');
            });
            
            // Update UI
            updateActiveFiltersBar();
            
            // Re-render table
            if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
                window.DDOptim.renderTable();
            }
        }
        
        // Attach Clear All button handler
        clearAllBtn.addEventListener('click', clearAllFilters);
        
        // Utility functions
        function formatColumnName(column) {
            const names = {
                name: 'Name',
                type: 'Type',
                leadTime: 'Lead Time',
                clt: 'CLT',
                dlt: 'DLT',
                independentADU: 'Independent ADU',
                calculatedADU: 'Calculated ADU',
                hasBuffer: 'Has Buffer',
                bufferLocked: 'Buffer Locked',
                bufferProfile: 'Buffer Profile',
                    toleranceMandatory: 'Tolerance Mandatory',
                customerTolerance: 'Customer Tolerance',
                missingCustomerLeadTime: 'Missing Tolerance',
                ltExceeding: 'LT Exceeding',
                inventoryValue: 'Inventory Value',
                moq: 'MOQ',
                unitCost: 'Unit Cost'
            };
            return names[column] || column;
        }
        
        function formatEnumValue(column, value) {
            if (column === 'type') {
                const typeNames = {
                    finished_product: 'Finished Product',
                    intermediate: 'Intermediate',
                    machined: 'Machined',
                    purchased_local: 'Purchased Local',
                    purchased_international: 'Purchased International'
                };
                return typeNames[value] || value;
            }
            return value;
        }
        
        // Export to global namespace
        window.DDOptim.updateActiveFiltersBar = updateActiveFiltersBar;
        window.DDOptim.clearAllFilters = clearAllFilters;
        
        // Initialize display
        updateActiveFiltersBar();
        
        console.log('[Active Filters Bar] âœ“ Initialized');
    }
    
    // Start initialization
    waitForDependencies();
    
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301290 | Filter Preset Manager ===
/**
 * Filter Preset Manager
 * CRUD operations for named filter presets
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Filter Preset Manager] Loading...');
    
    // Wait for dependencies
    function waitForDependencies() {
        if (window.DDOptim?.tabularView && window.currentNetwork) {
            initPresetManager();
        } else {
            setTimeout(waitForDependencies, 50);
        }
    }
    
    function initPresetManager() {
        const saveBtn = document.getElementById('save-preset-btn');
        const loadBtn = document.getElementById('load-preset-btn');
        const modalOverlay = document.getElementById('preset-modal-overlay');
        const modalTitle = document.getElementById('preset-modal-title');
        const modalBody = document.getElementById('preset-modal-body');
        const modalCancel = document.getElementById('preset-modal-cancel');
        const modalConfirm = document.getElementById('preset-modal-confirm');
        
        if (!saveBtn || !loadBtn || !modalOverlay || !modalTitle || !modalBody || !modalCancel || !modalConfirm) {
            console.warn('[Filter Preset Manager] Required elements not found');
            return;
        }
        
        // Ensure filterPresets array exists in model
        if (!window.currentNetwork.filterPresets) {
            window.currentNetwork.filterPresets = [];
        }
        
        // Modal state
        let modalMode = null; // 'save' or 'load'
        let selectedPresetId = null;
        
        /**
         * Open Save Preset modal
         */
        function openSaveModal() {
            modalMode = 'save';
            modalTitle.textContent = 'Save Filter Preset';
            
            const html = `
                <div class="preset-form-group">
                    <label class="preset-form-label">Preset Name *</label>
                    <input 
                        type="text" 
                        id="preset-name-input" 
                        class="preset-form-input"
                        placeholder="Enter a descriptive name..."
                        maxlength="50"
                    />
                </div>
                <div class="preset-form-group">
                    <label class="preset-form-label">Description (optional)</label>
                    <textarea 
                        id="preset-description-input" 
                        class="preset-form-textarea"
                        placeholder="Add notes about this filter combination..."
                        maxlength="200"
                    ></textarea>
                </div>
                <div class="preset-form-group">
                    <label class="preset-form-label">Filters to save:</label>
                    <div class="preset-item-filters">
                        ${formatFiltersForDisplay(window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            modalBody.innerHTML = html;
            modalConfirm.textContent = 'Save';
            modalConfirm.disabled = true;
            
            // Attach input listener
            const nameInput = document.getElementById('preset-name-input');
            nameInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
                modalConfirm.disabled = this.value.trim().length === 0;
            });
            nameInput.focus();
            
            modalOverlay.classList.add('show');
        }
        
        /**
         * Open Load Preset modal
         */
        function openLoadModal() {
            modalMode = 'load';
            modalTitle.textContent = 'Load Filter Preset';
            selectedPresetId = null;
            
            const presets = window.currentNetwork.filterPresets || [];
            
            if (presets.length === 0) {
                modalBody.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align: center; color: #6b7280; padding: 40px 20px;">No saved presets yet. Save your first filter combination!</p>';
                modalConfirm.disabled = true;
            } else {
                const html = `
                    <div class="preset-list">
                        ${presets.map(preset => createPresetListItem(preset)).join('')}
                    </div>
                `;
                modalBody.innerHTML = html;
                attachPresetListHandlers();
                modalConfirm.disabled = true;
            }
            
            modalConfirm.textContent = 'Load';
            modalOverlay.classList.add('show');
        }
        
        /**
         * Create HTML for preset list item
         */
        function createPresetListItem(preset) {
            return `
                <div class="preset-list-item" data-preset-id="${preset.id}">
                    <div class="preset-item-header">
                        <div class="preset-item-name">${escapeHtml(preset.name)}</div>
                        <div class="preset-item-actions">
                            <button class="preset-item-btn" data-action="delete" data-preset-id="${preset.id}" title="Delete preset">ðŸ—‘ï¸</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    ${preset.description ? `<div class="preset-item-description">${escapeHtml(preset.description)}</div>` : ''}
                    <div class="preset-item-filters">
                        ${formatFiltersForDisplay(preset.filters)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        /**
         * Attach handlers to preset list items
         */
        function attachPresetListHandlers() {
            const items = modalBody.querySelectorAll('.preset-list-item');
            items.forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    // Check if delete button was clicked
                    if (e.target.closest('[data-action="delete"]')) {
                        return; // Let delete handler handle it
                    }
                    
                    // Select this preset
                    items.forEach(i => i.classList.remove('selected'));
                    this.classList.add('selected');
                    selectedPresetId = this.dataset.presetId;
                    modalConfirm.disabled = false;
                });
            });
            
            // Delete button handlers
            const deleteBtns = modalBody.querySelectorAll('[data-action="delete"]');
            deleteBtns.forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const presetId = this.dataset.presetId;
                    deletePreset(presetId);
                });
            });
        }
        
        /**
         * Save current filters as preset
         */
        function savePreset() {
            const nameInput = document.getElementById('preset-name-input');
            const descInput = document.getElementById('preset-description-input');
            
            const name = nameInput.value.trim();
            const description = descInput.value.trim();
            
            if (!name) {
                alert('Please enter a preset name');
                return;
            }
            
            // Check for duplicate name
            const presets = window.currentNetwork.filterPresets || [];
            if (presets.some(p => p.name === name)) {
                alert('A preset with this name already exists. Please choose a different name.');
                return;
            }
            
            // Create preset
            const preset = {
                id: generatePresetId(),
                name: name,
                description: description || '',
                filters: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters)), // Deep copy
                createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                modifiedAt: new Date().toISOString()
            };
            
            // Add to model
            window.currentNetwork.filterPresets.push(preset);
            
            console.log('[Filter Preset Manager] Saved preset:', preset.name);
            
            // Close modal
            closeModal();
            
            // Show success feedback
            alert(`Filter preset "${name}" saved successfully!`);
        }
        
        /**
         * Load selected preset
         */
        function loadPreset() {
            if (!selectedPresetId) return;
            
            const preset = window.currentNetwork.filterPresets.find(p => p.id === selectedPresetId);
            if (!preset) {
                alert('Preset not found');
                return;
            }
            
            // Replace active filters with preset filters
            window.DDOptim.tabularView.activeFilters = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(preset.filters)); // Deep copy
            
            console.log('[Filter Preset Manager] Loaded preset:', preset.name);
            
            // Update filter icons
            if (window.DDOptim.columnFilter?.updateAllFilterIcons) {
                window.DDOptim.columnFilter.updateAllFilterIcons();
            }
            
            // Update active filters bar
            if (window.DDOptim.updateActiveFiltersBar) {
                window.DDOptim.updateActiveFiltersBar();
            }
            
            // Re-render table
            if (window.DDOptim.renderTable) {
                window.DDOptim.renderTable();
            }
            
            // Close modal
            closeModal();
        }
        
        /**
         * Delete a preset
         */
        function deletePreset(presetId) {
            const preset = window.currentNetwork.filterPresets.find(p => p.id === presetId);
            if (!preset) return;
            
            if (!confirm(`Delete preset "${preset.name}"?`)) {
                return;
            }
            
            // Remove from model
            window.currentNetwork.filterPresets = window.currentNetwork.filterPresets.filter(p => p.id !== presetId);
            
            console.log('[Filter Preset Manager] Deleted preset:', preset.name);
            
            // Refresh modal if still open
            if (modalMode === 'load') {
                openLoadModal();
            }
        }
        
        /**
         * Close modal
         */
        function closeModal() {
            modalOverlay.classList.remove('show');
            modalMode = null;
            selectedPresetId = null;
        }
        
        /**
         * Format filters for display
         */
        function formatFiltersForDisplay(filters) {
            if (!filters || filters.length === 0) {
                return '<span style="color: #9ca3af;">No filters</span>';
            }
            
            return filters.map(filter => {
                const columnName = formatColumnName(filter.column);
                const valueText = formatFilterValue(filter);
                return `<span style="color: #1e40af; font-weight: 500;">${columnName}</span>: ${valueText}`;
            }).join(' â€¢ ');
        }
        
        /**
         * Format column name
         */
        function formatColumnName(column) {
            const names = {
                name: 'Name',
                type: 'Type',
                leadTime: 'Lead Time',
                clt: 'CLT',
                dlt: 'DLT',
                independentADU: 'Independent ADU',
                calculatedADU: 'Calculated ADU',
                hasBuffer: 'Has Buffer',
                bufferLocked: 'Buffer Locked',
                bufferProfile: 'Buffer Profile',
                    toleranceMandatory: 'Tolerance Mandatory',
                customerTolerance: 'Customer Tolerance',
                missingCustomerLeadTime: 'Missing Tolerance',
                ltExceeding: 'LT Exceeding',
                inventoryValue: 'Inventory Value',
                moq: 'MOQ',
                unitCost: 'Unit Cost'
            };
            return names[column] || column;
        }
        
        /**
         * Format filter value
         */
        function formatFilterValue(filter) {
            const { operator, value } = filter;
            
            if (operator === 'contains') {
                return `contains "${value}"`;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'equals') {
                if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
                    return value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
                }
                return `= ${value}`;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'in') {
                if (Array.isArray(value)) {
                    return value.join(', ');
                }
                return value;
            }
            
            if (operator === 'range') {
                if (value.min !== null && value.max !== null) {
                    return `${value.min} - ${value.max}`;
                } else if (value.min !== null) {
                    return `â‰¥ ${value.min}`;
                } else if (value.max !== null) {
                    return `â‰¤ ${value.max}`;
                }
                return 'range';
            }
            
            return String(value);
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate unique preset ID
         */
        function generatePresetId() {
            return 'preset_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
        }
        
        /**
         * Escape HTML
         */
        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }
        
        // Button handlers
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', openSaveModal);
        loadBtn.addEventListener('click', openLoadModal);
        modalCancel.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
        
        modalConfirm.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (modalMode === 'save') {
                savePreset();
            } else if (modalMode === 'load') {
                loadPreset();
            }
        });
        
        // Close modal on overlay click
        modalOverlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (e.target === modalOverlay) {
                closeModal();
            }
        });
        
        // Export to global namespace
        window.DDOptim.filterPresets = {
            openSaveModal,
            openLoadModal,
            deletePreset
        };
        
        console.log('[Filter Preset Manager] âœ“ Initialized');
    }
    
    // Start initialization
    waitForDependencies();
    
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301300 | Scenario Management Constants ===
/**
 * Scenario Management Constants
 * Defines modifiable attributes and allowed operators
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Scenario Constants] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES
     * Defines which node attributes can be modified in scenarios
     * and their validation rules
     */
    const MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES = {
        // === NUMERIC ATTRIBUTES (support =, +, -, *, /) ===
        independentADU: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'Independent ADU',
            unit: 'units/day'
        },
        leadTime: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'Lead Time',
            unit: 'days'
        },
        customerTolerance: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'Customer Tolerance',
            unit: 'days'
        },
        moq: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'MOQ',
            unit: 'units'
        },
        orderCycle: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'Order Cycle',
            unit: 'days'
        },
        unitCost: {
            type: 'number',
            min: 0,
            label: 'Unit Cost',
            unit: 'â‚¬'
        },
        
        // === BOOLEAN ATTRIBUTES (only operator =) ===
        hasBuffer: {
            type: 'boolean',
            label: 'Has Buffer'
        },
        bufferLocked: {
            type: 'boolean',
            label: 'Buffer Locked'
        },
        toleranceMandatory: {
            type: 'boolean',
            label: 'Tolerance Mandatory'
        },
        
        // === ENUM ATTRIBUTES (only operator =) ===
        bufferProfile: {
            type: 'enum',
            label: 'Buffer Profile',
            values: ['F', 'I', 'U', 'AL', 'AI']
        },
        type: {
            type: 'enum',
            label: 'Node Type',
            values: [
                'finished_product',
                'intermediate',
                'machined',
                'purchased_local',
                'purchased_international'
            ]
        },
        
        // === TEXT ATTRIBUTES (only operator =) ===
        bufferRationale: {
            type: 'text',
            label: 'Buffer Rationale',
            maxLength: 200
        }
    };
    
    /**
     * OPERATORS_BY_TYPE
     * Allowed operators for each attribute type
     */
    const OPERATORS_BY_TYPE = {
        number: ['=', '+', '-', '*', '/'],
        boolean: ['='],
        enum: ['='],
        text: ['=']
    };

    /**
     * SCENARIO_DEFAULTS
     * Default values for scenario-level attributes (not node attributes)
     * budgetMax: max inventory value budget for OPT algorithm (null = unlimited)
     */
    const SCENARIO_DEFAULTS = {
        budgetMax: null
    };
    
    /**
     * Get attribute definition
     */
    function getAttributeDefinition(attribute) {
        return MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES[attribute] || null;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if attribute is modifiable
     */
    function isModifiable(attribute) {
        return attribute in MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get allowed operators for attribute
     */
    function getAllowedOperators(attribute) {
        const attrDef = MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES[attribute];
        if (!attrDef) return [];
        return OPERATORS_BY_TYPE[attrDef.type] || [];
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate operator for attribute
     */
    function isValidOperator(attribute, operator) {
        const allowedOps = getAllowedOperators(attribute);
        return allowedOps.includes(operator);
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants = {
        MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES,
        OPERATORS_BY_TYPE,
        SCENARIO_DEFAULTS,
        getAttributeDefinition,
        isModifiable,
        getAllowedOperators,
        isValidOperator
    };
    
    console.log('[Scenario Constants] âœ“ Loaded');
    console.log('[Scenario Constants] Modifiable attributes:', Object.keys(MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES).length);
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301305 | Baseline Snapshot Management ===
/**
 * Baseline Snapshot Management
 * Capture and restore original network configuration
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Baseline Manager] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Capture baseline snapshot
     * Creates deep clone of all modifiable node attributes
     * Called once after loading model JSON
     */
    function captureBaseline() {
        console.log('[Baseline Manager] Capturing baseline snapshot...');
        
        if (!window.currentNetwork || !window.currentNetwork.nodes) {
            console.error('[Baseline Manager] No network available to capture');
            return false;
        }
        
        const snapshot = {};
        let nodeCount = 0;
        
        // Clone all modifiable attributes for each node
        for (const [nodeId, node] of window.currentNetwork.nodes.entries()) {
            snapshot[nodeId] = {
                // Numeric attributes
                independentADU: node.independentADU,
                leadTime: node.leadTime,
                customerTolerance: node.customerTolerance,
                moq: node.moq,
                orderCycle: node.orderCycle,
                unitCost: node.unitCost,
                
                // Boolean attributes
                hasBuffer: node.hasBuffer,
                bufferLocked: node.bufferLocked,
                toleranceMandatory: node.toleranceMandatory,
                
                // Enum attributes
                bufferProfile: node.bufferProfile,
                type: node.type,
                
                // Text attributes
                bufferRationale: node.bufferRationale
            };
            nodeCount++;
        }
        
        // Store in runtime state
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
            window.DDOptim.scenarios = {};
        }
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.baselineSnapshot = snapshot;
        
        console.log('[Baseline Manager] âœ“ Baseline captured:', nodeCount, 'nodes');
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Restore baseline snapshot
     * Reverts all nodes to baseline state
     * Called before applying a different scenario
     */
    function restoreBaseline() {
        console.log('[Baseline Manager] Restoring baseline...');
        
        const snapshot = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot;
        if (!snapshot) {
            console.error('[Baseline Manager] No baseline snapshot available!');
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!window.currentNetwork || !window.currentNetwork.nodes) {
            console.error('[Baseline Manager] No network available to restore');
            return false;
        }
        
        let restoredCount = 0;
        let missingCount = 0;
        
        // Restore all modifiable attributes from snapshot
        for (const [nodeId, savedAttrs] of Object.entries(snapshot)) {
            const node = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get(nodeId);
            if (node) {
                // Restore each attribute
                node.independentADU = savedAttrs.independentADU;
                node.leadTime = savedAttrs.leadTime;
                node.customerTolerance = savedAttrs.customerTolerance;
                node.moq = savedAttrs.moq;
                node.orderCycle = savedAttrs.orderCycle;
                node.unitCost = savedAttrs.unitCost;
                node.hasBuffer = savedAttrs.hasBuffer;
                node.bufferLocked = savedAttrs.bufferLocked;
                node.toleranceMandatory = savedAttrs.toleranceMandatory;
                node.bufferProfile = savedAttrs.bufferProfile;
                node.type = savedAttrs.type;
                node.bufferRationale = savedAttrs.bufferRationale;
                restoredCount++;
            } else {
                console.warn('[Baseline Manager] Node not found in network:', nodeId);
                missingCount++;
            }
        }
        
        console.log('[Baseline Manager] âœ“ Baseline restored:', restoredCount, 'nodes');
        if (missingCount > 0) {
            console.warn('[Baseline Manager] Missing nodes:', missingCount);
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if baseline snapshot exists
     */
    function hasBaselineSnapshot() {
        return !!(window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot);
    }
    
    /**
     * Get baseline snapshot info
     */
    function getBaselineInfo() {
        const snapshot = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot;
        if (!snapshot) {
            return { exists: false, nodeCount: 0 };
        }
        return {
            exists: true,
            nodeCount: Object.keys(snapshot).length
        };
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.baselineManager = {
        captureBaseline,
        restoreBaseline,
        hasBaselineSnapshot,
        getBaselineInfo
    };
    
    console.log('[Baseline Manager] âœ“ Loaded');

    // Auto-capture if a network was already loaded before this module initialised
    // (happens because SCRIPT 1200 calls loadFromLibrary before SCRIPT 1305 executes)
    if (window.currentNetwork?.nodes && !hasBaselineSnapshot()) {
        console.log('[Baseline Manager] Network already loaded - capturing baseline now...');
        const ok = captureBaseline();
        console.log(ok
            ? '[Baseline Manager] âœ… Late baseline capture successful'
            : '[Baseline Manager] âŒ Late baseline capture failed');
    }
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301310 | Node Selection Engine ===
/**
 * Node Selection Engine
 * Selects nodes based on nodeSelector criteria
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Node Selection] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Select nodes based on nodeSelector
     * @param {Map} nodesMap - Network nodes Map
     * @param {Object} nodeSelector - Selection criteria
     * @param {Array<string>} nodeSelector.nodeIds - Optional: explicit node IDs
     * @param {Array<Object>} nodeSelector.filters - Optional: filter criteria (same as table filters)
     * @returns {Array<Object>} Array of selected nodes
     */
    function selectNodes(nodesMap, nodeSelector) {
        const allNodes = Array.from(nodesMap.values());
        
        // Case 1: Empty selector â†’ ALL nodes
        if ((!nodeSelector.nodeIds || nodeSelector.nodeIds.length === 0) && 
            (!nodeSelector.filters || nodeSelector.filters.length === 0)) {
            console.log('[Node Selection] Empty selector â†’ selecting ALL nodes:', allNodes.length);
            return allNodes;
        }
        
        let selectedNodes = [];
        
        // Case 2: Explicit node IDs
        if (nodeSelector.nodeIds && nodeSelector.nodeIds.length > 0) {
            const explicitNodes = nodeSelector.nodeIds
                .map(id => nodesMap.get(id))
                .filter(Boolean);  // Remove undefined (invalid IDs)
            
            selectedNodes = [...selectedNodes, ...explicitNodes];
            console.log('[Node Selection] Selected by IDs:', explicitNodes.length, 'nodes');
            
            // Log missing IDs
            const missingIds = nodeSelector.nodeIds.filter(id => !nodesMap.has(id));
            if (missingIds.length > 0) {
                console.warn('[Node Selection] Node IDs not found:', missingIds);
            }
        }
        
        // Case 3: Filter criteria (reuse filterEngine.applyFilters)
        if (nodeSelector.filters && nodeSelector.filters.length > 0) {
            if (window.DDOptim?.filterEngine?.applyFilters) {
                const filteredNodes = window.DDOptim.filterEngine.applyFilters(
                    allNodes,
                    nodeSelector.filters
                );
                selectedNodes = [...selectedNodes, ...filteredNodes];
                console.log('[Node Selection] Selected by filters:', filteredNodes.length, 'nodes');
            } else {
                console.error('[Node Selection] Filter engine not available!');
            }
        }
        
        // Remove duplicates (Union: nodeIds OR filters)
        const uniqueNodes = [];
        const seen = new Set();
        for (const node of selectedNodes) {
            if (!seen.has(node.id)) {
                seen.add(node.id);
                uniqueNodes.push(node);
            }
        }
        
        console.log('[Node Selection] Total unique nodes selected:', uniqueNodes.length);
        return uniqueNodes;
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate nodeSelector structure
     */
    function validateNodeSelector(nodeSelector) {
        if (!nodeSelector) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'nodeSelector is required' };
        }
        
        if (typeof nodeSelector !== 'object') {
            return { valid: false, error: 'nodeSelector must be an object' };
        }
        
        // At least one of nodeIds or filters should be present (or both empty for "all")
        const hasNodeIds = nodeSelector.nodeIds && Array.isArray(nodeSelector.nodeIds);
        const hasFilters = nodeSelector.filters && Array.isArray(nodeSelector.filters);
        
        if (!hasNodeIds && !hasFilters) {
            // Empty selector is valid (selects all nodes)
            return { valid: true, message: 'Empty selector - will select all nodes' };
        }
        
        // Validate nodeIds format
        if (hasNodeIds) {
            const invalidIds = nodeSelector.nodeIds.filter(id => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim());
            if (invalidIds.length > 0) {
                return { valid: false, error: 'nodeIds must be non-empty strings' };
            }
        }
        
        // Validate filters format (basic check)
        if (hasFilters) {
            const invalidFilters = nodeSelector.filters.filter(f => 
                !f || typeof f !== 'object' || !f.column || !f.operator
            );
            if (invalidFilters.length > 0) {
                return { valid: false, error: 'Each filter must have column and operator' };
            }
        }
        
        return { valid: true };
    }
    
    /**
     * Get selection statistics
     */
    function getSelectionStats(nodesMap, nodeSelector) {
        const selectedNodes = selectNodes(nodesMap, nodeSelector);
        const allNodes = Array.from(nodesMap.values());
        
        return {
            selectedCount: selectedNodes.length,
            totalCount: allNodes.length,
            percentage: allNodes.length > 0 
                ? Math.round((selectedNodes.length / allNodes.length) * 100) 
                : 0,
            selectedIds: selectedNodes.map(n => n.id)
        };
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.nodeSelection = {
        selectNodes,
        validateNodeSelector,
        getSelectionStats
    };
    
    console.log('[Node Selection] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301320 | Modification Application Engine ===
/**
 * Modification Application Engine
 * Applies a single modification to a node
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Modification Engine] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Apply a single modification to a node
     * @param {Object} node - Node to modify
     * @param {Object} modification - Modification definition
     * @param {string} modification.attribute - Attribute to modify
     * @param {string} modification.operator - Operator (=, +, -, *, /)
     * @param {*} modification.value - Value to apply
     * @returns {boolean} Success flag
     */
    function applyModification(node, modification) {
        const { attribute, operator, value } = modification;
        
        // Validate attribute exists
        if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarioConstants?.isModifiable(attribute)) {
            console.warn('[Modification Engine] Unknown or non-modifiable attribute:', attribute);
            return false;
        }
        
        const attrDef = window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.getAttributeDefinition(attribute);
        
        // Validate operator for attribute type
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.isValidOperator(attribute, operator)) {
            console.warn('[Modification Engine] Invalid operator', operator, 'for attribute', attribute, '(type:', attrDef.type + ')');
            return false;
        }
        
        // Store original value for logging
        const originalValue = node[attribute];
        
        // Apply transformation based on operator
        try {
            switch (operator) {
                case '=':
                    node[attribute] = value;
                    break;
                    
                case '+':
                    node[attribute] = (node[attribute] || 0) + value;
                    break;
                    
                case '-':
                    node[attribute] = (node[attribute] || 0) - value;
                    break;
                    
                case '*':
                    node[attribute] = (node[attribute] || 0) * value;
                    break;
                    
                case '/':
                    if (value === 0) {
                        console.warn('[Modification Engine] Division by zero ignored for node', node.id);
                        return false;
                    }
                    node[attribute] = (node[attribute] || 0) / value;
                    break;
                    
                default:
                    console.warn('[Modification Engine] Unknown operator:', operator);
                    return false;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('[Modification Engine] Error applying modification:', error);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Apply constraints (e.g., min value)
        if (attrDef.min !== undefined && node[attribute] < attrDef.min) {
            console.warn('[Modification Engine] Value', node[attribute], 'below min', attrDef.min, 
                         'for', attribute, 'on node', node.id, '- clamping to min');
            node[attribute] = attrDef.min;
        }
        
        // Apply max length for text attributes
        if (attrDef.type === 'text' && attrDef.maxLength && typeof node[attribute] === 'string') {
            if (node[attribute].length > attrDef.maxLength) {
                console.warn('[Modification Engine] Text value exceeds maxLength', attrDef.maxLength, 
                             'for', attribute, 'on node', node.id, '- truncating');
                node[attribute] = node[attribute].substring(0, attrDef.maxLength);
            }
        }
        
        // Validate enum values
        if (attrDef.type === 'enum' && attrDef.values) {
            if (!attrDef.values.includes(node[attribute])) {
                console.warn('[Modification Engine] Invalid enum value', node[attribute], 
                             'for', attribute, 'on node', node.id, 
                             '- valid values:', attrDef.values);
                return false;
            }
        }
        
        // Log successful transformation
        console.log('[Modification Engine] Applied:', 
                    node.id, ':', attribute, operator, value, 
                    '| ', originalValue, 'â†’', node[attribute]);
        
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate modification structure
     */
    function validateModification(modification) {
        if (!modification) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Modification is required' };
        }
        
        if (!modification.attribute) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Attribute is required' };
        }
        
        if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarioConstants?.isModifiable(modification.attribute)) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Attribute is not modifiable: ' + modification.attribute };
        }
        
        if (!modification.operator) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Operator is required' };
        }
        
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.isValidOperator(modification.attribute, modification.operator)) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Invalid operator for attribute type' };
