        }
        
        if (modification.value === null || modification.value === undefined) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Value is required' };
        }
        
        return { valid: true };
    }
    
    /**
     * Preview modification result without applying
     */
    function previewModification(node, modification) {
        const { attribute, operator, value } = modification;
        const currentValue = node[attribute] || 0;
        let previewValue;
        
        switch (operator) {
            case '=':
                previewValue = value;
                break;
            case '+':
                previewValue = currentValue + value;
                break;
            case '-':
                previewValue = currentValue - value;
                break;
            case '*':
                previewValue = currentValue * value;
                break;
            case '/':
                previewValue = value !== 0 ? currentValue / value : currentValue;
                break;
            default:
                previewValue = currentValue;
        }
        
        return {
            attribute: attribute,
            currentValue: currentValue,
            operator: operator,
            operand: value,
            previewValue: previewValue,
            change: previewValue - currentValue
        };
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.modificationEngine = {
        applyModification,
        validateModification,
        previewModification
    };
    
    console.log('[Modification Engine] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301330 | Scenario Application Engine ===
/**
 * Scenario Application Engine
 * Applies all modifications from a scenario
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Scenario Application] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Apply scenario modifications to network
     * @param {Object} scenario - Scenario object with modifications array
     * @returns {Object} Result with success flag and statistics
     */
    function applyScenario(scenario) {
        console.log('[Scenario Application] Applying scenario:', scenario.name);
        console.log('[Scenario Application] Modifications count:', scenario.modifications?.length || 0);
        
        if (!scenario.modifications || scenario.modifications.length === 0) {
            console.log('[Scenario Application] No modifications to apply');
            return {
                success: true,
                modificationsApplied: 0,
                nodesModified: 0,
                errors: []
            };
        }
        
        const stats = {
            modificationsApplied: 0,
            nodesModified: 0,
            errors: []
        };
        
        const modifiedNodeIds = new Set();
        
        // Apply each modification in order (last wins on conflicts)
        for (let i = 0; i < scenario.modifications.length; i++) {
            const mod = scenario.modifications[i];
            const modNumber = i + 1;
            
            console.log('[Scenario Application] Processing modification', modNumber, ':', 
                        mod.label || mod.attribute);
            
            // Validate modification
            if (!window.DDOptim?.modificationEngine?.validateModification) {
                stats.errors.push(`Modification ${modNumber}: Modification engine not available`);
                continue;
            }
            
            const validation = window.DDOptim.modificationEngine.validateModification(mod);
            if (!validation.valid) {
                console.warn('[Scenario Application] Invalid modification', modNumber, ':', validation.error);
                stats.errors.push(`Modification ${modNumber}: ${validation.error}`);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Select target nodes
            if (!window.DDOptim?.nodeSelection?.selectNodes) {
                stats.errors.push(`Modification ${modNumber}: Node selection engine not available`);
                continue;
            }
            
            const targetNodes = window.DDOptim.nodeSelection.selectNodes(
                window.currentNetwork.nodes,
                mod.nodeSelector || {}
            );
            
            if (targetNodes.length === 0) {
                console.warn('[Scenario Application] No nodes selected for modification', modNumber);
                stats.errors.push(`Modification ${modNumber}: No nodes matched selector`);
                continue;
            }
            
            console.log('[Scenario Application] Applying to', targetNodes.length, 'node(s)');
            
            // Apply to each selected node
            let successCount = 0;
            for (const node of targetNodes) {
                const success = window.DDOptim.modificationEngine.applyModification(node, mod);
                if (success) {
                    successCount++;
                    modifiedNodeIds.add(node.id);
                }
            }
            
            if (successCount > 0) {
                stats.modificationsApplied++;
                console.log('[Scenario Application] âœ“ Modification', modNumber, 
                           'applied to', successCount, 'node(s)');
            } else {
                stats.errors.push(`Modification ${modNumber}: Failed to apply to any nodes`);
            }
        }
        
        stats.nodesModified = modifiedNodeIds.size;
        
        const hasErrors = stats.errors.length > 0;
        console.log('[Scenario Application] âœ“ Complete:', 
                   stats.modificationsApplied, '/', scenario.modifications.length, 
                   'modifications applied to', stats.nodesModified, 'unique node(s)');
        
        if (hasErrors) {
            console.warn('[Scenario Application] Errors encountered:', stats.errors.length);
        }
        
        return {
            success: !hasErrors || stats.modificationsApplied > 0,
            ...stats
        };
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate scenario structure
     */
    function validateScenario(scenario) {
        if (!scenario) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Scenario is required' };
        }
        
        if (!scenario.id) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Scenario ID is required' };
        }
        
        if (!scenario.name) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Scenario name is required' };
        }
        
        if (!scenario.modifications) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Modifications array is required' };
        }
        
        if (!Array.isArray(scenario.modifications)) {
            return { valid: false, error: 'Modifications must be an array' };
        }
        
        return { valid: true };
    }
    
    /**
     * Preview scenario application without modifying network
     */
    function previewScenario(scenario) {
        console.log('[Scenario Application] Previewing scenario:', scenario.name);
        
        const validation = validateScenario(scenario);
        if (!validation.valid) {
            return {
                valid: false,
                error: validation.error
            };
        }
        
        const preview = {
            scenarioName: scenario.name,
            modificationCount: scenario.modifications.length,
            affectedNodes: new Set(),
            modificationPreviews: []
        };
        
        // Preview each modification
        for (let i = 0; i < scenario.modifications.length; i++) {
            const mod = scenario.modifications[i];
            
            // Select target nodes
            const targetNodes = window.DDOptim?.nodeSelection?.selectNodes(
                window.currentNetwork.nodes,
                mod.nodeSelector || {}
            ) || [];
            
            targetNodes.forEach(node => preview.affectedNodes.add(node.id));
            
            preview.modificationPreviews.push({
                modificationIndex: i,
                label: mod.label || `${mod.attribute} ${mod.operator} ${mod.value}`,
                targetNodeCount: targetNodes.length,
                targetNodeIds: targetNodes.map(n => n.id)
            });
        }
        
        return {
            valid: true,
            ...preview,
            affectedNodeCount: preview.affectedNodes.size,
            affectedNodeIds: Array.from(preview.affectedNodes)
        };
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.scenarioApplication = {
        applyScenario,
        validateScenario,
        previewScenario
    };
    
    console.log('[Scenario Application] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301335 | Scenarios View Renderer ===
// SCRIPT 1335: Scenarios View Renderer (Table Format)

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('ðŸ“¦ Loading Scenarios View Renderer...');
  
  // Wait for DDOptim to be ready
  function waitForReady(callback) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const maxAttempts = 50;
    
    const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
      attempts++;
      
      if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        callback();
      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        console.error('âŒ Scenarios renderer initialization timeout');
      }
    }, 100);
  }
  
  waitForReady(init);
  
  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ“Œ Initializing scenarios view renderer...');
    
    // Expose render function globally
    window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView = renderScenariosView;
    window.DDOptim.selectScenarioInList = selectScenarioInList;
    
    console.log('âœ… Scenarios view renderer initialized');
  }
  
  // Track which scenario is highlighted in the list (independent of loaded scenario)
  let listSelectedId = null;
  
  /**
   * Main render function for scenarios view
   */
  function renderScenariosView() {
    console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Rendering scenarios view...');
    
    const scenariosList = document.getElementById('scenarios-list');
    if (!scenariosList) {
      console.error('âŒ scenarios-list element not found');
      return;
    }
    
    const scenarios = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list || [];
    const currentId = window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId;
    const selectedIds = window.DDOptim.scenarios.selectedForComparison || [];
    
    console.log(`ðŸ“Š Rendering ${scenarios.length} scenarios`);
    
    // Clear list
    scenariosList.innerHTML = '';
    
    // Show empty state if no scenarios
    if (scenarios.length === 0) {
      scenariosList.innerHTML = `
        <div class="scenarios-empty-state">
          <div class="empty-state-icon">ðŸ“¦</div>
          <div class="empty-state-text">No scenarios yet</div>
          <div class="empty-state-hint">Use "Save" button to create your first scenario</div>
        </div>
      `;
      
      // Clear comparison chart
      updateComparisonChart([]);
      return;
    }
    
    // Create table
    const table = createScenariosTable(scenarios, currentId, selectedIds);
    scenariosList.appendChild(table);
    
    // Update comparison chart with selected scenarios
    updateComparisonChart(selectedIds);
    
    console.log('âœ… Scenarios view rendered');
  }
  
  /**
   * Create scenarios table element
   */
  function createScenariosTable(scenarios, currentId, selectedIds) {
    const table = document.createElement('table');
    table.className = 'scenarios-table';
    
    // Create header
    const thead = document.createElement('thead');
    thead.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <th class="col-checkbox"></th>
        <th class="col-name">Scenario</th>
        <th class="col-metric">Inventory</th>
        <th class="col-metric">Buffers</th>
        <th class="col-metric">Missing (d)</th>
        <th class="col-metric">LT Exceed (d)</th>
        <th class="col-actions">Actions</th>
      </tr>
    `;
    table.appendChild(thead);
    
    // Create body
    const tbody = document.createElement('tbody');
    scenarios.forEach(scenario => {
      const row = createScenarioRow(scenario, currentId, selectedIds);
      tbody.appendChild(row);
    });
    table.appendChild(tbody);
    
    return table;
  }
  
  /**
   * Create a scenario table row
   */
  function createScenarioRow(scenario, currentId, selectedIds) {
    const isActive = scenario.id === currentId;
    const isSelected = selectedIds.includes(scenario.id);
    const isBaseline = scenario.isBaseline === true;
    
    const row = document.createElement('tr');
    row.className = 'scenario-row';
    if (isActive) row.classList.add('active');
    if (isSelected) row.classList.add('selected');
    row.dataset.scenarioId = scenario.id;
    
    // Format metrics
    const metrics = scenario.metrics || {};
    const inventoryValue = formatCurrency(metrics.totalInventoryValue || 0);
    const bufferCount = metrics.bufferCount || 0;
    const missingTolerance = (metrics.missingCustomerTolerance || 0).toFixed(1);
    const ltExceeding = (metrics.ltExceedingTolerance || 0).toFixed(1);
    
    // Determine icon and name display
    const icon = isBaseline ? 'ðŸ“Š' : 'ðŸ“‹';
    const nameDisplay = `${icon} ${escapeHtml(scenario.name)}`;
    const activeIndicator = isActive ? '<span class="active-indicator" title="Active scenario">â­</span>' : '';
    
    row.innerHTML = `
      <td class="col-checkbox">
        <input type="checkbox" 
               class="scenario-checkbox" 
               ${isSelected ? 'checked' : ''}
               data-scenario-id="${scenario.id}">
      </td>
      <td class="col-name">
        ${activeIndicator}
        <span class="scenario-name-text">${nameDisplay}</span>
      </td>
      <td class="col-metric">${inventoryValue}</td>
      <td class="col-metric">${bufferCount}</td>
      <td class="col-metric">${missingTolerance}</td>
      <td class="col-metric">${ltExceeding}</td>
      <td class="col-actions">
        <button class="action-icon-btn" 
                data-action="rename" 
                data-scenario-id="${scenario.id}"
                title="Rename scenario"
                ${isBaseline ? 'disabled' : ''}>
          âœï¸
        </button>
        <button class="action-icon-btn danger" 
                data-action="delete" 
                data-scenario-id="${scenario.id}"
                title="Delete scenario"
                ${isBaseline || isActive ? 'disabled' : ''}>
          ðŸ—‘ï¸
        </button>
      </td>
    `;
    
    return row;
  }
  
  /**
   * Update comparison chart title and trigger render
   */
  function updateComparisonChart(selectedIds) {
    const title = document.getElementById('comparison-title');
    if (!title) return;
    
    if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
      title.textContent = 'Select scenarios to compare';
    } else {
      title.textContent = `Comparing ${selectedIds.length} scenario${selectedIds.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    }
    
    // Trigger D3 chart render (SCRIPT 1345)
    if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderComparisonChart === 'function') {
      window.DDOptim.renderComparisonChart(selectedIds);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Format currency value
   */
  function formatCurrency(value) {
    if (value >= 1000000) {
      return 'â‚¬' + (value / 1000000).toFixed(1) + 'M';
    } else if (value >= 1000) {
      return 'â‚¬' + (value / 1000).toFixed(0) + 'k';
    } else {
      return 'â‚¬' + value.toFixed(0);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Escape HTML to prevent XSS
   */
  function escapeHtml(text) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.textContent = text;
    return div.innerHTML;
  }

  /**
   * Select a scenario in the list (highlight row + show detail panel).
   * Does NOT load it into the network â€” use scenarioLoader for that.
   */
  function selectScenarioInList(scenarioId) {
    listSelectedId = scenarioId;

    // Update row highlights
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.scenarios-table tbody tr');
    rows.forEach(row => {
      if (row.dataset.scenarioId === scenarioId) {
        row.classList.add('list-selected');
      } else {
        row.classList.remove('list-selected');
      }
    });

    // Render detail panel
    renderDetailPanel(scenarioId);
  }

  /**
   * Render the modifications detail panel for a given scenario.
   * Displays a flat table: Node | Attribute | Baseline | New Value
   */
  function renderDetailPanel(scenarioId) {
    const panel = document.getElementById('scenario-detail-panel');
    const title = document.getElementById('scenario-detail-title');
    const badge = document.getElementById('scenario-detail-badge');
    const body  = document.getElementById('scenario-detail-body');

    if (!panel || !body) return;

    const scenarios = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list || [];
    const scenario  = scenarios.find(s => s.id === scenarioId);

    if (!scenario) {
      panel.classList.add('hidden');
      return;
    }

    panel.classList.remove('hidden');
    title.textContent = escapeHtml(scenario.name) + ' â€” Modifications';

    // Baseline: special message
    if (scenario.isBaseline) {
      badge.textContent = 'Baseline';
      badge.style.background = '#d1fae5';
      badge.style.color = '#065f46';
      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="scenario-detail-baseline">
          <span class="scenario-detail-baseline-icon">ðŸ“Š</span>
          <span>This is the baseline scenario â€” the reference state of the network with no modifications.</span>
        </div>`;
      return;
    }

    const mods = scenario.modifications || [];

    if (mods.length === 0) {
      badge.textContent = '0 modifications';
      badge.style.background = '#f3f4f6';
      badge.style.color = '#6b7280';
      body.innerHTML = `<div class="scenario-detail-no-mods">No recorded modifications for this scenario.</div>`;
      return;
    }

    badge.textContent = `${mods.length} modification${mods.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    badge.style.background = '#e0e7ff';
    badge.style.color = '#4338ca';

    // Friendly attribute labels
    const attrLabels = {
      hasBuffer:         'Buffer',
      bufferProfile:     'Profile',
      independentADU:    'Independent ADU',
      leadTime:          'Lead time',
      customerTolerance: 'Customer tolerance',
      moq:               'MOQ',
      orderCycle:        'Order cycle',
      unitCost:          'Unit cost',
      bufferLocked:      'Buffer locked',
      bufferRationale:   'Rationale',
      toleranceMandatory: 'Tolerance Mandatory',
    };

    // Baseline snapshot for "from" values
    const baseline = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot || {};
    // Network nodes for display names
    const nodesMap  = window.currentNetwork?.nodes || new Map();

    let rows = '';
    mods.forEach(mod => {
      // mod.nodeSelector.nodeIds is an array (may be 1 or many after grouping)
      const nodeIds   = (mod.nodeSelector && mod.nodeSelector.nodeIds) || [];
      const attr      = mod.attribute || '';
      const attrLabel = escapeHtml(attrLabels[attr] || attr || 'â€”');
      const newVal    = formatModValue(attr, mod.value);

      if (nodeIds.length === 0) {
        // Grouped mod without nodeIds â€” show as single row
        rows += `
          <tr>
            <td class="smod-node">â€”</td>
            <td class="smod-attr">${attrLabel}</td>
            <td class="smod-from">â€”</td>
            <td class="smod-to">${newVal}</td>
          </tr>`;
      } else {
        // One row per node
        nodeIds.forEach(nodeId => {
          const nodeObj     = nodesMap.get(nodeId);
          const nodeName    = escapeHtml(nodeObj ? nodeObj.product || nodeId : nodeId);
          const baseNode    = baseline[nodeId] || {};
          const baselineVal = formatModValue(attr, baseNode[attr]);

          rows += `
            <tr>
              <td class="smod-node">${nodeName}</td>
              <td class="smod-attr">${attrLabel}</td>
              <td class="smod-from">${baselineVal}</td>
              <td class="smod-to">${newVal}</td>
            </tr>`;
        });
      }
    });

    body.innerHTML = `
      <table class="smod-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th class="smod-th">Node</th>
            <th class="smod-th">Attribute</th>
            <th class="smod-th">Baseline</th>
            <th class="smod-th">New value</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
      </table>`;
  }

  /**
   * Format a modification value for display.
   */
  function formatModValue(attr, value) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) return 'â€”';
    if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
    if (attr === 'unitCost') return 'â‚¬' + Number(value).toFixed(2);
    if (typeof value === 'number') return Number(value).toFixed(value % 1 !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
    return escapeHtml(String(value));
  }

  /**
   * Attach row-click listeners on the scenarios table.
   * Called after table is rendered. Ignores clicks on checkbox and action buttons.
   */
  function attachRowClickListeners() {
    const tbody = document.querySelector('.scenarios-table tbody');
    if (!tbody) return;

    tbody.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      // Ignore checkbox and button clicks
      if (e.target.closest('input[type="checkbox"]') || e.target.closest('button')) return;

      const row = e.target.closest('tr');
      if (!row || !row.dataset.scenarioId) return;

      selectScenarioInList(row.dataset.scenarioId);
    });
  }

  // Patch renderScenariosView to attach listeners after render
  const _originalRender = renderScenariosView;
  renderScenariosView = function() {
    _originalRender();
    // Attach click listeners after DOM is updated
    setTimeout(attachRowClickListeners, 0);
    // Auto-select first scenario if none selected
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (!listSelectedId) {
        const scenarios = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list || [];
        if (scenarios.length > 0) selectScenarioInList(scenarios[0].id);
      } else {
        // Re-apply highlight after re-render
        selectScenarioInList(listSelectedId);
      }
    }, 10);
  };

  console.log('âœ… Scenarios View Renderer loaded');
  
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301337 | Scenarios Split Panel - Drag Resize ===
// SCRIPT 1337: Scenarios Split Panel - Drag Resize

(function () {
  'use strict';

  const MIN_PANE_HEIGHT = 60; // px

  function initSplit() {
    const container = document.getElementById('scenarios-split-container');
    const topPane   = document.getElementById('scenarios-split-top');
    const botPane   = document.getElementById('scenarios-split-bottom');
    const divider   = document.getElementById('scenarios-split-divider');

    if (!container || !topPane || !botPane || !divider) {
      // Elements not in DOM yet - retry
      return false;
    }

    let dragging = false;
    let startY   = 0;
    let startTop = 0;

    divider.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      dragging  = true;
      startY    = e.clientY;
      startTop  = topPane.getBoundingClientRect().height;

      divider.classList.add('dragging');
      document.body.style.cursor    = 'row-resize';
      document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
      if (!dragging) return;

      const containerH = container.getBoundingClientRect().height;
      const dividerH   = divider.getBoundingClientRect().height;
      const available  = containerH - dividerH;

      const delta   = e.clientY - startY;
      let newTopH   = startTop + delta;

      // Enforce minimum heights
      newTopH = Math.max(MIN_PANE_HEIGHT, Math.min(newTopH, available - MIN_PANE_HEIGHT));

      const newBotH = available - newTopH;

      // Switch from flex percentage to explicit pixel sizes
      topPane.style.flex = '0 0 ' + newTopH + 'px';
      botPane.style.flex = '0 0 ' + newBotH + 'px';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function () {
      if (!dragging) return;
      dragging = false;
      divider.classList.remove('dragging');
      document.body.style.cursor    = '';
      document.body.style.userSelect = '';
    });

    console.log('âœ… Scenarios split drag initialized');
    return true;
  }

  // Try immediately, then retry until scenarios view is in the DOM
  if (!initSplit()) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const poll = setInterval(function () {
      attempts++;
      if (initSplit() || attempts > 100) clearInterval(poll);
    }, 200);
  }

})();


=== BLOCK: 0301340 | Load Scenario - Main Workflow ===
/**
 * Load Scenario - Main Workflow
 * Orchestrates complete scenario loading process
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Load Scenario] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Load a scenario by ID
     * Complete workflow: check unsaved â†’ restore baseline â†’ apply â†’ recalculate â†’ update UI
     * @param {string} scenarioId - Scenario ID to load
     * @param {boolean} skipUnsavedCheck - Skip unsaved changes warning (default: false)
     * @returns {boolean} Success flag
     */
    function loadScenario(scenarioId, skipUnsavedCheck = false) {
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Loading scenario:', scenarioId);
        
        // 1. Check for unsaved changes
        if (!skipUnsavedCheck && window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.hasUnsavedChanges) {
            const confirm = window.confirm(
                'You have unsaved changes to the current scenario. ' +
                'Discard changes and load another scenario?'
            );
            if (!confirm) {
                console.log('[Load Scenario] Load cancelled by user');
                return false;
            }
        }
        
        // 2. Find scenario
        if (!window.currentNetwork?.scenarios) {
            console.error('[Load Scenario] No scenarios available in network');
            alert('No scenarios available. Please load a model first.');
            return false;
        }
        
        const scenario = window.currentNetwork.scenarios.find(s => s.id === scenarioId);
        if (!scenario) {
            console.error('[Load Scenario] Scenario not found:', scenarioId);
            alert('Scenario not found: ' + scenarioId);
            return false;
        }
        
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Found scenario:', scenario.name);
        
        // 3. Restore baseline FIRST
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Step 1: Restoring baseline...');
        if (!window.DDOptim?.baselineManager?.restoreBaseline) {
            console.error('[Load Scenario] Baseline manager not available');
            alert('Cannot load scenario: Baseline manager not initialized.');
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline()) {
            alert('Failed to restore baseline. Cannot load scenario.');
            return false;
        }
        
        // 4. Apply scenario modifications (if not baseline)
        if (scenarioId !== 'baseline' && scenario.modifications && scenario.modifications.length > 0) {
            console.log('[Load Scenario] Step 2: Applying modifications...');
            
            if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarioApplication?.applyScenario) {
                console.error('[Load Scenario] Scenario application engine not available');
                alert('Cannot load scenario: Application engine not initialized.');
                return false;
            }
            
            const result = window.DDOptim.scenarioApplication.applyScenario(scenario);
            
            if (!result.success) {
                console.error('[Load Scenario] Failed to apply scenario modifications');
                alert('Failed to apply scenario modifications. Errors: ' + result.errors.join(', '));
                return false;
            }
            
            console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ Applied', result.modificationsApplied, 'modifications to', 
                       result.nodesModified, 'nodes');
        } else {
            console.log('[Load Scenario] Baseline scenario - no modifications to apply');
        }
        
        // 5. Recalculate everything
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Step 3: Recalculating network...');
        if (!recalculateNetwork()) {
            console.warn('[Load Scenario] Network recalculation had issues');
        }
        
        // 6. Update state
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
            window.DDOptim.scenarios = {};
        }
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId = scenarioId;
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId = scenarioId; // Also update currentId
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
        window.currentNetwork.currentScenarioId = scenarioId;
        
        // 7. Update UI
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Step 4: Updating UI...');
        updateScenarioUI();
        
        // 8. Refresh visualization
        if (window.NetworkRenderer?.render) {
            window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork, true); // preserveView on scenario change
        }
        
        // 9. Refresh table view if active
        refreshTableView();
        
        // 10. Refresh scenarios view if active
        refreshScenariosView();
        
        console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ Scenario loaded successfully:', scenario.name);
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Recalculate all derived values
     * Same pipeline as after manual changes
     */
    function recalculateNetwork() {
        console.log('[Load Scenario] Recalculating network...');
        
        try {
            // Run full calculation pipeline
            if (window.ADUPropagation?.propagateADU) {
                window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU(window.currentNetwork.nodes, 1.0);
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] ADU propagation not available');
            }
            
            if (window.CLTCalculator?.calculateCLT) {
                window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] CLT calculator not available');
            }
            
            if (window.DLTCalculator?.calculateDLT) {
                window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] DLT calculator not available');
            }
            
            if (window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator?.calculateDeliveryLeadTime) {
                window.DeliveryLeadTimeCalculator.calculateDeliveryLeadTime(window.currentNetwork.nodes);
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] Delivery lead time calculator not available');
            }
            
            // Calculate buffer sizing for buffered nodes
            if (typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
                let bufferCount = 0;
                window.currentNetwork.nodes.forEach(node => {
                    if (node.hasBuffer) {
                        window.calculateBufferSizing(node);
                        bufferCount++;
                    }
                });
                console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ Buffer sizing calculated for', bufferCount, 'nodes');
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] Buffer sizing calculator not available');
            }
            
            // Update metrics display
            if (typeof window.updateMetricsDisplay === 'function') {
                window.updateMetricsDisplay();
            } else {
                console.warn('[Load Scenario] Metrics display updater not available');
            }
            
            console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ Network recalculated');
            return true;
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('[Load Scenario] Error during recalculation:', error);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Update scenario UI elements
     */
    function updateScenarioUI() {
        // Update scenario dropdown selection
        const scenarioSelect = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (scenarioSelect && window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId) {
            scenarioSelect.value = window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId;
        }
        
        console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ UI updated');
    }
    
    /**
     * Refresh table view if active
     */
    function refreshTableView() {
        const currentView = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView;
        if (currentView === 'table' && typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') {
            console.log('[Load Scenario] Refreshing table view...');
            window.DDOptim.renderTable();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Refresh scenarios view if active
     */
    function refreshScenariosView() {
        const currentView = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView;
        if (currentView === 'scenarios' && typeof window.DDOptim?.renderScenariosView === 'function') {
            console.log('[Load Scenario] Refreshing scenarios view...');
            window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get current scenario
     */
    function getCurrentScenario() {
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId || 
                         window.currentNetwork?.currentScenarioId;
        
        if (!currentId) return null;
        
        return window.currentNetwork?.scenarios?.find(s => s.id === currentId) || null;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if current state differs from loaded scenario
     */
    function hasUnsavedChanges() {
        // This will be implemented with the diff engine (Phase 2)
        // For now, return the flag value
        return window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.hasUnsavedChanges || false;
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.scenarioLoader = {
        loadScenario,
        recalculateNetwork,
        updateScenarioUI,
        getCurrentScenario,
        hasUnsavedChanges
    };
    
    console.log('[Load Scenario] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301345 | Comparison Bar Chart (D3) ===
// SCRIPT 1345: Comparison Bar Chart (D3)

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('ðŸ“Š Loading Comparison Bar Chart (D3)...');
  
  // Wait for D3 and DDOptim
  function waitForReady(callback) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const maxAttempts = 50;
    
    const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
      attempts++;
      
      if (window.d3 && window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarios) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        callback();
      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        console.error('âŒ Comparison chart initialization timeout');
        console.log('d3:', !!window.d3, 'DDOptim:', !!window.DDOptim);
      }
    }, 100);
  }
  
  waitForReady(init);
  
  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ“Œ Initializing comparison bar chart...');
    
    // Expose render function
    window.DDOptim.renderComparisonChart = renderComparisonChart;
    
    console.log('âœ… Comparison bar chart initialized');
  }
  
  /**
   * Render D3 bar chart comparing selected scenarios
   * @param {Array<string>} selectedIds - Array of scenario IDs to compare
   */
  function renderComparisonChart(selectedIds) {
    console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Rendering comparison chart for', selectedIds.length, 'scenarios');
    
    const container = document.getElementById('comparison-chart');
    if (!container) {
      console.error('âŒ comparison-chart element not found');
      return;
    }
    
    // Clear container
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    // Show empty state if no scenarios selected
    if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="comparison-empty-state">
          <div class="empty-state-icon">ðŸ“Š</div>
          <div class="empty-state-text">No scenarios selected</div>
          <div class="empty-state-hint">Check scenarios from the list to compare their metrics</div>
        </div>
      `;
      return;
    }
    
    // Get scenario data
    const scenarios = selectedIds
      .map(id => window.DDOptim.scenarios.list.find(s => s.id === id))
      .filter(s => s); // Remove nulls
    
    if (scenarios.length === 0) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ No valid scenarios found');
      return;
    }
    
    // Prepare data for 4 KPIs
    const data = prepareChartData(scenarios);
    
    // Render D3 chart
    renderD3Chart(container, data);
    
    console.log('âœ… Comparison chart rendered');
  }
  
  /**
   * Prepare chart data structure
   */
  function prepareChartData(scenarios) {
    return [
      {
        kpi: 'Total Inventory Value (â‚¬)',
        values: scenarios.map(s => ({
          name: s.name,
          value: s.metrics?.totalInventoryValue || 0,
          format: v => formatCurrency(v)
        }))
      },
      {
        kpi: 'Buffer Count',
        values: scenarios.map(s => ({
          name: s.name,
          value: s.metrics?.bufferCount || 0,
          format: v => v.toString()
        }))
      },
      {
        kpi: 'Missing Tolerance (days)',
        values: scenarios.map(s => ({
          name: s.name,
          value: s.metrics?.missingCustomerTolerance || 0,
          format: v => v.toFixed(1) + 'd',
          alert: v => v > 0
        }))
      },
      {
        kpi: 'LT Exceeding (days)',
        values: scenarios.map(s => ({
          name: s.name,
          value: s.metrics?.ltExceedingTolerance || 0,
          format: v => v.toFixed(1) + 'd',
          alert: v => v < 0.5
        }))
      }
    ];
  }
  
  /**
   * Render D3 horizontal bar chart
   */
  function renderD3Chart(container, data) {
    // Dimensions
    const margin = {top: 20, right: 120, bottom: 40, left: 200};
    const width = container.clientWidth - margin.left - margin.right;
    const kpiHeight = 120; // Height per KPI group
    const height = data.length * kpiHeight;
    
    // Create SVG
    const svg = d3.select(container)
      .append('svg')
      .attr('width', width + margin.left + margin.right)
      .attr('height', height + margin.top + margin.bottom)
      .append('g')
      .attr('transform', `translate(${margin.left},${margin.top})`);
    
    // Color scale for scenarios
    const colorScale = d3.scaleOrdinal()
      .domain(data[0].values.map(d => d.name))
      .range(['#3b82f6', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#ef4444', '#8b5cf6']);
    
    // Render each KPI group
    data.forEach((kpi, kpiIndex) => {
      const yOffset = kpiIndex * kpiHeight;
      
      // KPI title
      svg.append('text')
        .attr('x', -10)
        .attr('y', yOffset + 15)
        .attr('text-anchor', 'end')
        .attr('font-weight', 600)
        .attr('font-size', '14px')
        .attr('fill', '#1f2937')
        .text(kpi.kpi);
      
      // Scale for this KPI
      const maxValue = d3.max(kpi.values, d => d.value);
      const xScale = d3.scaleLinear()
        .domain([0, maxValue])
        .range([0, width]);
      
      // Bar height
      const barHeight = 30;
      const barSpacing = 8;
      
      // Render bars for each scenario
      kpi.values.forEach((val, valIndex) => {
        const barY = yOffset + 30 + (valIndex * (barHeight + barSpacing));
        
        // Bar background (light grey)
        svg.append('rect')
          .attr('x', 0)
          .attr('y', barY)
          .attr('width', width)
          .attr('height', barHeight)
          .attr('fill', '#f3f4f6')
          .attr('rx', 4);
        
        // Actual bar
        const barColor = val.alert && val.alert(val.value) ? '#ef4444' : colorScale(val.name);
        
        svg.append('rect')
          .attr('x', 0)
          .attr('y', barY)
          .attr('width', xScale(val.value))
          .attr('height', barHeight)
          .attr('fill', barColor)
          .attr('rx', 4);
        
        // Scenario name (inside bar if space, otherwise outside)
        const textX = xScale(val.value) > 100 ? 8 : xScale(val.value) + 8;
        const textColor = xScale(val.value) > 100 ? 'white' : '#1f2937';
        
        svg.append('text')
          .attr('x', textX)
          .attr('y', barY + barHeight / 2 + 5)
          .attr('font-size', '13px')
          .attr('font-weight', 600)
          .attr('fill', textColor)
          .text(val.name);
        
        // Value label (always outside on the right)
        svg.append('text')
          .attr('x', width + 10)
          .attr('y', barY + barHeight / 2 + 5)
          .attr('font-size', '14px')
          .attr('font-weight', 600)
          .attr('fill', '#1f2937')
          .text(val.format(val.value));
      });
    });
  }
  
  /**
   * Format currency value
   */
  function formatCurrency(value) {
    if (value >= 1000000) {
      return 'â‚¬' + (value / 1000000).toFixed(1) + 'M';
    } else if (value >= 1000) {
      return 'â‚¬' + (value / 1000).toFixed(0) + 'k';
    } else {
      return 'â‚¬' + value.toFixed(0);
    }
  }
  
  console.log('âœ… Comparison Bar Chart (D3) loaded');
  
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301350 | Scenario UI Handler ===
/**
 * Scenario UI Handler
 * Manages scenario selection and save functionality
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Scenario UI] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Calculate current metrics snapshot for scenario
     */
    function calculateCurrentMetrics() {
        const network = window.currentNetwork;
        if (!network || !network.nodes) {
            return {
                totalInventoryValue: 0,
                bufferCount: 0,
                missingCustomerTolerance: 0,
                ltExceedingTolerance: 0
            };
        }
        
        let bufferCount = 0;
        let totalInventoryValue = 0;
        let totalMissingCustomerTolerance = 0;
        let totalLtExceeding = 0;
        let totalAduWithTolerance = 0;
        
        const nodesIterable = network.nodes instanceof Map ? network.nodes.values() : Object.values(network.nodes);
        
        for (const node of nodesIterable) {
            if (node.hasBuffer && node.bufferSizing) {
                bufferCount++;
                totalInventoryValue += node.bufferSizing.inventoryValue || 0;
            }
            if (node.independentADU > 0 && node.missingCustomerLeadTime !== null && node.missingCustomerLeadTime !== undefined) {
                totalMissingCustomerTolerance += node.missingCustomerLeadTime;
            }
            if (node.independentADU > 0 && node.customerTolerance && node.ltExceeding !== null && node.ltExceeding !== undefined) {
                totalLtExceeding += node.ltExceeding * node.calculatedADU;
                totalAduWithTolerance += node.calculatedADU;
            }
        }
        
        const avgLtExceeding = totalAduWithTolerance > 0 ? totalLtExceeding / totalAduWithTolerance : 0;
        
        return {
            totalInventoryValue: totalInventoryValue,
            bufferCount: bufferCount,
            missingCustomerTolerance: totalMissingCustomerTolerance,
            ltExceedingTolerance: avgLtExceeding
        };
    }
    
    /**
     * Sync scenarios between currentNetwork and DDOptim.scenarios.list
     */
    function syncScenarios() {
        if (!window.currentNetwork?.scenarios) {
            window.currentNetwork = window.currentNetwork || {};
            window.currentNetwork.scenarios = [];
        }
        if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarios) {
            window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
            window.DDOptim.scenarios = {
                list: [],
                currentId: null,
                hasUnsavedChanges: false,
                selectedForComparison: []
            };
        }
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.list = window.currentNetwork.scenarios;
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Synced', window.currentNetwork.scenarios.length, 'scenarios');
    }
    
    /**
     * Populate scenario dropdown from current network
     */
    function populateScenarioDropdown() {
        const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (!dropdown) {
            console.warn('[Scenario UI] Dropdown not found');
            return;
        }
        
        syncScenarios();
        const scenarios = window.currentNetwork?.scenarios || [];
        
        if (scenarios.length === 0) {
            console.warn('[Scenario UI] No scenarios available');
            dropdown.innerHTML = '<option value="">No scenarios</option>';
            return;
        }
        
        dropdown.innerHTML = '';
        scenarios.forEach(scenario => {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = scenario.id;
            const icon = scenario.isBaseline ? 'ðŸ“Š' : 'ðŸ“‹';
            option.textContent = `${icon} ${scenario.name}`;
            dropdown.appendChild(option);
        });
        
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId || 
                         window.currentNetwork?.currentScenarioId;
        if (currentId) {
            dropdown.value = currentId;
        }
        
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Populated with', scenarios.length, 'scenario(s)');
    }
    
    /**
     * Handle scenario selection change
     */
    function handleScenarioChange(event) {
        const scenarioId = event.target.value;
        console.log('[Scenario UI] User selected scenario:', scenarioId);
        
        if (!scenarioId) {
            console.warn('[Scenario UI] No scenario ID');
            return;
        }
        
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId;
        if (scenarioId === currentId) {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] Already on this scenario');
            return;
        }
        
        if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarioLoader?.loadScenario) {
            console.error('[Scenario UI] Scenario loader not available');
            alert('Cannot load scenario: Loader not initialized');
            return;
        }
        
        const success = window.DDOptim.scenarioLoader.loadScenario(scenarioId);
        if (success) {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] Scenario loaded successfully');
        } else {
            console.error('[Scenario UI] Failed to load scenario');
            event.target.value = currentId;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Show a 3-button modal for save choice:
     *   - Update: overwrite current scenario
     *   - New: create new scenario
     *   - Cancel: do nothing
     *
     * @param {string} scenarioName - Name of the current scenario to display
     * @param {object} summary - Diff summary {modificationCount, nodesAffected}
     * @param {function} onUpdate - Called when user clicks Update
     * @param {function} onNew - Called when user clicks New
     */
    function showSaveChoiceModal(scenarioName, summary, onUpdate, onNew) {
        // Remove any existing modal
        const existing = document.getElementById('ddoptim-save-choice-modal');
        if (existing) existing.remove();
        
        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.id = 'ddoptim-save-choice-modal';
        overlay.style.cssText = [
            'position: fixed',
            'inset: 0',
            'background: rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
            'z-index: 10000',
            'display: flex',
            'align-items: center',
            'justify-content: center'
        ].join(';');
        
        overlay.innerHTML = `
            <div style="
                background: white;
                border-radius: 12px;
                box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
                padding: 28px 32px;
                max-width: 420px;
                width: 90%;
                font-family: inherit;
            ">
                <h3 style="margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 16px; color: #111827; font-weight: 700;">
                    Save Scenario
                </h3>
                <p style="margin: 0 0 6px; font-size: 14px; color: #374151;">
                    <strong>${scenarioName}</strong>
                </p>
                <p style="margin: 0 0 20px; font-size: 13px; color: #6b7280;">
                    ${summary.modificationCount} modification(s) &middot; ${summary.nodesAffected} node(s) affected
                </p>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px; justify-content: flex-end;">
                    <button id="dsc-update" style="
                        padding: 8px 18px;
                        background: #10b981;
                        border: 1px solid #10b981;
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 14px;
                        font-weight: 600;
                        color: white;
                        cursor: pointer;
                    ">Update</button>
                    <button id="dsc-new" style="
                        padding: 8px 18px;
                        background: #3b82f6;
                        border: 1px solid #3b82f6;
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 14px;
                        font-weight: 600;
                        color: white;
                        cursor: pointer;
                    ">Save as New</button>
                    <button id="dsc-cancel" style="
                        padding: 8px 18px;
                        background: white;
                        border: 1px solid #d1d5db;
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 14px;
                        font-weight: 500;
                        color: #374151;
                        cursor: pointer;
                    ">Cancel</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        
        function close() {
            overlay.remove();
        }
        
        document.getElementById('dsc-cancel').addEventListener('click', close);
        document.getElementById('dsc-update').addEventListener('click', function() {
            close();
            onUpdate();
        });
        document.getElementById('dsc-new').addEventListener('click', function() {
            close();
            onNew();
        });
        
        // Close on overlay click
        overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (e.target === overlay) close();
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Handle Save Scenario button click (Smart Button)
     * - On baseline â†’ Always create new scenario
     * - On custom scenario â†’ Show 3-button modal: Update / Save as New / Cancel
     */
    function handleSaveScenario() {
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Save Scenario clicked');
        
        if (!window.DDOptim?.diffEngine?.generateModificationsFromCurrentState) {
            console.error('[Scenario UI] Diff engine not available');
            alert('Cannot save scenario: Diff engine not initialized');
            return;
        }
        
        const modifications = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState();
        const summary = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.getDiffSummary();
        
        if (!summary.hasChanges) {
            alert('No changes detected. Current state matches the loaded scenario.');
            return;
        }
        
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId || 
                         window.currentNetwork?.currentScenarioId;
        const scenarios = window.currentNetwork?.scenarios || [];
        const currentScenario = scenarios.find(s => s.id === currentId);
        
        if (!currentScenario || currentScenario.isBaseline) {
            // Baseline â†’ always create new
            console.log('[Scenario UI] On baseline â†’ Creating new scenario');
            createNewScenario(modifications, summary);
        } else {
            // Custom scenario â†’ show 3-button modal
            console.log('[Scenario UI] On custom scenario â†’ Showing save choice modal');
            showSaveChoiceModal(
                currentScenario.name,
                summary,
                function onUpdate() {
                    updateCurrentScenario(currentScenario, modifications, summary);
                },
                function onNew() {
                    createNewScenario(modifications, summary);
                }
            );
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Update existing scenario with new modifications
     */
    function updateCurrentScenario(scenario, modifications, summary) {
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Updating scenario:', scenario.name);
        
        scenario.modifications = modifications;
        scenario.lastModified = new Date().toISOString();
        scenario.description = `Last updated on ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`;
        scenario.metrics = calculateCurrentMetrics();
        
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarios) window.DDOptim.scenarios = {};
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
        
        syncScenarios();
        refreshScenariosView();
        
        console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Scenario updated:', scenario.name);
    }
    
    /**
     * Create new scenario from current state
     */
    function createNewScenario(modifications, summary) {
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Creating new scenario');
        
        const name = prompt(
            `Save current state as NEW scenario\n\n` +
            `Detected changes:\n` +
            `- ${summary.modificationCount} modification(s)\n` +
            `- ${summary.nodesAffected} node(s) affected\n` +
            `- ${summary.attributesChanged} attribute(s) changed\n\n` +
            `Enter scenario name:`
        );
        
        if (!name || !name.trim()) {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] Save cancelled');
            return;
        }
        
        const scenarios = window.currentNetwork?.scenarios || [];
        if (scenarios.some(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === name.trim().toLowerCase())) {
            alert('A scenario with this name already exists. Please choose a different name.');
            return;
        }
        
        const metrics = calculateCurrentMetrics();
        
        const newScenario = {
            id: `scenario_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`,
            name: name.trim(),
            description: `Created from current state on ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
            isBaseline: false,
            budgetMax: null,
            modifications: modifications,
            metrics: metrics,
            created: new Date().toISOString(),
            lastModified: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        
        if (!window.currentNetwork.scenarios) window.currentNetwork.scenarios = [];
        window.currentNetwork.scenarios.push(newScenario);
        
        syncScenarios();
        populateScenarioDropdown();
        
        const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (dropdown) dropdown.value = newScenario.id;
        
        if (!window.DDOptim.scenarios) window.DDOptim.scenarios = {};
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId = newScenario.id;
        window.DDOptim.scenarios.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
        window.currentNetwork.currentScenarioId = newScenario.id;
        
        refreshScenariosView();
        
        console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Scenario created:', newScenario.name);
    }
    
    /**
     * Refresh scenarios view if it's currently visible
     */
    function refreshScenariosView() {
        const currentView = window.DDOptim?.viewState?.currentView;
        if (currentView === 'scenarios' && typeof window.DDOptim?.renderScenariosView === 'function') {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] Refreshing scenarios view...');
            window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Update UI to reflect current scenario state
     */
    function updateScenarioUI() {
        const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (!dropdown) return;
        
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId || 
                         window.currentNetwork?.currentScenarioId;
        
        if (currentId && dropdown.value !== currentId) {
            dropdown.value = currentId;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Save current scenario if there are unsaved changes.
     * Called by JSON export before serializing.
     */
    function saveCurrentIfNeeded() {
        let hasChanges = false;
        if (window.DDOptim?.diffEngine?.hasUnsavedChanges) {
            hasChanges = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.hasUnsavedChanges();
        } else {
            hasChanges = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.hasUnsavedChanges || false;
        }

        if (!hasChanges) return true;
        
        const currentId = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.currentScenarioId ||
                         window.currentNetwork?.currentScenarioId;
        const scenarios = window.currentNetwork?.scenarios || [];
        const currentScenario = scenarios.find(s => s.id === currentId);
        const scenarioName = currentScenario ? `"${currentScenario.name}"` : 'current';
        
        const action = confirm(
            `The ${scenarioName} scenario has unsaved changes.\n\n` +
            `Save before exporting?\n\n` +
            `OK = Save then export\n` +
            `Cancel = Export without saving (changes will be lost on reload)`
        );
        
        if (action) {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] saveCurrentIfNeeded: saving before export...');
            handleSaveScenario();
        } else {
            console.log('[Scenario UI] saveCurrentIfNeeded: user chose to export without saving');
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Initialize scenario UI
     */
    function initializeScenarioUI() {
        console.log('[Scenario UI] Initializing...');
        
        syncScenarios();
        populateScenarioDropdown();
        
        const dropdown = document.getElementById('scenario-select');
        if (dropdown) {
            dropdown.addEventListener('change', handleScenarioChange);
            console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Dropdown listener attached');
        } else {
            console.warn('[Scenario UI] Dropdown not found, will retry');
        }
        
        const saveBtn = document.getElementById('save-scenario-btn');
        if (saveBtn) {
            saveBtn.addEventListener('click', handleSaveScenario);
            console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Save button listener attached');
        } else {
            console.warn('[Scenario UI] Save button not found, will retry');
        }
        
        console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Initialized');
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.scenarioUI = {
        populateScenarioDropdown,
        updateScenarioUI,
        initializeScenarioUI,
        syncScenarios,
        calculateCurrentMetrics,
        saveCurrentIfNeeded
    };
    
    // Auto-initialize when DOM is ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            setTimeout(initializeScenarioUI, 500);
        });
    } else {
        setTimeout(initializeScenarioUI, 500);
    }
    
    console.log('[Scenario UI] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301355 | Scenarios Event Handlers ===
// SCRIPT 1355: Scenarios Event Handlers

(function() {
  'use strict';
  
  console.log('ðŸŽ¯ Loading Scenarios Event Handlers...');
  
  // Wait for DDOptim to be ready
  function waitForReady(callback) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const maxAttempts = 50;
    
    const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
      attempts++;
      
      if (window.DDOptim && window.DDOptim.scenarios && window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        callback();
      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
        clearInterval(checkReady);
        console.error('âŒ Scenarios event handlers initialization timeout');
      }
    }, 100);
  }
  
  waitForReady(init);
  
  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ“Œ Initializing scenarios event handlers...');
    
    // Attach event listeners using delegation
    attachEventListeners();
    
    console.log('âœ… Scenarios event handlers initialized');
  }
  
  /**
   * Attach event listeners using event delegation
   */
  function attachEventListeners() {
    const scenariosList = document.getElementById('scenarios-list');
    if (!scenariosList) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸ scenarios-list not found, deferring event listeners');
      return;
    }
    
    // Checkbox selection (event delegation)
    scenariosList.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.target.classList.contains('scenario-checkbox')) {
        handleCheckboxChange(e.target);
      }
    });
    
    // Action buttons (event delegation)
    scenariosList.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const btn = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
      if (!btn) return;
      
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      
      const action = btn.dataset.action;
      const scenarioId = btn.dataset.scenarioId;
      
      console.log('ðŸ‘† Action clicked:', action, 'for scenario:', scenarioId);
      
      switch (action) {
        case 'rename':
          handleRenameScenario(scenarioId);
          break;
        case 'delete':
          handleDeleteScenario(scenarioId);
          break;
        default:
          console.warn('Unknown action:', action);
      }
    });
    
    console.log('âœ… Event listeners attached to scenarios-list');
  }
  
  /**
   * Handle checkbox change (select/deselect for comparison)
   */
  function handleCheckboxChange(checkbox) {
    const scenarioId = checkbox.dataset.scenarioId;
    const isChecked = checkbox.checked;
    
    console.log(`â˜‘ï¸ Checkbox ${isChecked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'}:`, scenarioId);
    
    const selectedIds = window.DDOptim.scenarios.selectedForComparison || [];
    
    if (isChecked) {
      // Add to selection
      if (!selectedIds.includes(scenarioId)) {
        selectedIds.push(scenarioId);
      }
    } else {
      // Remove from selection
      const index = selectedIds.indexOf(scenarioId);
      if (index > -1) {
        selectedIds.splice(index, 1);
      }
    }
    
    window.DDOptim.scenarios.selectedForComparison = selectedIds;
    
    // Re-render view
    window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
  }
  
  /**
   * Handle Rename scenario action
   */
  function handleRenameScenario(scenarioId) {
    console.log('âœï¸ Renaming scenario:', scenarioId);
    
    const scenario = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list.find(s => s.id === scenarioId);
    if (!scenario) {
      alert('Scenario not found');
      return;
    }
    
    const newName = prompt('Enter new scenario name:', scenario.name);
    if (!newName || newName.trim() === '') {
      console.log('Rename cancelled');
      return;
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate names
    const exists = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list.some(s => 
      s.id !== scenarioId && s.name === newName.trim()
    );
    
    if (exists) {
      alert('A scenario with this name already exists. Please choose a different name.');
      return;
    }
    
    // Update scenario name
    scenario.name = newName.trim();
    scenario.lastModified = new Date().toISOString();
    
    // Update in currentNetwork.scenarios as well
    if (window.currentNetwork?.scenarios) {
      const networkScenario = window.currentNetwork.scenarios.find(s => s.id === scenarioId);
      if (networkScenario) {
        networkScenario.name = newName.trim();
        networkScenario.lastModified = scenario.lastModified;
      }
    }
    
    // Update scenario dropdown if this is the current scenario
    if (scenarioId === window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId) {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim.scenarioUI?.populateScenarioDropdown === 'function') {
        window.DDOptim.scenarioUI.populateScenarioDropdown();
      }
    }
    
    // Re-render view
    window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
    
    console.log('âœ… Scenario renamed to:', newName);
  }
  
  /**
   * Handle Delete scenario action
   */
  function handleDeleteScenario(scenarioId) {
    console.log('ðŸ—‘ï¸ Deleting scenario:', scenarioId);
    
    const scenario = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list.find(s => s.id === scenarioId);
    if (!scenario) {
      alert('Scenario not found');
      return;
    }
    
    // Check if baseline
    if (scenario.isBaseline) {
      alert('Cannot delete the baseline scenario.');
      return;
    }
    
    // Check if active scenario
    if (scenarioId === window.DDOptim.scenarios.currentId) {
      alert('Cannot delete the active scenario. Please switch to a different scenario first.');
      return;
    }
    
    // Confirm deletion
    const confirmed = confirm(`Delete scenario "${scenario.name}"?\n\nThis action cannot be undone.`);
    if (!confirmed) {
      console.log('Deletion cancelled');
      return;
    }
    
    // Remove from DDOptim.scenarios.list
    window.DDOptim.scenarios.list = window.DDOptim.scenarios.list.filter(s => s.id !== scenarioId);
    
    // Remove from currentNetwork.scenarios
    if (window.currentNetwork?.scenarios) {
      window.currentNetwork.scenarios = window.currentNetwork.scenarios.filter(s => s.id !== scenarioId);
    }
    
    // Remove from selection if selected
    const selectedIds = window.DDOptim.scenarios.selectedForComparison || [];
    const index = selectedIds.indexOf(scenarioId);
    if (index > -1) {
      selectedIds.splice(index, 1);
    }
    
    // Re-render view
    window.DDOptim.renderScenariosView();
    
    console.log('âœ… Scenario deleted:', scenario.name);
  }
  
  console.log('âœ… Scenarios Event Handlers loaded');
  
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301360 | Scenario Diff Engine ===
/**
 * Scenario Diff Engine
 * Generates modifications from current state vs baseline
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    console.log('[Diff Engine] Loading...');
    
    /**
     * Generate modifications from current state vs baseline
     * Compares each node's modifiable attributes with baseline snapshot
     * @returns {Array<Object>} Array of modification objects
     */
    function generateModificationsFromCurrentState() {
        console.log('[Diff Engine] Comparing current state with baseline...');
        
        const baseline = window.DDOptim?.scenarios?.baselineSnapshot;
        if (!baseline) {
            console.error('[Diff Engine] No baseline snapshot available');
            return [];
        }
        
        const current = window.currentNetwork?.nodes;
        if (!current) {
            console.error('[Diff Engine] No current network available');
            return [];
        }
        
        const modifications = [];
        let changesDetected = 0;
        
        // Phase 1: Simple approach - operator "=" only
        // For each node, detect changes in modifiable attributes
        for (const [nodeId, currentNode] of current.entries()) {
            const baselineNode = baseline[nodeId];
            if (!baselineNode) {
                console.warn('[Diff Engine] Node not in baseline:', nodeId);
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check each modifiable attribute
            if (!window.DDOptim?.scenarioConstants?.MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES) {
                console.error('[Diff Engine] Constants not available');
                return [];
            }
            
            Object.keys(window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES).forEach(attr => {
                const baseValue = baselineNode[attr];
                const currentValue = currentNode[attr];
                
                if (!valuesEqual(baseValue, currentValue, attr)) {
                    // Value changed - create modification
                    modifications.push({
                        nodeSelector: { nodeIds: [nodeId] },
                        attribute: attr,
                        operator: '=',
                        value: currentValue,
                        label: formatModificationLabel(currentNode.product || currentNode.id, attr, currentValue)
                    });
                    changesDetected++;
                }
            });
        }
        
        console.log('[Diff Engine] Detected', changesDetected, 'changes');
        
        // Phase 1: Basic grouping - same attribute + same value
        const grouped = groupModifications(modifications);
        console.log('[Diff Engine] After grouping:', grouped.length, 'modification(s)');
        
        return grouped;
    }
    
    /**
     * Compare two values for equality
     * Handles special cases for numbers (float comparison) and null/undefined
     */
    function valuesEqual(a, b, attrType) {
        // Exact equality
        if (a === b) return true;
        
        // Both null or undefined
        if (a == null && b == null) return true;
        
        // One is null, other is not
        if (a == null || b == null) return false;
        
        // Float comparison for numbers
        if (typeof a === 'number' && typeof b === 'number') {
            return Math.abs(a - b) < 0.0001;
        }
        
        // String comparison (case-sensitive)
        if (typeof a === 'string' && typeof b === 'string') {
            return a === b;
        }
        
        // Boolean comparison
        if (typeof a === 'boolean' && typeof b === 'boolean') {
            return a === b;
        }
        
        // Default: not equal
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Group modifications with same attribute + operator + value
     * Merges multiple single-node modifications into multi-node modifications
     */
    function groupModifications(mods) {
        if (mods.length === 0) return [];
        
        // Group by attribute + operator + value
        const groups = {};
        
        mods.forEach(mod => {
            const key = `${mod.attribute}_${mod.operator}_${JSON.stringify(mod.value)}`;
            if (!groups[key]) {
                groups[key] = [];
            }
            groups[key].push(mod);
        });
        
        // Merge groups with 2+ nodes
        const result = [];
        Object.values(groups).forEach(group => {
            if (group.length === 1) {
                // Single node - keep as is
                result.push(group[0]);
            } else {
                // Multiple nodes - merge into single modification
                const nodeIds = group.map(m => m.nodeSelector.nodeIds[0]);
                const nodeNames = group.map(m => m.label.split(':')[0]).join(', ');
                
                result.push({
                    nodeSelector: { nodeIds: nodeIds },
                    attribute: group[0].attribute,
                    operator: group[0].operator,
                    value: group[0].value,
                    label: `${group[0].attribute} = ${formatValue(group[0].value, group[0].attribute)} (${nodeIds.length} nodes: ${nodeNames})`
                });
            }
        });
        
        return result;
    }
    
    /**
     * Format modification label for display
     */
    function formatModificationLabel(nodeName, attribute, value) {
        const attrDef = window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES[attribute];
        const label = attrDef?.label || attribute;
        const formattedValue = formatValue(value, attribute);
        return `${nodeName}: ${label} = ${formattedValue}`;
    }
    
    /**
     * Format value for display based on attribute type
     */
    function formatValue(value, attribute) {
        const attrDef = window.DDOptim.scenarioConstants.MODIFIABLE_ATTRIBUTES[attribute];
        
        if (value === null || value === undefined) {
            return 'null';
        }
        
        // Boolean
        if (attrDef?.type === 'boolean') {
            return value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
        }
        
        // Number with unit
        if (attrDef?.type === 'number' && attrDef.unit) {
            return `${value} ${attrDef.unit}`;
        }
        
        // Number without unit
        if (attrDef?.type === 'number') {
            return value.toString();
        }
        
        // String (truncate if long)
        if (typeof value === 'string') {
            return value.length > 50 ? value.substring(0, 47) + '...' : value;
        }
        
        return String(value);
    }
    
    /**
     * Get diff summary statistics
     */
    function getDiffSummary() {
        const modifications = generateModificationsFromCurrentState();
        
        const stats = {
            hasChanges: modifications.length > 0,
            modificationCount: modifications.length,
            attributesChanged: new Set(modifications.map(m => m.attribute)).size,
            nodesAffected: new Set(
                modifications.flatMap(m => m.nodeSelector.nodeIds || [])
            ).size
        };
        
        return stats;
    }
    
    /**
     * Preview modifications without generating them
     * Returns quick summary
     */
    function hasUnsavedChanges() {
        const summary = getDiffSummary();
        return summary.hasChanges;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get detailed change report
     */
    function getChangeReport() {
        const modifications = generateModificationsFromCurrentState();
        const summary = getDiffSummary();
        
        return {
            summary: summary,
            modifications: modifications,
            byAttribute: groupByAttribute(modifications),
            byNode: groupByNode(modifications)
        };
    }
    
    /**
     * Group modifications by attribute
     */
    function groupByAttribute(mods) {
        const groups = {};
        mods.forEach(mod => {
            if (!groups[mod.attribute]) {
                groups[mod.attribute] = [];
            }
            groups[mod.attribute].push(mod);
        });
        return groups;
    }
    
    /**
     * Group modifications by node
     */
    function groupByNode(mods) {
        const groups = {};
        mods.forEach(mod => {
            (mod.nodeSelector.nodeIds || []).forEach(nodeId => {
                if (!groups[nodeId]) {
                    groups[nodeId] = [];
                }
                groups[nodeId].push(mod);
            });
        });
        return groups;
    }
    
    // Export to global namespace
    window.DDOptim = window.DDOptim || {};
    window.DDOptim.diffEngine = {
        generateModificationsFromCurrentState,
        getDiffSummary,
        hasUnsavedChanges,
        getChangeReport,
        valuesEqual,
        formatValue
    };
    
    console.log('[Diff Engine] âœ“ Loaded');
})();


=== BLOCK: 0301380 | Buffer Profiles View Renderer ===
// SCRIPT 1380: Buffer Profiles View Renderer
// AUDITOR:LARGE_BLOCK_JUSTIFIED - Tightly coupled rendering logic for the profiles view

(function() {
  'use strict';

  console.log('ðŸŽ›ï¸ Loading Buffer Profiles View Renderer...');

  // â”€â”€ Module State â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  let selectedProfileCode = null;  // code of the profile currently shown in edit panel
  let isDirty = false;             // true when edit panel has unsaved changes

  // â”€â”€ Helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  /**
   * Returns buffer profiles in normalised format { code: { variabilityFactor, dltThresholds:{C,M}, leadTimeFactors:{short,medium,long} } }
   * Supports two sources:
   *   1. window.DDOptim.model.bufferProfiles  (JSON import or after user edits)
   *   2. window.BUFFER_PROFILES               (legacy flat format from SCRIPT 300)
   */
  function getProfiles() {
    // Prefer model-level profiles if populated
    if (window.DDOptim?.model?.bufferProfiles && Object.keys(window.DDOptim.model.bufferProfiles).length > 0) {
      return window.DDOptim.model.bufferProfiles;
    }

    // Convert legacy BUFFER_PROFILES flat format â†’ normalised format
    const raw = window.BUFFER_PROFILES;
    if (!raw) return {};

    const normalised = {};
    Object.entries(raw).forEach(([code, p]) => {
      normalised[code] = {
        variabilityFactor: p.variabilityFactor ?? 0.5,
        dltThresholds: {
          // SCRIPT 300 uses: dlt_threshold_short = short/medium boundary (C)
          //                  dlt_threshold_medium = medium/long boundary (M)
          C: p.dlt_threshold_short  ?? p.dltThresholds?.C ?? 5,
          M: p.dlt_threshold_medium ?? p.dltThresholds?.M ?? 15
        },
        leadTimeFactors: {
          short:  p.leadTimeFactor_short  ?? p.leadTimeFactors?.short  ?? 0.5,
          medium: p.leadTimeFactor_medium ?? p.leadTimeFactors?.medium ?? 0.4,
          long:   p.leadTimeFactor_long   ?? p.leadTimeFactors?.long   ?? 0.3
        }
      };
    });

    // Populate model.bufferProfiles so subsequent edits have a stable store
    if (!window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim = {};
    if (!window.DDOptim.model) window.DDOptim.model = {};
    window.DDOptim.model.bufferProfiles = normalised;

    return normalised;
  }

  /** Get a plain array of all nodes regardless of storage format (Map or Array) */
  function getAllNodes() {
    const n = window.currentNetwork?.nodes;
    if (!n) return [];
    if (n instanceof Map) return Array.from(n.values());
    if (Array.isArray(n)) return n;
    return Object.values(n);
  }

  /** Count nodes using a given profile code */
  function getUsageCount(code) {
    return getAllNodes().filter(n => n.bufferProfile === code).length;
  }

  /** Get nodes using a given profile code */
  function getUsageNodes(code) {
    return getAllNodes().filter(n => n.bufferProfile === code);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Render: Profile List â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function renderProfilesList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('profiles-list');
    if (!container) return;

    const profiles = getProfiles();
    const codes = Object.keys(profiles);

    if (codes.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 20px; text-align: center; color: #9ca3af; font-size: 13px;">No profiles defined</div>';
      return;
    }

    // Default profiles first, then custom alphabetically
    const DEFAULT_ORDER = ['F', 'I', 'U', 'AL', 'AI'];
    const sorted = [
      ...DEFAULT_ORDER.filter(c => codes.includes(c)),
      ...codes.filter(c => !DEFAULT_ORDER.includes(c)).sort()
    ];

    container.innerHTML = sorted.map(code => {
      const count = getUsageCount(code);
      const isActive = code === selectedProfileCode;
      const badgeClass = count > 0 ? 'profile-item-badge used' : 'profile-item-badge';
      return `
        <div class="profile-list-item${isActive ? ' active' : ''}" data-profile-code="${code}">
          <span class="profile-item-code">${code}</span>
          <span class="${badgeClass}" title="${count} node(s) use this profile">${count}</span>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');

    // Attach click listeners
    container.querySelectorAll('.profile-list-item').forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
