        const code = item.dataset.profileCode;
        if (code === selectedProfileCode) return;
        if (isDirty) {
          if (!confirm('You have unsaved changes. Discard them?')) return;
        }
        selectedProfileCode = code;
        isDirty = false;
        renderProfilesList();
        renderProfileEditPanel();
      });
    });
  }

  // â”€â”€ Render: Edit Panel â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function renderProfileEditPanel() {
    const emptyPanel = document.getElementById('profiles-empty-panel');
    const editPanel = document.getElementById('profiles-edit-panel');
    if (!emptyPanel || !editPanel) return;

    if (!selectedProfileCode) {
      emptyPanel.style.display = 'flex';
      editPanel.style.display = 'none';
      return;
    }

    const profiles = getProfiles();
    const profile = profiles[selectedProfileCode];
    if (!profile) {
      emptyPanel.style.display = 'flex';
      editPanel.style.display = 'none';
      return;
    }

    emptyPanel.style.display = 'none';
    editPanel.style.display = 'flex';

    // Header
    document.getElementById('profiles-edit-title').textContent = `Profile: ${selectedProfileCode}`;
    document.getElementById('profiles-edit-subtitle').textContent = '';

    // Populate fields
    const usageCount = getUsageCount(selectedProfileCode);
    const codeField = document.getElementById('profile-field-code');
    const readonlyHint = document.getElementById('profile-code-readonly-hint');

    codeField.value = selectedProfileCode;
    codeField.readOnly = false;
    codeField.classList.remove('readonly');
    if (usageCount > 0) {
      readonlyHint.style.display = 'flex';
      readonlyHint.textContent = `â„¹ï¸ Used by ${usageCount} node${usageCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} â€” renaming will update them`;
    } else {
      readonlyHint.style.display = 'none';
    }

    document.getElementById('profile-field-vf').value = profile.variabilityFactor;
    document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-c').value = profile.dltThresholds.C;
    document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-m').value = profile.dltThresholds.M;
    document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-short').value = profile.leadTimeFactors.short;
    document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-medium').value = profile.leadTimeFactors.medium;
    document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-long').value = profile.leadTimeFactors.long;

    // Clear validation errors
    ['code', 'vf', 'dlt-c', 'dlt-m', 'ltf-short', 'ltf-medium', 'ltf-long'].forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`profile-field-${id}-error`);
      if (el) el.textContent = '';
      const input = document.getElementById(`profile-field-${id}`);
      if (input) input.classList.remove('invalid');
    });

    // Usage panel
    renderUsagePanel(selectedProfileCode);

    // Button states (clean state, no changes yet)
    setDirty(false);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Render: Usage Panel â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function renderUsagePanel(code) {
    const countEl = document.getElementById('profile-usage-count');
    const listEl = document.getElementById('profile-usage-list');
    const toggleEl = document.getElementById('profile-usage-toggle');
    const headerEl = document.getElementById('profile-usage-header');
    if (!countEl || !listEl) return;

    const nodes = getUsageNodes(code);
    countEl.textContent = nodes.length;

    if (nodes.length === 0) {
      listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 8px 0; font-size: 12px; color: #9ca3af;">No nodes use this profile</div>';
    } else {
      listEl.innerHTML = nodes.map(n =>
        `<div class="profile-usage-node" data-node-id="${n.id}">
          <span class="profile-usage-node-product">${n.product || n.id}</span>
          <span class="profile-usage-node-location">${n.location ? 'Â· ' + n.location : ''}</span>
        </div>`
      ).join('');

      // Click on node â†’ switch to network view and select node
      listEl.querySelectorAll('.profile-usage-node').forEach(el => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const nodeId = el.dataset.nodeId;
          if (window.DDOptim?.switchView) window.DDOptim.switchView('network');
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (window.DDOptim?.selectNode) window.DDOptim.selectNode(nodeId);
          }, 100);
        });
      });
    }

    // Toggle collapse/expand
    if (headerEl) {
      headerEl.onclick = () => {
        const isOpen = listEl.classList.toggle('open');
        if (toggleEl) toggleEl.textContent = isOpen ? 'â–² Hide' : 'â–¼ Show';
      };
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€ Dirty State & Button Control â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function setDirty(dirty) {
    isDirty = dirty;
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('profile-btn-save');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('profile-btn-reset');
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = !dirty;
    if (resetBtn) resetBtn.disabled = !dirty;
  }

  // â”€â”€ Main Entry Point â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function renderProfilesView() {
    console.log('ðŸŽ›ï¸ Rendering profiles view...');
    renderProfilesList();
    renderProfileEditPanel();
  }

  // â”€â”€ Expose Public API â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  if (!window.DDOptim) window.DDOptim = {};

  window.DDOptim.renderProfilesView = renderProfilesView;

  // Internal API for event handlers (SCRIPT 1385)
  window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer = {
    renderProfilesList,
    renderProfileEditPanel,
    renderUsagePanel,
    setDirty,
    getSelectedCode: () => selectedProfileCode,
    setSelectedCode: (code) => { selectedProfileCode = code; },
    isDirty: () => isDirty,
    getUsageCount,
    getUsageNodes,
    getProfiles
  };

  console.log('âœ… Buffer Profiles View Renderer loaded');

})();


=== BLOCK: 0301385 | Buffer Profiles Event Handlers ===
// SCRIPT 1385: Buffer Profiles Event Handlers
// AUDITOR:LARGE_BLOCK_JUSTIFIED - All handlers are tightly coupled to the profiles form state

(function() {
  'use strict';

  console.log('ðŸ”Œ Loading Buffer Profiles Event Handlers...');

  function waitForReady(callback) {
    let attempts = 0;
    const check = setInterval(() => {
      attempts++;
      if (window.DDOptim?._profilesRenderer && document.getElementById('profile-btn-save')) {
        clearInterval(check);
        callback();
      } else if (attempts >= 100) {
        clearInterval(check);
        console.error('âŒ Buffer Profiles handlers: init timeout');
      }
    }, 100);
  }

  waitForReady(init);

  // â”€â”€ Validation â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function validateForm() {
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const profiles = R.getProfiles ? R.getProfiles() : (window.DDOptim?.model?.bufferProfiles || {});
    const currentCode = R.getSelectedCode();
    let valid = true;

    // Helper
    function setError(fieldId, msg) {
      const errEl = document.getElementById(`profile-field-${fieldId}-error`);
      const input = document.getElementById(`profile-field-${fieldId}`);
      if (errEl) errEl.textContent = msg;
      if (input) input.classList.toggle('invalid', !!msg);
      if (msg) valid = false;
    }

    // Code
    const codeInput = document.getElementById('profile-field-code');
    const newCode = (codeInput?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codeInput?.readOnly) {
      if (!newCode) {
        setError('code', 'Code is required');
      } else if (!/^[A-Z0-9_]+$/.test(newCode)) {
        setError('code', 'Only A-Z, 0-9, _ allowed');
      } else if (newCode !== currentCode && profiles[newCode]) {
        setError('code', 'A profile with this code already exists');
      } else {
        setError('code', '');
      }
    } else {
      setError('code', '');
    }

    // Variability Factor
    const vf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('profile-field-vf')?.value);
    if (isNaN(vf) || vf < 0 || vf > 1) {
      setError('vf', 'Must be between 0 and 1');
    } else { setError('vf', ''); }

    // DLT C
    const dltC = parseInt(document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-c')?.value);
    if (isNaN(dltC) || dltC < 1) {
      setError('dlt-c', 'Must be â‰¥ 1');
    } else { setError('dlt-c', ''); }

    // DLT M
    const dltM = parseInt(document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-m')?.value);
    if (isNaN(dltM) || dltM < 1) {
      setError('dlt-m', 'Must be â‰¥ 1');
    } else if (!isNaN(dltC) && dltM <= dltC) {
      setError('dlt-m', 'M must be greater than C');
    } else { setError('dlt-m', ''); }

    // Lead Time Factors
    ['ltf-short', 'ltf-medium', 'ltf-long'].forEach(id => {
      const v = parseFloat(document.getElementById(`profile-field-${id}`)?.value);
      if (isNaN(v) || v < 0 || v > 1) {
        setError(id, 'Must be between 0 and 1');
      } else { setError(id, ''); }
    });

    return valid;
  }

  // â”€â”€ Recalculation Cascade (after profile save) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function recalculateAfterProfileChange(affectedCode) {
    const nodes = window.currentNetwork?.nodes || window.DDOptim?.model?.nodes || [];
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const profiles = R ? R.getProfiles() : (window.DDOptim?.model?.bufferProfiles || {});
    const profile = profiles[affectedCode];
    if (!profile) return;

    nodes.forEach(node => {
      if (node.bufferProfile === affectedCode && node.hasBuffer) {
        if (typeof window.calculateBufferSizing === 'function') {
          node.bufferSizing = window.calculateBufferSizing(node, profile);
        }
      }
    });

    if (typeof window.DDOptim?.updateMetrics === 'function') window.DDOptim.updateMetrics();
    else if (typeof window.DDOptim?.updateMetricsDashboard === 'function') window.DDOptim.updateMetricsDashboard();

    if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') window.DDOptim.renderTable();
    if (typeof window.NetworkRenderer?.render === 'function' && window.currentNetwork) {
      window.NetworkRenderer.render(window.currentNetwork);
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€ Save Handler â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleSave() {
    if (!validateForm()) return;

    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const profiles = R.getProfiles();
    const currentCode = R.getSelectedCode();

    const codeInput = document.getElementById('profile-field-code');
    const newCode = (codeInput?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase() || currentCode;

    const updatedProfile = {
      variabilityFactor: parseFloat(document.getElementById('profile-field-vf').value),
      dltThresholds: {
        C: parseInt(document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-c').value),
        M: parseInt(document.getElementById('profile-field-dlt-m').value)
      },
      leadTimeFactors: {
        short:  parseFloat(document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-short').value),
        medium: parseFloat(document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-medium').value),
        long:   parseFloat(document.getElementById('profile-field-ltf-long').value)
      }
    };

    // Apply: remove old key if code changed, set new
    if (newCode !== currentCode) {
      delete profiles[currentCode];
      // Update all nodes using the old code
      const nodes = window.currentNetwork?.nodes || window.DDOptim?.model?.nodes || [];
      nodes.forEach(n => { if (n.bufferProfile === currentCode) n.bufferProfile = newCode; });
    }
    profiles[newCode] = updatedProfile;

    R.setSelectedCode(newCode);
    R.setDirty(false);

    recalculateAfterProfileChange(newCode);

    R.renderProfilesList();
    R.renderProfileEditPanel();
    console.log(`âœ… Profile '${newCode}' saved`);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Reset Handler â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleReset() {
    window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer.renderProfileEditPanel();
  }

  // â”€â”€ Duplicate Handler â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleDuplicate() {
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const profiles = R.getProfiles();
    const currentCode = R.getSelectedCode();
    const source = profiles[currentCode];
    if (!source) return;

    // Generate unique code
    let base = currentCode + '_COPY';
    let candidate = base;
    let i = 2;
    while (profiles[candidate]) { candidate = base + i; i++; }

    profiles[candidate] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(source));
    R.setSelectedCode(candidate);
    R.setDirty(false);
    R.renderProfilesList();
    R.renderProfileEditPanel();
    console.log(`ðŸ“‹ Profile duplicated as '${candidate}'`);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Delete Handler â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleDelete() {
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const currentCode = R.getSelectedCode();
    const usageCount = R.getUsageCount(currentCode);
    const profiles = R.getProfiles();

    if (usageCount === 0) {
      // Simple confirmation
      if (!confirm(`Delete profile "${currentCode}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
      executeDelete(currentCode, null);
    } else {
      // Show replacement modal
      openDeleteModal(currentCode, usageCount, profiles);
    }
  }

  function openDeleteModal(code, usageCount, profiles) {
    const overlay = document.getElementById('profile-delete-modal-overlay');
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('profile-delete-modal-title');
    const descEl  = document.getElementById('profile-delete-modal-desc');
    const listEl  = document.getElementById('profile-delete-replacement-list');
    const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('profile-delete-confirm-btn');
    if (!overlay) return;

    titleEl.textContent = `âš ï¸ Delete Profile "${code}"`;
    descEl.textContent  = `This profile is used by ${usageCount} node${usageCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}. ` +
                          `Choose a replacement profile (or leave unassigned):`;

    // Build replacement options: all other profiles + null option
    const otherCodes = Object.keys(profiles).filter(c => c !== code).sort();
    let selectedReplacement = null;

    const options = [
      { value: '__NULL__', label: 'Leave unassigned (null)' },
      ...otherCodes.map(c => ({ value: c, label: c }))
    ];

    listEl.innerHTML = options.map(opt => `
      <label class="profile-delete-replacement-option" data-value="${opt.value}">
        <input type="radio" name="profile-replace" value="${opt.value}" /> ${opt.label}
      </label>
    `).join('');

    // Radio change handler
    listEl.querySelectorAll('input[type=radio]').forEach(radio => {
      radio.addEventListener('change', () => {
        listEl.querySelectorAll('.profile-delete-replacement-option').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('selected'));
        radio.closest('.profile-delete-replacement-option').classList.add('selected');
        selectedReplacement = radio.value === '__NULL__' ? null : radio.value;
        confirmBtn.disabled = false;
      });
    });

    confirmBtn.disabled = true;
    overlay.classList.add('open');

    // Confirm
    confirmBtn.onclick = () => {
      overlay.classList.remove('open');
      executeDelete(code, selectedReplacement);
    };
    // Cancel
    document.getElementById('profile-delete-cancel-btn').onclick = () => {
      overlay.classList.remove('open');
    };
  }

  function executeDelete(code, replacementCode) {
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    const profiles = R.getProfiles();
    const nodes = window.currentNetwork?.nodes || window.DDOptim?.model?.nodes || [];

    // Update nodes
    nodes.forEach(n => { if (n.bufferProfile === code) n.bufferProfile = replacementCode; });

    // Remove profile
    delete profiles[code];

    // Recalculate if replacement exists
    if (replacementCode) recalculateAfterProfileChange(replacementCode);
    else {
      if (typeof window.DDOptim?.renderTable === 'function') window.DDOptim.renderTable();
      if (typeof window.DDOptim?.updateMetrics === 'function') window.DDOptim.updateMetrics();
    }

    R.setSelectedCode(null);
    R.setDirty(false);
    R.renderProfilesList();
    R.renderProfileEditPanel();
    console.log(`ðŸ—‘ï¸ Profile '${code}' deleted. Replacement: ${replacementCode}`);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Add New Profile Handler â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function handleAddProfile() {
    const R = window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer;
    if (R.isDirty()) {
      if (!confirm('You have unsaved changes. Discard them?')) return;
    }

    const profiles = R.getProfiles();

    // Copy from selected profile or use defaults
    let source = null;
    const selCode = R.getSelectedCode();
    if (selCode && profiles[selCode]) {
      source = profiles[selCode];
    }

    // Find unique code
    let candidate = 'NEW';
    let i = 2;
    while (profiles[candidate]) { candidate = 'NEW' + i; i++; }

    profiles[candidate] = source
      ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(source))
      : { variabilityFactor: 0.5, dltThresholds: { C: 5, M: 15 }, leadTimeFactors: { short: 0.5, medium: 0.4, long: 0.3 } };

    R.setSelectedCode(candidate);
    R.setDirty(true);   // New profile requires Save to confirm
    R.renderProfilesList();
    R.renderProfileEditPanel();

    // Focus the code field so user can rename immediately
    setTimeout(() => {
      const codeField = document.getElementById('profile-field-code');
      if (codeField && !codeField.readOnly) {
        codeField.focus();
        codeField.select();
      }
    }, 50);
    console.log(`âž• New profile '${candidate}' added (unsaved)`);
  }

  // â”€â”€ Field Change â†’ Dirty â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function attachFieldListeners() {
    const fieldIds = [
      'profile-field-code',
      'profile-field-vf',
      'profile-field-dlt-c',
      'profile-field-dlt-m',
      'profile-field-ltf-short',
      'profile-field-ltf-medium',
      'profile-field-ltf-long'
    ];
    fieldIds.forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) {
        el.addEventListener('input', () => {
          if (window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer.getSelectedCode()) {
            window.DDOptim._profilesRenderer.setDirty(true);
          }
        });
      }
    });
  }

  // â”€â”€ Wire Up All Buttons â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  function init() {
    console.log('ðŸ”Œ Attaching Buffer Profiles button handlers...');

    document.getElementById('profile-btn-save')     ?.addEventListener('click', handleSave);
    document.getElementById('profile-btn-reset')    ?.addEventListener('click', handleReset);
    document.getElementById('profile-btn-duplicate')?.addEventListener('click', handleDuplicate);
    document.getElementById('profile-btn-delete')   ?.addEventListener('click', handleDelete);
    document.getElementById('profiles-add-btn')     ?.addEventListener('click', handleAddProfile);

    attachFieldListeners();

    console.log('âœ… Buffer Profiles Event Handlers initialized');
  }

})();


=== BLOCK: 0400000 | [SYSTEM] Script Close / Data Open ===

/* ===== DATA SECTION ===== */


=== BLOCK: 0400100 | EXPLORE: Warehouse Parts Sample (latest import) ===
/* ======================================================================
   B2WISE: data fetcher (Module Pattern)
   - Global module: window.B2W_DATA_EXPLORE_WAREHOUSEPARTS_SAMPLE_25UMMT
   - Persistent state for debugging and cross-block access
   - Standard interface: getRawRecords(), isLoaded(), getLoadError()
   ====================================================================== */

// Standard naming: B2W_DATA_[DESCRIPTOR]
window.B2W_DATA_EXPLORE_WAREHOUSEPARTS_SAMPLE_25UMMT = (function() {
  // Configuration
  var CONFIG = {
    customerCode: 'C3',
    environment: 'b2s',
    sqlQuery: `WITH latest AS (
    SELECT MAX(wimportdateid) AS max_id FROM vvwarehouseparts
),
deduped AS (
    SELECT
        wp.partcode,
        wp.partdescription,
        wp.itemtypecode,
        wp.itemtypename,
        wp.planningmodename,
        wp.bufferprofile,
        wp.adu,
        wp.dlt,
        wp.clt,
        wp.slt,
        wp.yellowzone,
        wp.greenzone,
        wp.topofred,
        wp.minimumorderquantity,
        wp.desiredordercycledaysactual,
        wp.unitcoststandard,
        wp.stockonhand,
        wp.warehousecode,
        wp.wimportdateid,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY wp.wwarehousepartid, wp.wimportdateid ORDER BY wp.importdatetime ASC) AS rn
    FROM vvwarehouseparts wp
    CROSS JOIN latest
    WHERE wp.wimportdateid = latest.max_id
)
SELECT
    partcode,
    partdescription,
    itemtypecode,
    itemtypename,
    planningmodename,
    bufferprofile,
    adu,
    dlt,
    clt,
    slt,
    yellowzone,
    greenzone,
    topofred,
    minimumorderquantity,
    desiredordercycledaysactual,
    unitcoststandard,
    stockonhand,
    warehousecode
FROM deduped
WHERE rn = 1
ORDER BY partcode
LIMIT 50`,
    pollIntervalMs: 2000,
    maxPolls: 60
  };

  // Environment mapping
  var appMap = { 
    'b2s': 'DATAMART_PROD', 
    'test': 'DATAMART_UAT' 
  };
  var application = appMap[CONFIG.environment] || 'DATAMART_PROD';

  // Internal state
  var isLoaded = false;
  var isLoading = false;
  var loadError = null;
  var queryResults = null;  // Stores { rows, rowCount, isLimited, maxRows, stats }

  // Core query function
  async function b2wQuery(customerCode, application, sql, options) {
    options = options || {};
    var nonce = options.nonce || (window.commwiseConfig && window.commwiseConfig.nonce) || '';
    var pollIntervalMs = options.pollIntervalMs || 2000;
    var maxPolls = options.maxPolls || 60;

    // Validation
    var code = String(customerCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!/^C\d+(_\d+)?$/.test(code)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid customer code (e.g., C3, C47, C101_1)');
    }
    var app = String(application || '').trim();
    if (!app) throw new Error('Missing application (e.g., DATAMART_PROD)');
    var query = String(sql || '').trim();
    if (!query) throw new Error('Missing SQL query');

    // Helper to call the proxy
    async function proxy(body) {
      var res = await fetch('/wp-json/commwise-config/v1/secure-proxy', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-WP-Nonce': nonce },
        body: JSON.stringify({ customer: code, application: app, method: 'POST', body: body })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        var text = await res.text().catch(function() { return ''; });
        throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status + ' ' + res.statusText + (text ? (': ' + text) : ''));
      }
      var json = await res.json();
      if (!json || !json.body || json.body.success === false) {
        var err = (json && json.body && json.body.error) ? (': ' + json.body.error) : '';
        throw new Error('Proxy error' + err);
      }
      return json.body;
    }

    // 1) Start query
    var start = await proxy({ action: 'start-query', query: query });
    var queryId = start.queryId;
    if (!queryId) throw new Error('No queryId returned from start-query');

    // 2) Poll for completion
    var attempts = 0;
    while (true) {
      attempts += 1;
      if (attempts > maxPolls) throw new Error('Query timeout');
      var s = await proxy({ action: 'check-status', queryId: queryId });
      var st = s.status;
      if (st === 'SUCCEEDED') break;
      if (st === 'FAILED') throw new Error('Query failed' + (s.error ? (': ' + s.error) : ''));
      if (st === 'CANCELLED') throw new Error('Query cancelled');
      await new Promise(function(r) { setTimeout(r, pollIntervalMs); });
    }

    // 3) Get results
    var out = await proxy({ action: 'get-results', queryId: queryId });
    return {
      rows: out.results || [],
      rowCount: out.rowCount || 0,
      isLimited: !!out.isLimited,
      maxRows: out.maxRows,
      stats: out.stats || null
    };
  }

  // Execute query and store results
  async function executeQuery() {
    if (isLoading || isLoaded) return;
    isLoading = true;
    loadError = null;

    try {
      var results = await b2wQuery(CONFIG.customerCode, application, CONFIG.sqlQuery, {
        pollIntervalMs: CONFIG.pollIntervalMs,
        maxPolls: CONFIG.maxPolls
      });
      
      queryResults = results;
      isLoaded = true;
      isLoading = false;
      console.log('[B2WISE] Data Block loaded: ' + results.rowCount + ' rows');
    } catch (error) {
      isLoading = false;
      loadError = error.message;
      console.error('[B2WISE] Data Block failed:', error.message);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  // REQUIRED: Standard interface for debug scanner and cross-block access
  return {
    // Get raw data rows
    getRawRecords: function() { 
      return queryResults ? queryResults.rows : []; 
    },
    // Get full query results object
    getResults: function() { 
      return queryResults; 
    },
    // Check if data is loaded
    isLoaded: function() { 
      return isLoaded; 
    },
    // Check if currently loading
    isLoading: function() { 
      return isLoading; 
    },
    // Get any load error
    getLoadError: function() { 
      return loadError; 
    },
    // Get configuration (useful for debugging)
    getConfig: function() { 
      return CONFIG; 
    },
    // Execute/reload the query
    executeQuery: executeQuery
  };
})();

// Auto-execute on initialization
(function() {
  var checkAndExecute = function() {
    if (typeof window.commwiseConfig !== 'undefined' && window.commwiseConfig.nonce) {
      window.B2W_DATA_EXPLORE_WAREHOUSEPARTS_SAMPLE_25UMMT.executeQuery();
    } else {
      setTimeout(checkAndExecute, 100);
    }
  };
  checkAndExecute();
})();

=== BLOCK: 0520100 | ON_DEMAND: RLT Algorithm Test ===
(function() {
  // Delay execution to wait for model load + baseline capture
  // (SCRIPT 1200 loads the model via polling, baseline captured shortly after)
  function runWhenReady(attempt) {
    attempt = attempt || 1;

    // Step 1: ensure ModelLoader is available
    if (!window.ModelLoader?.loadFromLibrary) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      else { CommwiseDiagnostics.send({ status: 'error', summary: 'Timed out: ModelLoader not available' }, { diagnosticBlockId: 'scenario_engine_test' }); }
      return;
    }

    // Step 2: wait for any model to be loaded (SCRIPT 1200 loads Simple Chain at startup)
    if (!window.currentNetwork?.nodes) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      else { CommwiseDiagnostics.send({ status: 'error', summary: 'Timed out: no network loaded' }, { diagnosticBlockId: 'scenario_engine_test' }); }
      return;
    }
    // Load Weber Pignons only if needed for tests (current model lacks velo node)
    const hasVelo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo') || window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('VELO');
    if (!hasVelo) {
      console.log('[ScenarioEngineTest] Loading Weber Pignons for tests...');
      window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary('weber_pignons');
      setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 600);
      return;
    }

    // Step 3: wait for baseline capture (triggered by loadModel)
    const hasBaseline = window.DDOptim?.baselineManager?.getBaselineInfo()?.exists;
    if (!hasBaseline) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      else { CommwiseDiagnostics.send({ status: 'error', summary: 'Timed out: baseline not captured after Weber Pignons load' }, { diagnosticBlockId: 'scenario_engine_test' }); }
      return;
    }

    runTests();
  }

  function runTests() {
  console.log('=== SCENARIO ENGINE TEST ===');
  
  const results = {
    tests: [],
    passed: 0,
    failed: 0
  };
  
  function test(name, fn) {
    try {
      fn();
      results.tests.push({ name, status: 'PASS' });
      results.passed++;
      console.log('âœ…', name);
    } catch (error) {
      results.tests.push({ name, status: 'FAIL', error: error.message });
      results.failed++;
      console.error('âŒ', name, ':', error.message);
    }
  }
  
  function assert(condition, message) {
    if (!condition) {
      throw new Error(message || 'Assertion failed');
    }
  }
  
  // Test 1: Baseline snapshot exists
  test('Baseline snapshot captured', function() {
    assert(window.DDOptim?.baselineManager, 'Baseline manager not found');
    const info = window.DDOptim.baselineManager.getBaselineInfo();
    assert(info.exists, 'Baseline snapshot not captured');
    assert(info.nodeCount > 0, 'Baseline snapshot is empty');
    console.log('  â†’ Snapshot contains', info.nodeCount, 'nodes');
  });
  
  // Test 2: Node selection - explicit IDs
  test('Node selection by IDs', function() {
    assert(window.DDOptim?.nodeSelection, 'Node selection engine not found');
    const nodes = window.DDOptim.nodeSelection.selectNodes(
      window.currentNetwork.nodes,
      { nodeIds: ['VELO', 'ROUE'] }
    );
    assert(nodes.length === 2, 'Expected 2 nodes, got ' + nodes.length);
    assert(nodes[0].id === 'VELO' || nodes[1].id === 'VELO', 'VELO not selected');
    console.log('  â†’ Selected:', nodes.map(n => n.id).join(', '));
  });
  
  // Test 3: Node selection - filters
  test('Node selection by filters', function() {
    const nodes = window.DDOptim.nodeSelection.selectNodes(
      window.currentNetwork.nodes,
      {
        filters: [
          { column: 'type', operator: 'equals', value: 'finished_product' }
        ]
      }
    );
    assert(nodes.length > 0, 'No finished products found');
    assert(nodes.every(n => n.type === 'finished_product'), 'Non-finished product selected');
    console.log('  â†’ Selected', nodes.length, 'finished products');
  });
  
  // Test 4: Node selection - empty (all)
  test('Node selection - empty selector (all nodes)', function() {
    const nodes = window.DDOptim.nodeSelection.selectNodes(
      window.currentNetwork.nodes,
      {}
    );
    assert(nodes.length === window.currentNetwork.nodes.size, 'Not all nodes selected');
    console.log('  â†’ Selected all', nodes.length, 'nodes');
  });
  
  // Test 5: Apply modification - operator =
  test('Apply modification - operator =', function() {
    assert(window.DDOptim?.modificationEngine, 'Modification engine not found');
    const velo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo');
    const original = velo.independentADU;
    
    const success = window.DDOptim.modificationEngine.applyModification(velo, {
      attribute: 'independentADU',
      operator: '=',
      value: 999
    });
    
    assert(success, 'Modification failed');
    assert(velo.independentADU === 999, 'Value not updated: ' + velo.independentADU);
    
    // Restore
    velo.independentADU = original;
    console.log('  â†’ Set independentADU to 999, then restored to', original);
  });

    const success = window.DDOptim.modificationEngine.applyModification(velo, {
      attribute: 'independentADU',

    });

    assert(velo.independentADU === original * 2, 'Value not doubled: ' + velo.independentADU);

    velo.independentADU = original;

  });

    const velo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo');
    const originalADU = velo.independentADU;
    velo.independentADU = 12345;

  });

  test('Apply complete scenario', function() {

    const testScenario = {
      id: 'test',
      name: 'Test Scenario',
      modifications: [
        {
          nodeSelector: { nodeIds: ['velo'] },
          attribute: 'independentADU',
          operator: '=',
          value: 100
        },
        {
          nodeSelector: { nodeIds: ['velo'] },

        }
      ]
    };
    
    // Restore baseline first
    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();

    const result = window.DDOptim.scenarioApplication.applyScenario(testScenario);
    assert(result.success, 'Scenario application failed');
    assert(result.modificationsApplied === 2, 'Expected 2 modifications, got ' + result.modificationsApplied);
    
    // Check results
    const velo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo');
    assert(velo.independentADU === 100, 'ADU not set: ' + velo.independentADU);
    assert(velo.leadTime === 10, 'Lead time not set: ' + velo.leadTime);
    
    // Restore baseline
    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();
    
    console.log('  â†’ Applied 2 modifications to velo, then restored');
  });
  
  // Test 10: Load scenario workflow (dry run)
  test('Load scenario workflow', function() {
    assert(window.DDOptim?.scenarioLoader, 'Scenario loader not found');
    assert(window.currentNetwork?.scenarios, 'No scenarios in network');
    assert(window.currentNetwork.scenarios.length > 0, 'Scenarios array is empty');

  });
  
  // Test 11: Diff engine - no changes
  test('Diff engine - no changes', function() {
    assert(window.DDOptim?.diffEngine, 'Diff engine not found');

    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();
    
    // Generate modifications (should be empty)

  });
  
  // Test 12: Diff engine - detect changes
  test('Diff engine - detect changes', function() {

    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();
    
    // Make some changes
    const velo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo');
    const roue = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('roue');
    const originalVeloADU = velo.independentADU;
    const originalRoueADU = roue.independentADU;
    
    velo.independentADU = 999;

    // Generate modifications
    const mods = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState();
    assert(mods.length > 0, 'Expected modifications, got none');
    
    const summary = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.getDiffSummary();
    assert(summary.hasChanges, 'hasChanges should be true');
    assert(summary.nodesAffected >= 2, 'Should affect at least 2 nodes, got ' + summary.nodesAffected);
    
    console.log('  â†’ Detected', mods.length, 'modification(s) affecting', summary.nodesAffected, 'node(s)');
    
    // Restore

    roue.independentADU = originalRoueADU;
  });
  
  // Test 13: Diff engine - grouping
  test('Diff engine - grouping same changes', function() {
    // Restore baseline
    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();
    
    // Make identical changes to multiple nodes
    const velo = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo');
    const roue = window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('roue');
    const originalVelo = velo.leadTime;
    const originalRoue = roue.leadTime;
    
    velo.leadTime = 99;
    roue.leadTime = 99;
    
    // Generate modifications
    const mods = window.DDOptim.diffEngine.generateModificationsFromCurrentState();
    
    // Should group into single modification with nodeIds array
    const leadTimeMods = mods.filter(m => m.attribute === 'leadTime' && m.value === 99);
    assert(leadTimeMods.length === 1, 'Expected 1 grouped modification, got ' + leadTimeMods.length);
    assert(leadTimeMods[0].nodeSelector.nodeIds.length >= 2, 
           'Expected 2+ nodes in group, got ' + leadTimeMods[0].nodeSelector.nodeIds.length);
    
    console.log('  â†’ Grouped', leadTimeMods[0].nodeSelector.nodeIds.length, 'nodes with same change');
    
    // Restore

    roue.leadTime = originalRoue;
    window.DDOptim.baselineManager.restoreBaseline();
  });
  
  // Summary
  console.log('\n=== SUMMARY ===');
  console.log('Total tests:', results.tests.length);
  console.log('Passed:', results.passed, 'âœ…');
  console.log('Failed:', results.failed, 'âŒ');
  
  if (results.failed > 0) {
    console.error('\nFailed tests:');
    results.tests.filter(t => t.status === 'FAIL').forEach(t => {
      console.error('  -', t.name, ':', t.error);
    });

  // Send diagnostics
  CommwiseDiagnostics.send({
    status: results.failed === 0 ? 'ok' : 'error',
    summary: results.passed + ' / ' + results.tests.length + ' tests passed',
    results: results
  }, { diagnosticBlockId: "scenario_engine_test" })
    .then(function(res) { console.log('[Diagnostics] sent', res); })
    .catch(function(err) { console.error('[Diagnostics] failed', err); });
  } // end runTests()

  // ON_DEMAND: triggered by CommWise when user clicks "Run" in the diagnostics panel
  // runWhenReady(1) is NOT called here - CommWise handles the trigger

=== BLOCK: 0520200 | ON_DEMAND: ADU Propagation Validation ===
// ON_DEMAND DIAGNOSTIC: ADU Propagation Validation
// Auto-loads Weber Pignons if not active, then runs tests.

(function() {
  function runWhenReady(attempt) {
    attempt = attempt || 1;
    if (!window.ModelLoader?.loadFromLibrary) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      return;
    }
    if (!window.currentNetwork?.nodes) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      return;
    }
    if (!window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo') && !window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('VELO')) {
      console.log('[ADU Test] Loading Weber Pignons...');
      window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary('weber_pignons');
      setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 600);
      return;
    }
    runTests();
  }

  function runTests() {
    console.log('\n=== ADU Propagation Test ===');

    if (!window.WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK) {
      console.error('âŒ WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK not loaded');
      return;
    }
    if (!window.ADUPropagation || !window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU) {
      console.error('âŒ ADUPropagation module not loaded');
      return;
    }

    const seasonalAdjustment = 1.3;
    const nodes = WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK.nodes;
    const success = window.ADUPropagation.propagateADU(nodes, seasonalAdjustment);

    if (!success) {
      console.error('âŒ ADU Propagation failed - network contains cycles or errors');
      return;
    }

    const tests = [
      { name: 'VÃ©lo (Finished Product)',          id: 'velo',    expected: 52,   tolerance: 0.1 },
      { name: 'Plateau (Machined with spare parts)', id: 'plateau', expected: 54.6, tolerance: 0.1 },
      { name: 'Roue (Intermediate, pure dependent)', id: 'roue',    expected: 104,  tolerance: 0.1 },
      { name: 'Rayons (Component, deep dependent)', id: 'rayons',  expected: 7488, tolerance: 1 }
    ];

    let passed = 0;
    let failed = 0;

    tests.forEach(function(test) {
      const node = nodes.get ? nodes.get(test.id) : nodes[test.id];
      if (!node) {
        console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': Node not found');
        failed++;
        return;
      }
      const actual = node.calculatedADU;
      const diff = Math.abs(actual - test.expected);
      if (diff <= test.tolerance) {
        console.log('âœ“ ' + test.name + ': ' + actual.toFixed(1) + ' (expected ' + test.expected + ')');
        passed++;
      } else {
        console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': ' + actual.toFixed(1) + ' (expected ' + test.expected + ', diff: ' + diff.toFixed(1) + ')');
        failed++;
      }
    });

    console.log('\n[ADU Test] ' + passed + '/' + tests.length + ' tests passed');
    if (failed > 0) { console.error(failed + ' tests failed'); }

    CommwiseDiagnostics.send({
      status: failed === 0 ? 'ok' : 'error',
      summary: passed + ' / ' + tests.length + ' tests passed',
      passed: passed,
      failed: failed
    }, { diagnosticBlockId: 'adu_propagation_test' })
      .then(function(res) { console.log('[Diagnostics] sent', res); })
      .catch(function(err) { console.error('[Diagnostics] failed', err); });
  }

  // ON_DEMAND: CommWise triggers execution when user clicks "Run"
})();

// Auto-loads Weber Pignons if not active, then runs tests.

(function() {
  function runWhenReady(attempt) {
    attempt = attempt || 1;
    if (!window.ModelLoader?.loadFromLibrary) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      return;
    }
    if (!window.currentNetwork?.nodes) {
      if (attempt < 30) { setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 300); }
      return;
    }
    if (!window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('velo') && !window.currentNetwork.nodes.get('VELO')) {
      console.log('[CLT/DLT Test] Loading Weber Pignons...');
      window.ModelLoader.loadFromLibrary('weber_pignons');
      setTimeout(function() { runWhenReady(attempt + 1); }, 600);
      return;
    }
    runTests();
  }

  function runTests() {
    console.log('\n=== CLT/DLT Test ===');

    if (!window.WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK) {
      console.error('âŒ WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK not loaded'); return;
    }
    if (!window.CLTCalculator || !window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT) {
      console.error('âŒ CLTCalculator module not loaded'); return;
    }
    if (!window.DLTCalculator || !window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT) {
      console.error('âŒ DLTCalculator module not loaded'); return;
    }

    const nodes = WEBER_PIGNONS_NETWORK.nodes;
    window.CLTCalculator.calculateCLT(nodes);

    const cltTests = [
      { name: 'Rayons (leaf)',       id: 'rayons',  expected: 15 },
      { name: 'Jante (leaf)',        id: 'jante',   expected: 10 },
      { name: 'Pneu (leaf,longest)', id: 'pneu',    expected: 30 },
      { name: 'Roue (assembly)',     id: 'roue',    expected: 34 },
      { name: 'E-Cadre (assembly)',  id: 'e_cadre', expected: 22 },
      { name: 'VÃ©lo (finished)',     id: 'velo',    expected: 39 }
    ];

    let passed = 0;
    let failed = 0;

    console.log('\nCLT Tests (Cumulative Lead Time - unbuffered):');
    cltTests.forEach(function(test) {
      const node = nodes.get ? nodes.get(test.id) : nodes[test.id];
      if (!node) { console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': Node not found'); failed++; return; }
      if (node.clt === test.expected) {
        console.log('âœ“ ' + test.name + ': ' + node.clt + ' days'); passed++;
      } else {
        console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': ' + node.clt + ' days (expected ' + test.expected + ')'); failed++;
      }
    });

    // Scenario 1: No buffers
    console.log('\nDLT Tests - Scenario 1: No Buffers:');
    window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(nodes);
    const n1 = nodes.get('velo');
    if (n1 && n1.dlt === 39) { console.log('âœ“ VÃ©lo (no buffers): DLT = 39'); passed++; }
    else { console.error('âœ— VÃ©lo (no buffers): DLT = ' + (n1 ? n1.dlt : 'N/A') + ' (expected 39)'); failed++; }

    // Scenario 2: Roue buffered only
    console.log('\nDLT Tests - Scenario 2: Wheel Buffered Only:');
    const roueNode = nodes.get('roue');
    if (roueNode) {
      roueNode.hasBuffer = true;
      window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(nodes);
      [{ name: 'Roue (buffered)', id: 'roue', expected: 4 },
       { name: 'VÃ©lo (wheel buffered)', id: 'velo', expected: 27 }]
      .forEach(function(test) {
        const node = nodes.get(test.id);
        if (!node) { failed++; return; }
        if (node.dlt === test.expected) { console.log('âœ“ ' + test.name + ': DLT = ' + node.dlt); passed++; }
        else { console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': DLT = ' + node.dlt + ' (expected ' + test.expected + ')'); failed++; }
      });
    }

    // Scenario 3: All semi-finished buffered
    console.log('\nDLT Tests - Scenario 3: All Semi-Finished Buffered:');
    const semiIds = ['roue', 'e_cadre', 'e_guidon', 'e_selle'];
    semiIds.forEach(function(id) { const n = nodes.get(id); if (n) n.hasBuffer = true; });
    window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(nodes);

    [{ name: 'Roue (buffered)',    id: 'roue',    expected: 4 },
     { name: 'E-Cadre (buffered)', id: 'e_cadre', expected: 7 },
     { name: 'E-Guidon (buffered)',id: 'e_guidon',expected: 5 },
     { name: 'E-Selle (buffered)', id: 'e_selle', expected: 2 },
     { name: 'VÃ©lo (all semi buffered)', id: 'velo', expected: 5 }]
    .forEach(function(test) {
      const node = nodes.get(test.id);
      if (!node) { failed++; return; }
      if (node.dlt === test.expected) { console.log('âœ“ ' + test.name + ': DLT = ' + node.dlt); passed++; }
      else { console.error('âœ— ' + test.name + ': DLT = ' + node.dlt + ' (expected ' + test.expected + ')'); failed++; }
    });

    // Buffer impact
    const veloNode = nodes.get('velo');
    if (veloNode) {
      const impact = veloNode.clt - veloNode.dlt;
      if (impact === 34) { console.log('âœ“ Buffer Impact: 34 days reduction'); passed++; }
      else { console.error('âœ— Buffer Impact: ' + impact + ' (expected 34)'); failed++; }
    }

    // Reset buffers
    semiIds.forEach(function(id) { const n = nodes.get(id); if (n) n.hasBuffer = false; });
    window.DLTCalculator.calculateDLT(nodes);

    const total = cltTests.length + 1 + 2 + 5 + 1;
    console.log('\n[CLT/DLT Test] ' + passed + '/' + total + ' tests passed');
    if (failed > 0) { console.error(failed + ' tests failed'); }

    CommwiseDiagnostics.send({
      status: failed === 0 ? 'ok' : 'error',
      summary: passed + ' / ' + total + ' tests passed',
      passed: passed,
      failed: failed
    }, { diagnosticBlockId: 'clt_dlt_test' })
      .then(function(res) { console.log('[Diagnostics] sent', res); })
      .catch(function(err) { console.error('[Diagnostics] failed', err); });
  }

  // ON_DEMAND: CommWise triggers execution when user clicks "Run"
})();
